### [1] [GRAVA: Grounded Reasoning-to-Action Representation and Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15169)
*Xiao Liu,Haoyu Li,Jianghao Leng,Lin Wang,Chao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAVA 提出 Grounded Reasoning-to-Action，将视觉 grounding、推理与动作生成统一在单一自回归流中，并通过数据构建与渐进训练提升驾驶 VLA 的可执行性，在 NAVSIM 等基准上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶 VLA 模型虽能先推理后行动，但中间推理往往缺乏物理场景证据支撑，且与可执行行为联系松散，导致 grounding 与动作生成脱节。

Method: GRA 将动作相关语言引用链接到 2D 视觉区域和自车物理状态，用轨迹锚定的类型图组织对象交互与决策，并序列化为 grounded reasoning；单个 VLM 生成该推理及紧凑的 Executable Planner 动作，再确定性解码为连续轨迹。作者还提出结合前向场景 grounding 与后向轨迹锚定的 agentic 数据构建流程，构建含 220 万 grounded QA 对和 7 万 GRA 推理轨迹的 GR-NavSim。训练采用渐进策略：预训练建立 grounded 认知，模仿学习建立推理到动作接口，强化学习与探索改进驾驶行为，仅用约 60% 人类驾驶示范进行动作监督。

Result: GRAVA-8B 在完整 NAVSIM 基准上于纯自回归驾驶模型中达到 SOTA；在内部长尾基准上，完整 GRA 相比仅动作预测将关键对象合规性和闭环驾驶分数分别提升 19.3% 与 20.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在可执行动作生成过程中保留来自 grounded reasoning 的动作相关物理证据，能有效提升驾驶 VLA 的性能与行为质量。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly reason before acting, but their intermediate reasoning is often weakly grounded in physical scene evidence and loosely connected to executable behavior. We present GRAVA, a framework built around Grounded Reasoning-to-Action (GRA), which unifies grounding, reasoning, and action generation in a single autoregressive stream. GRA links action-relevant language references to 2D visual regions and ego-centric physical states, organizes object interactions and decisions in a trajectory-anchored typed graph, and serializes this structure into grounded reasoning. A single VLM generates this reasoning followed by a compact Executable Planner action that is deterministically decoded into a continuous trajectory. We further introduce an agentic GRA data construction pipeline that combines forward scene grounding with backward trajectory anchoring, and use it to build GR-NavSim with 2.2M grounded question-answer pairs and 70K GRA reasoning traces. A progressive training strategy develops grounded cognition through pre-training, establishes the reasoning-to-action interface through imitation, and improves driving behavior through reinforcement learning and exploration. Using about 60% of the available human driving demonstrations for action supervision, GRAVA-8B achieves state-of-the-art performance among purely autoregressive driving models on the full NAVSIM benchmark. On an internal long-tail benchmark, full GRA improves key-object compliance and Closed-loop Driving Score by 19.3% and 20.5% over action-only prediction, respectively. These results show the benefit of preserving action-relevant physical evidence from grounded reasoning through executable action generation.

</details>


### [2] [LG-VLN: A Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation Framework with LangGraph State Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15098)
*Jianhe Zhao,Yanhua Qiu,Zhiyu Zhang,Zibo Zhao,Jinhua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LG-VLN：仅用单目RGB、零样本的连续环境视觉语言导航框架，通过共享视觉特征与LangGraph状态编排统一几何感知与语义导航，在R2R-CE val-unseen子集上达到21.3%成功率和12.1% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法依赖LiDAR、全景相机或额外传感器，且几何建图与语义导航使用分离的视觉表示，导致长轨迹上空间-语义不一致。

Method: LG-VLN采用在线前馈3D重建网络预测深度、相机位姿和稠密点云，用于智能体位姿估计与全局地图融合；几何与导航共享稠密CleanDIFT特征，利用语义一致性拒绝错误帧间对应，并通过目标实例约束定义视觉参考，将其相似度与局部BLIP-2图文相关性结合形成语义价值图；使用LangGraph将指令解析、几何感知、语义价值更新、路径规划、动作执行和失败恢复建模为带条件转移、持久状态和模块化恢复机制的有向状态图。

Result: 在R2R-CE val-unseen的固定550回合子集上，LG-VLN取得21.3%成功率和12.1%按路径长度加权的成功率；消融实验表明共享语义特征可提升导航性能，结合视觉相似度与图文相关性后进一步提升。

Conclusion: 共享视觉表示与显式状态编排对仅使用单目RGB的零样本VLN-CE有效，代码将公开以支持复现。

Abstract: Continuous-environment vision-and-language navigation (VLN-CE) requires interpreting natural-language instructions in unseen 3D environments and executing continuous low-level actions. Existing methods often depend on LiDAR, panoramic cameras, or extra sensors; separate geometric-mapping and semantic-navigation visual representations can cause long-trajectory spatial-semantic inconsistencies. We propose LG-VLN, a monocular zero-shot framework with shared visual features and LangGraph-based state orchestration. An online feed-forward 3D reconstruction network predicts depth, camera poses, and dense point clouds for agent-pose estimation and global map fusion. Geometry and navigation share dense CleanDIFT features: semantic consistency rejects incorrect inter-frame correspondences, while target-instance constraints define visual references whose similarity combines with local BLIP-2 image-text relevance to form a semantic value map. LangGraph represents instruction parsing, geometric perception, semantic value updates, path planning, action execution, and failure recovery as a directed state graph with conditional transitions, persistent state, and modular recovery mechanisms. On a fixed 550-episode subset of the R2R-CE val-unseen split, LG-VLN achieves 21.3% success and 12.1% success weighted by path length. Ablations show shared semantic features improve navigation, further boosted by combining visual similarity and image-text relevance. Results establish shared visual representations and explicit state orchestration as effective for zero-shot VLN-CE using monocular RGB alone. Code will be publicly released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [3] [PhysBrain 1.5: From Vision-Language Models to Physical Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14973)
*DeepCybo Team,Yu Bin,Haipeng Cao,Zheng Chang,Kai Chen,Youning Chen,Kailin Deng,Yichao Du,Xiaotong Fu,Haoyang Ge,Yunlong Guo,Chenliu Hao,Jiyan He,Xuguo He,Yakun Hou,Kai Hu,Cong Huang,Tuopusen Huang,Yu Huang,Hong Li,Peize Li,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Yun Lin,Haibao Liu,Haochen Liu,Qiuzhi Liu,Shengcai Liu,Zhiqiang Liu,Tao Luo,Peng Ren,Shuo Ren,Chaoyi Ruan,Zhaolong Shen,Yukun Shi,Qiyuan Su,Yuxuan Tian,Yining Wang,Changti Wu,Hao Wu,Xueyin Xu,Ruoqi Yang,Zhaoyang Yang,Hang Yuan,Zhaoyang Zeng,Hanwen Zhang,Ruimeng Zhang,Yao Zhang,Yibo Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zubin Zheng,Zishen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PhysBrain 1.5，一个统一物理环境理解、动作生成与未来状态预测的多模态具身模型，通过将语言、末端执行器运动和稠密视觉目标编码为离散序列并自回归联合优化，在28个具身理解基准上取得开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受“观察—交互—环境变化”的物理闭环启发，希望将具身理解、动作生成和未来状态预测统一到一个学习框架中。

Method: 以通用视觉-语言模型为起点，将语言响应、末端执行器运动、稠密视觉目标编码为离散序列，并用自回归下一token预测联合优化；预训练仅使用人类交互视频，以任务为中心的情节对齐语义和空间上下文、恢复的运动及后续观察；随后在人类示范、机器人轨迹和模拟经验的混合数据上进行监督微调。

Result: 8B模型在28个具身理解基准上平均得分72.5，达到开源SOTA，并与GPT-6-Astra、Gemini 3.6 Flash等领先专有模型相当；在14个基准上取得最佳开源结果，同时保持通用多模态能力；定性结果显示其能生成末端执行器轨迹，并通过空间对齐的RGB、深度和机器人掩码输出预测未来场景。

Conclusion: PhysBrain 1.5验证了统一建模具身理解、动作生成与未来状态预测的可行性，能在具身任务上取得领先性能，并兼顾通用多模态能力。

Abstract: We present PhysBrain 1.5, a unified model for understanding physical environments, generating actions, and predicting future states. Motivated by the physical loop of observation, interaction, and environmental change, we bring these capabilities into a common learning framework. Starting from a general vision--language model, we encode language responses, end-effector motion, and dense visual targets as discrete sequences and jointly optimize them with autoregressive next-token prediction. Pre-training draws its embodied supervision entirely from human interaction videos, using task-centered episodes to pair semantic and spatial context with recovered motion and subsequent observations. We then adapt the model through supervised fine-tuning on a mixture of human demonstrations, robot trajectories, and simulated experience. Across 28 embodied understanding benchmarks, our 8B model achieves an average score of 72.5, setting a new open-source state of the art and performing on par with leading proprietary models such as GPT-6-Astra and Gemini 3.6 Flash. It achieves the best open-source results on 14 benchmarks while retaining general multimodal capabilities. Beyond these understanding evaluations, qualitative examples show the model's ability to produce end-effector trajectories and predict future scenes through spatially aligned RGB, depth, and robot-mask outputs.

</details>


### [4] [DynEoMT: Learning Object Dynamicity from Online Segmentation Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14466)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DynEoMT，一个在线视频分割框架，在查询式分割基础上联合预测每个分割区域的动态/静态属性；仅用当前帧与传播查询即可推理，并借助离线相机补偿光流监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型能识别并跟踪目标，但不判断各分割区域是否相对观察相机独立运动；该动态性不能仅由语义推断，且受相机自运动干扰。

Method: 在查询式视频分割中增加区域级动态性预测，同时输出原分割结果与动态/静态状态；推理仅用当前帧和传播查询，无需光流、深度、相机位姿、历史RGB帧或特征图。为解决标注缺失，提出类无关离线监督流程，使用相机补偿光流与置信度感知时序滤波。

Result: 在 VIPSeg、OVIS、YouTube-VIS 2022 和 VSPW 上，平衡准确率分别达到 84.3、68.0、68.6 和 87.6，同时基本保持分割性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，分割区域动态性可从传播查询中学习，从而在推理时无需专用运动处理流程即可在线预测；完整代码将开源以支持方法与实验复现。

Abstract: Video segmentation models recognize and track objects over time, but they do not indicate whether each segmented region moves independently of the observing camera. This dynamicity attribute cannot be inferred from semantics alone and is confounded by camera ego-motion. We introduce \method, an online framework that augments query-based video segmentation with region-level dynamicity prediction. It jointly produces the original segmentation outputs and a dynamic or static state for each predicted region. At inference, DynEoMT uses only the current frame and propagated queries, without optical flow, depth, camera pose, previous RGB frames, or feature maps. Because established video segmentation benchmarks do not annotate this attribute, we also introduce a class-agnostic offline supervision pipeline using camera-compensated optical flow and confidence-aware temporal filtering. Across VIPSeg, OVIS, YouTube-VIS 2022, and VSPW, DynEoMT achieves balanced accuracies of 84.3, 68.0, 68.6, and 87.6, respectively, while largely preserving segmentation performance. These results show that segmentation-region dynamicity can be learned from propagated queries, enabling its online prediction without a dedicated motion-processing pipeline at inference. The complete code will be released as open source to enable full reproduction of the method and experiments.

</details>


### [5] [S3-Tracker: Self-Supervised Surgical Tissue Tracking With Contrastive Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14313)
*Jiaming Zhang,Zijian Wu,Mehran Armand,Septimiu Salcudean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向内镜视频的自监督 Track-Any-Point 点跟踪方法，无需标注即可学习像素对应与轨迹，性能接近半监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内镜视频中的鲁棒点跟踪对计算机辅助干预和自主机器人手术至关重要，可支持术中视频与术前影像在软组织形变下的连续配准；但监督方法依赖大规模标注数据，而手术场景中可靠轨迹标注困难。

Method: 利用未标注手术视频进行自监督学习，通过建立全局像素对应关系，并借助对比随机游走（contrastive random walks）推断点轨迹。

Result: 在无标注训练下，性能与现有半监督方法相当，并能隐式处理组织形变。

Conclusion: 证明了自监督点跟踪在手术环境中的可行性，有望减少对标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Robust point tracking in endoscopic videos is essential for computer-assisted intervention and autonomous robotic surgery, enabling continuous registration between intraoperative video and preoperative imaging despite soft tissue deformation. However, supervised tracking methods depend on large annotated datasets, while surgical conditions make reliable trajectory annotation challenging. We propose a self-supervised Track-Any-Point approach that learns from unlabeled surgical videos by establishing global pixel correspondences and inferring point trajectories through contrastive random walks. Trained without annotations, our method achieves performance comparable to existing semi-supervised approaches while implicitly handling tissue deformation. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of self-supervised point tracking in surgical environments and its potential to reduce reliance on annotated data.

</details>


### [6] [LPA-CWM: A Learned Physical Adjudicator for Motion Reasoning with Counterfactual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14073)
*Kunwei Wu,Xiang Liu,Guocai Yao,Junming Chen,Zhikang Chen,Min Zhang,Pengwei Wang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LPA-CWM：用轻量学习型物理裁决器（LPA）为反事实世界模型（CWM）的不同响应学习可靠性权重，替代均匀聚合，并引入完整性感知运动对应（CMC）评估协议；在DAVIS和Kinetics子集上显著提升DCA_avg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWM通过比较事实预测与干预预测来提取运动，但不同目标帧掩码下生成的响应可靠性不同，均匀聚合会同等对待不可靠响应；同时需要更全面地评估运动对应的完整性等指标。

Method: 将响应聚合形式化为候选可靠性学习。LPA-CWM包含3.0M参数的LPA，在MOVi-F密集轨迹上训练，对无序候选集比较视觉上下文与响应结构并预测相对权重，而CWM预测器和干预生成器保持冻结。加权响应经过窗口化定位和一次成对重评估以恢复运动。另提出CMC，真值锚定的评估协议，联合衡量定位、轨迹完整性、可见性和连续性，并将可见动态点上的缺失预测计为失败。

Result: 在DAVIS和Kinetics子集上，LPA-CWM相对Uniform CWM的DCA_avg分别提升60.0%和29.0%，并在TAP-Vid First下提升跟踪精度。

Conclusion: LPA-CWM通过学习候选响应可靠性，有效改进反事实世界模型的运动提取，且CMC提供了更完整的运动对应评估；方法轻量，无需微调CWM预测器与干预生成器。

Abstract: Counterfactual world models (CWM) extract motion from pretrained video predictors by comparing factual and intervened predictions. However, responses generated under different target-frame masks vary in reliability, while uniform aggregation weights them equally. We formulate response aggregation as candidate reliability learning and propose LPA-CWM with a lightweight Learned Physical Adjudicator (LPA). Trained on dense MOVi-F trajectories, the 3.0M-parameter LPA compares visual context and response structure across an unordered candidate set to predict relative weights, while the CWM predictor and intervention generator remain frozen. The weighted responses undergo windowed localization and one paired re-evaluation to recover motion. We also introduce Completeness-aware Motion Correspondence (CMC), a ground-truth-anchored evaluation protocol that jointly measures localization, trajectory completeness, visibility, and continuity, counting missing predictions as failures on visible dynamic points. On the evaluated DAVIS and Kinetics subsets, LPA-CWM improves $\mathrm{DCA}_{\mathrm{avg}}$ over Uniform CWM by 60.0\% and 29.0\%, respectively, and also improves tracking accuracy under TAP-Vid First. A quick overview is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [BLInD: Learning Driver Intent as a Distribution over Future Ego Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13941)
*Flavian Pegado,Ronit Hire,Shreyas Rajesh,Soham Phade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BLInD，一种仅用车辆状态历史的轻量盲模型，预测未来自车轨迹top-k分布，无需相机、LiDAR、地图或目标轨迹，可实时部署并在AEB任务中显著降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型轨迹分布模型依赖场景上下文，而盲车辆状态模型通常退化为单一路径；安全关键下游需要低延迟、跨域、可采样的多模态意图分布。

Method: BLInD将速度、曲率、转向灯、车型等近期车辆状态历史映射为未来自车轨迹top-k分布；研究自回归AR和流匹配两种分布架构；在混合平台开源数据集和Wayve数据上训练，无需数据集特定适配。

Result: 流匹配模型取得最佳top-k ADE/FDE：Wayve 0.15/0.37 m，Waymo 0.15/0.36 m，nuScenes 0.28/0.59 m；AR模型覆盖相当。集成到AEB触发任务中，严格全候选策略将误报从1.51%降至AR的0.11%（13.7倍，TP 94.9%）和流匹配的0.06%（25.1倍，TP 98.7%），对比1-CTRV策略100% TP。NVIDIA DRIVE Orin上推理耗时AR 0.87 ms、流匹配2.9 ms。

Conclusion: BLInD同时实现学习型、盲输入和跨域，这一组合此前未被展示；结果表明该分布可为下游系统提供可控且合理的意图采样接口，AEB仅是一个实例。

Abstract: We present BLInD (Blind Learned Intent Distribution), a compact network that maps recent vehicle-state history (e.g. speed, curvature, indicator, and vehicle type) to a top-k distribution of future ego trajectories, with no camera, LiDAR, map, or object-track inputs. We find that vehiclestate history alone is sufficient to learn a useful multimodal distribution over near-term ego trajectories, and its low-latency nature makes it well-suited for safety-critical deployment. We investigate two distribution architectures, autoregressive (AR) and flow-matching, and train on both mixed-platform opensource and Wayve datasets. Both generalize without datasetspecific adaptation; the flow-matching model achieves best topk ADE/FDE of 0.15/0.37 m on Wayve, 0.15/0.36 m on Waymo, and 0.28/0.59 m on nuScenes, with the AR model reaching comparable coverage. Integrating the distributions into an AEB trigger task, a strict all-candidates policy reduces false positives from 1.51% to 0.11% with AR (13.7x reduction, 94.9% TP) and to 0.06% with flow-matching (25.1x reduction, 98.7% TP) compared to a 1-CTRV policy with 100% true positive score. BLInD runs in 0.87 ms with the AR head and 2.9 ms with the flow-matching head on an NVIDIA DRIVE Orin ECU making it compatible with real-time deployment on automotive ECUs. While existing learned distribution models rely on scene context and blind vehicle-state models typically collapse to a single path, BLInD is learned, blind, and cross-domain simultaneously, a combination not demonstrated by prior work. These results show that such a distribution provides a controllable and plausible intent sampling interface for downstream systems, with AEB as one instantiation.

</details>


### [8] [GroundBench: A Factorized, Counterfactual Benchmark for Locating VLM Affordance Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13308)
*Sarthak Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GroundBench 诊断基准，用于解耦视觉 grounding、机械推理与类别到动作关联，检验视觉语言模型在操作提示中看似 grounded 的表现是否可由文本捷径复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前评估发现，在操作提示中命名目标部件可使八个视觉语言模型的动作准确率提升 0.32-0.63，且未命名时没有模型超过常量基线；但命名部件提供了真实系统本应推断的信息，混淆了视觉 grounding、机械推理和类别-动作关联，因此需要受控诊断来分离这些解释。

Method: 构建 GroundBench：包含六种 branch-and-merge 条件，每种条件逐步加入受控信息包；并设置反事实重问，要求模型指向同一图像中可见的真实替代部件。评估三个 OpenAI 模型、共 1068 条预测，加入无视觉对照；在来自 32 个物体的 74 个反事实对上测试 GPT-5 的成对加权合规率与捷径率。

Result: 仅提供目标区域但不提供身份时，动作准确率为 0.26、0.26、0.53，处于或低于 0.53 的多数类基线，尽管模型大多能复述所给区域；仅提供身份但不提供位置时，准确率为 0.74、0.68、0.68。该精选集合中所有高于基线的提升都出现在所给部件类别本身决定动作的情况。无视觉对照下 GPT-5 分数不变或提升，支持存在显著类别-动作关联；但 GPT-4o mini 在某一条件下下降，说明该解释不具普遍性。加入 joint type 与 motion axis 在六组模型-层比较中均未提升准确率。在 74 个反事实对上，GPT-5 成对加权合规率为 0.86、捷径率为 0.07，但所有观察到的 push-to-lift-vertical 案例均失败。

Conclusion: GroundBench 能识别哪些供给信息改变 affordance 行为，并检验表面 grounded 的性能是否可由文本类别-动作捷径复现；结果显示存在显著类别-动作关联，但并非所有模型或条件一致，且特定物理推理仍会失败。

Abstract: A companion evaluation found that naming the target part in a manipulation prompt increased action accuracy by 0.32-0.63 across eight vision-language models, with no model outperforming a constant baseline until the part was named. However, naming the part supplies information that a real system must infer, confounding visual grounding, mechanical reasoning, and category-to-action association. We introduce GroundBench, a diagnostic benchmark that separates these explanations through six branch-and-merge conditions, each adding a controlled information bundle, and a counterfactual re-ask targeting a real alternate part visible in the same image. Across three OpenAI models and 1,068 predictions, supplying the target region without its identity leaves action accuracy at or below the 0.53 majority baseline (0.26, 0.26, and 0.53), although the models largely reproduce the supplied region. Supplying identity without location instead yields 0.74, 0.68, and 0.68. Every above-baseline gain in this curated set occurs where the supplied part category itself determines the action. A no-vision control leaves GPT-5's scores unchanged or improved, providing evidence consistent with substantial category-to-action association. GPT-4o mini declines on one condition, so this interpretation is not universal. Adding joint type and motion axis does not improve accuracy across six model-stratum comparisons. On 74 counterfactual pairs from 32 objects, GPT-5 achieves 0.86 pair-weighted compliance with a 0.07 shortcut rate but fails all observed push-to-lift-vertical cases. GroundBench identifies which supplied information changes affordance behavior and tests whether apparently grounded performance can be reproduced through textual shortcuts.

</details>


### [9] [Part Grounding, Not Action Knowledge: Locating the Bottleneck in VLM Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13225)
*Sarthak Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将视觉语言模型的低层操作推理拆分为“定位可操作部件”与“判断该部件所需动作”两步，发现主要瓶颈是部件接地而非动作知识：不指明部件时模型表现甚至不如忽略图像的常数基线，指明目标部件后动作准确率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅给出总体准确率，无法说明视觉语言模型在低层操作推理中究竟哪一步失败；而 affordance 问题常混淆“操作哪个部件”与“该部件对应什么动作”。

Method: 在19个铰接物体上，让来自3家开发者的8个模型回答机器人应施加何种运动；设置开放提示、命名目标部件提示，以及无标签集的自由文本描述同一部件。并测试真实照片与渲染物体上的抓取点定位，且用平凡替代方案核查评测阈值与标注规则。

Result: 开放提示下，push 在64次正确应为push的评测中仅出现1次，尽管push对8/19物体正确且总在标签集中；模型常描述与评分不同的部件。命名目标部件后，各模型动作准确率提升0.32–0.63，从0.158–0.474升至0.684–0.947，push召回从0–1/8升至7–8/8；开放提示下无一模型超过忽略图像的常数答案，命名部件后8个模型均超过。自由描述同一部件时，6–8/8模型产出按压语言。真实照片上仅3/8模型抓取点定位优于常数基线，渲染物体上无一模型优于。还发现两个测量错误：阈值使常数基线得0.929，标注规则在4/19物体上错误。

Conclusion: 结果表明动作知识并非主要缺失，部件接地是主导瓶颈，且跨三个模型家族成立、不随能力增强而消失。命名部件提供了接地变量，因此该结果界定的是完美部件检测器能带来的上限，而非证明模型掌握通用机械原理；同时强调需用平凡替代方案检验评测指标。

Abstract: Benchmarks agree that vision-language models reason poorly about low-level manipulation, but an aggregate accuracy score does not say which step fails. We separate two steps that affordance questions conflate: identifying which part of an object to act on, and knowing what action that part requires. Across 19 articulated objects we asked eight models, spanning three developers, what motion a robot should apply. Under an open prompt, push was produced once in 64 evaluations where it was correct, despite being correct for 8 of 19 objects and appearing in the offered label set every time. Inspecting the outputs showed why: models described a different part than the one being scored, e.g. explaining how to pick up a camera rather than press its button. Naming the target part raises action accuracy by 0.32 to 0.63 for every model, from 0.158-0.474 to 0.684-0.947, and push recall from 0-1/8 to 7-8/8. No model beats a constant answer that ignores the image under the open prompt; once the part is named, all eight do. Asked to describe the same part in free prose with no label set, models produce pressing language for 6 to 8 of 8. These results are hard to reconcile with missing action knowledge, and instead point to part grounding as the dominant bottleneck, a pattern that holds across all three model families and does not diminish with capability. Naming the part supplies the grounding variable, so this bounds what a perfect part detector would offer rather than demonstrating a general model of mechanics. Two supporting results agree: on real photographs only three of eight models localize grasp points better than a constant baseline, and on rendered objects none do. We also document two measurement errors of our own, a threshold that let a constant baseline score 0.929 and a labelling rule wrong on 4 of 19 objects, both caught only by testing our numbers against trivial alternatives.

</details>

### [10] [Embracing Flow Unsteadiness: A High-Throughput Learning Platform Enables Vortex-Exploiting Bioinspired Propulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14254)
*Fei Han,Xinyu Cui,Zhipeng Wang,Ning Yang,Hang Xu,Haifeng Zhang,Zhongming Hu,Jun Wang,Junfeng Du,Dixia Fan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出REEF物理-学习协同框架，将高通量真实流固交互阵列SHOAL与分阶段算法V-STAR结合，在非定常流体中学习可迁移的尾流耦合推进策略，使可达推力包络超过参数化搜索的两倍，并零样本迁移至自由游动机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物游动者和飞行者利用非定常涡进行推进，而工程载具通常将涡视为扰动并加以抑制。在机器中学习这种流动利用很困难，因为真实流体交互数据稀缺，且在高维、历史依赖流场中无结构探索不稳定。

Method: REEF是协同设计的物理-学习框架：SHOAL为八通道高通量阵列，用于获取真实流体-结构交互；V-STAR为分阶段算法，通过模仿、离线内化和在线适应将交互转化为策略。方法覆盖升力型、阻力型和动量射流型推进器。

Result: 相比参数化搜索，REEF将可达力包络扩大至两倍以上。粒子图像测速表明，这些增益源于涡的协同形成、增长与力投影，而非细化固定的运动-力映射。力训练策略零样本迁移到自由移动机器人，即使其身体运动改变周围流场。

Conclusion: REEF学习到可迁移的尾流耦合原理，可用于非定常流体中的具身推进。

Abstract: Biological swimmers and flyers exploit unsteady vortices for propulsion, whereas engineered vehicles usually suppress them as disturbances. Learning such flow exploitation in machines is difficult because real-fluid interaction data are scarce and unstructured exploration is unstable in high-dimensional, history-dependent flows. Here we present REEF, a co-designed physical-learning framework that integrates SHOAL, an eight-channel high-throughput array for real fluid--structure interaction, with V-STAR, a staged algorithm that converts these interactions into policies through imitation, offline internalization, and online adaptation. Across lift-based, drag-based, and momentum-jet propulsors, REEF expands the attainable force envelope to more than twice that of parameterized search. Particle image velocimetry shows that these gains arise from coordinated vortex formation, growth, and force projection, rather than refinement of a fixed motion-to-force mapping. Force-trained policies transfer zero-shot to free-moving robots whose body motion changes the surrounding flow, suggesting that REEF learns transferable wake-coupling principles for embodied propulsion in unsteady fluids.

</details>

### [11] [Diffusion-Based Generation of Gait Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14642)
*Damian Benasco,Juan Carballeira-Lopez,Jaime Ramos-Rojas,Julio S. Lora-Millan,Antonio J. Del-Ama,David Rodriguez-Cianca,Pablo Lanillos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索条件扩散模型生成以步长等步态参数为条件下肢关节角轨迹，并在4590个步态周期数据上验证其生成真实周期步态及一定可控性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人与康复领域需要适应患者个体形态和治疗目标的参考步态轨迹，同时保持生物力学真实性；传统手工模板或优化方法难以跨受试者和行走条件扩展。

Method: 采用条件扩散模型生成下肢关节角轨迹，条件为步长等步态参数；比较基线Transformer扩散模型与引入自适应归一化和无分类器引导的可控扩散Transformer变体，并在4590个步态周期数据集上实验。

Result: 扩散模型能够生成真实的周期性步态轨迹，并在步态特征上实现一定程度的可控变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明扩散模型在辅助机器人个性化步态合成方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Generation of musculoskeletal gait trajectories conditioned on patient-specific parameters remains a key challenge for wearable robotics and rehabilitation. Assistive systems such as lower-limb exoskeletons require reference trajectories that adapt to individual morphology and therapeutic goals while preserving biomechanical realism. Traditional approaches rely on hand-crafted gait templates or optimization procedures that scale poorly across subjects and walking conditions. In this work, we explore conditional diffusion models for generating lower-limb joint-angle trajectories conditioned on gait parameters such as step length. We compare a baseline transformer diffusion model with a controllable diffusion transformer variant incorporating adaptive normalization and classifier-free guidance. Experiments on a dataset of 4,590 gait cycles show that diffusion models can generate realistic periodic gait trajectories while enabling some controllability variation in gait characteristics, highlighting their potential for personalized gait synthesis in assistive robotics.

</details>

### [12] [Real-Time Synthesis of Robust Controlled Invariant Sets for Monotone Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14115)
*Yasin Sonmez,Mahmoud Khaled,Majid Zamani,Murat Arcak*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出阈值函数重构，将最大不动点迭代转化为逐列独立一维二分搜索，实现高度并行的控制不变集在线计算，在超大网格上实现毫秒至分钟级合成，并用于安全感知MPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键自主系统需要在条件变化时在线计算形式化安全证书（如控制不变集）。标准合成算法随状态维数扩展性差；现有懒惰不动点算法虽利用单调性和下闭安全规范并仅跟踪反链基，但其成员测试与冗余检查仍是主要瓶颈。

Method: 引入阈值函数重构：将d维网格上的下闭集合表示为沿指定轴的列高度。由此把最大不动点迭代重写为每个网格列独立的一维二分搜索，形成embarrassingly parallel的迭代，并具有渐近低于懒惰不动点算法的计算复杂度。

Result: 在3D网格实验中，10^9个单元的不变集合成耗时低于50 ms，10^14个单元低于2分钟；并在一个安全感知模型预测控制器示例中展示了在线重新合成。

Conclusion: 该阈值函数重构方法能显著加速大规模高维控制不变集的在线计算，支持安全关键系统的实时或近实时安全证书合成与在线重合成。

Abstract: Safety-critical control of autonomous systems requires formal safety certificates, such as controlled invariant sets, that must be computed online as conditions change. Although standard synthesis algorithms scale poorly with state dimension, monotone dynamical systems with lower-closed safety specifications allow for accelerated computation of controlled invariant sets. In particular, lazy fixed-point algorithms exploit monotonicity and track only the antichain basis of the set. However, membership tests and redundancy checks against an evolving basis remain major bottlenecks. We introduce a threshold-function reformulation in which a lower-closed set on a d-dimensional grid is represented by its column heights along a designated axis. This reformulates the greatest-fixed-point iteration as independent one-dimensional binary searches, one per grid column, yielding an embarrassingly parallel iteration with asymptotically lower computational complexity than the lazy fixed-point algorithm. Experiments synthesize invariant sets on 3D grids with 10^9 cells in under 50 ms and 10^14 cells in under two minutes. We further demonstrate online re-synthesis in a safety-informed model predictive controller example.

</details>

### [13] [Early Prediction of Satellite Collision Probability Using a Hybrid TCN-Transformer Model for a CDM-Based Conjunction Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13191)
*Rabia Tüylek Tok,Burak Yağlıoğlu,Enes Dağ,Emre Onur Kahya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的框架，利用历史CDM序列提前预测同一次交会事件在后续CDM更新中的碰撞概率PoC，以支持LEO卫星、尤其是电推进卫星的碰撞规避决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO中运行卫星和轨道碎片快速增加，近距离交会事件频繁，加重卫星运营商负担；电推进卫星低推力机动能力带来额外时间约束。现有CDM虽提供相对状态、协方差、最近接近距离、TCA和PoC，但轨道不确定性非线性传播及PoC对协方差演化高度敏感，使连续CDM的风险解读困难。

Method: 首先采用无迹变换UT的传播与反向传播框架，评估碰撞风险指标对CDM参数的敏感性；其次对数值CDM参数进行主成分分析PCA，识别与PoC变化最相关的特征。基于敏感性和PCA结果，选择原始CDM参数并构造表征相对运动、交会几何和协方差相关不确定性的派生指标。利用得到的序列增强交会数据集，训练混合TCN-Transformer模型，学习交会风险的时序演化。

Result: 该框架应用于TÜBİTAK UZAY接收并分析的CDM数据，展示了其在LEO卫星交会中进行更早期且更一致的操作风险评估的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提学习型框架有望通过预测后续CDM的PoC，提升LEO卫星交会风险评估的时效性和一致性，尤其适用于受低推力机动时间约束的电推进卫星。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of operational satellites and orbital debris has increased the frequency of close approach events in low Earth orbit (LEO), creating a higher operational burden for satellite operators. This problem is especially critical for satellites using electric propulsion, where low-thrust maneuver capability imposes additional time constraints on collision avoidance planning. In current practice, Conjunction Data Messages (CDMs) provide relative state, covariance, miss distance, time of closest approach, and probability of collision (PoC) information for conjunction assessment. However, the nonlinear propagation of orbital uncertainties and the sensitivity of PoC to covariance evolution make the interpretation of sequential CDMs challenging. This study proposes a learning-based framework for early prediction of satellite conjunction risk by estimating the PoC expected in the subsequent CDM update of the same close approach event. In the proposed methodology, an Unscented Transform-based propagation and backpropagation framework is first used to evaluate the sensitivity of the collision risk metric to CDM parameters. In addition, Principal Component Analysis is applied to the numerical CDM parameters to identify the features most relevant to PoC variation. The results obtained from the sensitivity analysis and PCA are then used to justify the selected raw CDM parameters and to construct derived metrics representing relative motion, encounter geometry, and covariance-related uncertainty. Using the resulting sequential enriched conjunction dataset, a hybrid Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN)-Transformer model is trained to learn the temporal evolution of conjunction risk. The framework is applied to CDMs received and analyzed within TÜBİTAK UZAY, demonstrating its potential for earlier and more consistent operational risk evaluation for LEO satellite conjunctions.

</details>

### [14] [Real-time Learning and Evolution in Robotic Art Installations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13352)
*Sofian Audry,Stephen Kelly*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍三件探索自适应行为美学的机器人艺术装置，利用具身机器学习与数字进化构建实时人工生态系统，并讨论研究-创作实践及观众审美体验提升策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索自适应行为的美学，重新定义艺术家在人机集体中的角色，并考察艺术与工程方法在自适应机器人中的异同，推动跨学科艺术-科学研究。

Method: 通过具身机器学习与数字进化构建三件机器人艺术装置，使开放式新颖性、试错学习、竞争与合作在实时生态系统中涌现；采用研究-创作实践进行分析，并讨论展示语境及时空与材料考量。

Result: 作品将学习与进化过程不是作为优化特定方案的手段，而是作为审美体验本身；呈现了艺术与工程路径的汇聚与分歧，并提出了提升观众审美体验的策略。

Conclusion: 基于具身自适应系统的机器人艺术可成为跨学科艺术-科学研究的模式，其核心在于以自适应过程本身为审美对象，并需重视展示语境与时间/材料因素。

Abstract: We present three robotic art installations which explore the aesthetics of adaptive behavior. Through embodied machine leaning and digital evolution, these works draw viewers into an artificial ecosystem in which open-ended novelty, trial-and-error learning, competition, and cooperation emerge in real time. Research-creation practices are examined in relation to these works, focusing on how they redefine the role of artists within a human-machine collective while examining points of convergence and divergence between artistic and engineering approaches to adaptive robotics. The systems in question use learning and evolutionary processes not as a means to optimize a specific solution, but as an aesthetic experience on its own, suggesting new modes of interdisciplinary art-science research. Finally, we discuss strategies and practices to elevate the aesthetic experience for audiences, including contexts of presentation as well as temporal and material considerations for artworks based on embodied adaptive systems.

</details>

### [15] [Seeing What the Vehicle Sees: Video-Augmented Virtual Reality for Physical Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13224)
*Md Tanjemul Islam,Mohammad Shafin,Md Rafiul Kabir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视频增强VR框架，将物理ROS 2自动驾驶机器人车辆与部署在Meta Quest 3S上的Unity 6应用耦合，使乘客能在VR中同步观察实车运动、第一人称摄像头画面及导航决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶有望提升道路安全与效率，但乘客往往不清楚车辆感知到什么以及为何采取相应动作；现有面向乘客的VR研究多依赖全仿真车辆或预编排场景，其展示的运动与感知并非来自真实运行的自动驾驶系统。

Method: 构建视频增强VR框架：物理ROS 2自动驾驶机器人车辆通过两条独立通信通道传输车辆状态与车载相机实时视频流；Unity 6应用运行于Meta Quest 3S头显，虚拟车辆镜像物理机器人的运动，乘客可通过车内仪表盘界面同时查看第一人称相机画面与导航决策；开展20次重复闭环导航试验进行评估。

Result: 系统平均状态更新延迟为29.63 ms，物理与虚拟车辆之间的平均相对路线进度误差为2.28%，视频传输帧率为10.006 FPS，丢帧率为0.25%；所有监测到的导航决策均在VR界面中正确反映，无漏报或错误通知。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架能够支持时间同步、语义一致且路线进度准确的表示，可用于沉浸式观察物理自动驾驶车辆的行为。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles are expected to improve road safety and efficiency, but passengers often remain uncertain about what the vehicle perceives and why it acts as it does. Virtual reality (VR) offers a safe and repeatable medium for presenting this information, yet most passenger-facing VR studies rely on fully simulated vehicles or pre-scripted scenarios, so the motion and perception shown to the user do not originate from a physically operating autonomous system. This paper presents a video-augmented VR framework that couples a physical ROS 2 autonomous robot vehicle to a Unity 6 application deployed on a Meta Quest 3S headset. The vehicle state and live onboard camera stream are transmitted over two independent communication channels, allowing the virtual vehicle to mirror the physical robot's motion while the passenger simultaneously views the vehicle's first-person camera feed and its navigation decisions through an in-vehicle dashboard interface. We evaluate the framework over 20 repeated closed-loop navigation trials. The system achieves a mean state-update latency of 29.63 ms, a mean relative route-progress error of 2.28% between the physical and virtual vehicles, and video delivery at 10.006 frames per second with 0.25% frame loss. All monitored navigation decisions were correctly reflected in the VR interface with no missed or incorrect notifications. The results indicate that the framework can support temporally synchronized, semantically consistent, and accurate route-progress representation for immersive observation of physical autonomous-vehicle behavior.

</details>


### [16] [EMoG: Emotion-Modulated Gait Generation for Expressive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14432)
*Yi Lu,Tianhao Jiang,Honglong Tian,Yumeng Zhang,Qingrui Zhao,Zhengtao Wang,Xiao-Xiao Long,Qiu Shen,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMoG：一种情感调制的人形机器人步态生成框架，利用连续可调的情感风格码与轻量MLP实时生成富有表现力且符合指令的周期步态，并由统一强化学习策略执行，支持基于LLM的自然语言交互控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人运动系统主要关注稳定性与任务执行，难以在显式运动控制中融入表现力（情感表达）。

Method: 提出EMoG框架：引入强度连续可调的情感风格码；以该码和物理指令为条件，用轻量MLP实时生成富有表现力且与指令一致的周期步态轨迹，并由统一强化学习策略进行物理执行。构建了来自专业表演者的大规模情感标注步态数据集，并开发自动化流水线提取物理一致的周期步态循环；同时集成基于LLM的解析器，将自由语言转换为情感风格与运动参数，实现交互控制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可实现连续的步态风格调制，产生可感知的表现力线索，同时保持指令跟踪能力。

Conclusion: EMoG为面向人机交互的参数化情感风格行走提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Existing humanoid locomotion systems primarily focus on stability and task execution, while integrating expressiveness with explicit locomotion control remains challenging. We propose EMoG, an emotion-modulated gait generation framework for expressive humanoid locomotion. EMoG introduces an emotional-style code with continuously adjustable intensity. Conditioned on this code and physical commands, a lightweight MLP generates expressive, command-consistent periodic gait trajectories in real time, which are tracked by a unified reinforcement learning policy for physical execution. To support training, we collect a large-scale emotion-annotated gait dataset from professional performers and develop an automated pipeline to extract physically consistent periodic gait cycles. EMoG also integrates an LLM-based parser that converts free-form language into emotional style and motion parameters for interactive control. Experiments demonstrate continuous gait-style modulation with perceptible expressive cues while maintaining command tracking. EMoG provides a practical approach to parameterized emotional-style walking for human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [17] [Learning-Based Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance for a UAV Using Only Three Range Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14426)
*Mohammad Reza Ranjbar Divkoti,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向未知动态环境中固定高度UAV的在线运动规划，提出行为栅格图与PPO-DRL协同设计，仅用三个低成本方向测距传感器实现静态/动态障碍实时避障与目标到达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端部分可观测、仅偏航单自由度导致非完整约束（类固定翼）的条件下，UAV需在未知动态环境中实时规避静态与动态障碍，且避免依赖LiDAR或视觉等密集感知。

Method: 将行为栅格图表示与深度强化学习集成，采用PPO进行连续控制策略稳定学习；核心是状态表示与控制策略协同设计，使行为栅格图动态聚合稀疏测距测量为结构化局部表示，支持避障与目标到达的实时决策；提出标准与增强方法。

Result: 多尺寸、不同障碍密度仿真中，所提标准与增强方法成功率高于PPO变体与MPC：小规模高拥堵场景94% vs 79--90%，大规模高拥堵场景83% vs 62--71%，并保持实时性；四类真实场景实验验证可行性，均能持续到达目标且测试条件下无碰撞。

Conclusion: 表明仅依赖三个低成本方向测距传感器，通过行为栅格图与PPO-DRL协同设计，可在强部分可观测非完整UAV上实现可靠的在线动态避障与目标导航，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: We present a learning-based approach to kinodynamic online motion planning for an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) operating at a fixed altitude in unknown dynamic environments, where real-time avoidance of both static and dynamic obstacles must be achieved under conditions of extreme partial observability. The UAV is controlled with a single degree of freedom (yaw only), resulting in constrained, nonholonomic motion similar to fixed-wing platforms. The proposed framework integrates a behavior grid map representation with Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL), using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) for stable policy learning in continuous control. The key idea is the co-design of a state representation and control policy that enables reliable navigation using only three low-cost directional range sensors, without reliance on dense sensing modalities such as LiDAR or vision-based systems. The behavior grid map dynamically aggregates sparse measurements into a structured local representation that supports real-time decision-making for obstacle avoidance and target reaching. Extensive simulations across environments of varying sizes and obstacle densities demonstrate that the proposed standard and enhanced methods achieve higher success rates than PPO variants and Model Predictive Control (MPC) (94\% vs. 79--90\% in small-scale high-congestion scenarios, and 83\% vs. 62--71\% in large-scale high-congestion scenarios), while maintaining real-time performance. Real-world experiments across four scenarios further confirm practical feasibility, with consistent target-reaching behaviour and no collisions under the tested conditions.

</details>


### [18] [Orientation Control of Soft Robots via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14376)
*Aron Karakai,Roshan S. Kaundinya,Mike Yan Michelis,Robert Katzschmann,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文扩展绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的数据驱动降维方法，利用更大的可观测数据集辨识aSSM并改进aSSM-MPC，从而提升软体机器人位置与姿态控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人常用于安全关键和精细环境，需要精确的位置与姿态控制；模型预测控制（MPC）虽适用，但依赖既准确又计算廉价的无限维非线性动力学模型。

Method: 扩展现有aSSM理论，从扩大的可观测数据集中辨识aSSM；升级已有的aSSM-MPC控制方案，以构建并利用数据驱动降维模型。

Result: 在高保真有限元仿真的压力驱动软体臂上评估，所提控制器相比现有数据驱动基线，将位置和方向跟踪误差降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于扩展可观测数据集的aSSM辨识与改进aSSM-MPC能显著提高软体机器人位姿控制精度。

Abstract: Soft robots are commonly sought for safety-critical interactions in delicate environments, where accurate position and orientation control is imperative. Model predictive control (MPC) offers a solution, but it requires a model of the robot's infinite-dimensional nonlinear dynamics that is at once accurate and computationally cheap. Recent theory on adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) and their applications to soft robots provide data-driven model-reduction methods to construct such models. Here, we extend these methods to identify aSSMs from enlarged observable datasets and upgrade the currently available aSSM-MPC schemes. Evaluated on a high-fidelity finite-element simulation of a pressure-actuated soft arm, our controller reduces position and orientation tracking error by more than 60% compared to existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [19] [BIG-CBF: Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with Shared Uncertainty for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14343)
*Shibo Li,Zhongcheng Wang,Jiahe Cao,Jianhua Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BIG-CBF 两速率导航架构，将行为想象引导的机动选择与硬 CBF 安全滤波结合，并共享不确定性；在基准和实物实验中实现高任务成功率与低 CBF 干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小干预 CBF 安全滤波器缺乏任务级机动意识，在几何模糊环境中即使存在多个局部可行机动，也可能选不到有进展的避障方向，导致安全但停滞。

Method: 采用低速率机动选择与高速率安全滤波分离的两速率架构；短时域内想象并评估六种闭环反馈行为，评估指标包括解析 CBF 兼容性、任务进展、冻结、平滑和切换代价；想象层与执行层共享相对运动延迟、障碍物预测、零阶保持运动和命令执行残差等不确定性源；硬 CBF 作为最终安全权威。

Result: 在覆盖九个场景的 3,600 回合对比基准中，总体任务成功率达 99.78%，并显著减少下游 CBF 干预；在搭载 Jetson Orin Nano 的物理全向机器人上完成 15 次评估均无接触事件；与非共享变体相比，CBF 干预能量和激活频率更低。

Conclusion: 共享不确定性有助于缩小规划-执行失配，提升机动选择与安全关键执行的一致性；BIG-CBF 在几何模糊环境中兼顾安全约束与任务推进。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide a mathematically grounded framework for enforcing local collision-avoidance constraints in autonomous mobile robots, commonly through optimization-based safety filters. However, a minimum-intervention CBF filter lacks task-level maneuver awareness and may fail to select a productive avoidance direction when multiple distinct maneuvers are locally viable, leading to safe but stalled behavior in geometrically ambiguous environments. This paper presents BIG-CBF, Behavior-Imagination-Guided Control Barrier Function with shared uncertainty, a two-rate navigation architecture that separates low-rate maneuver selection from high-rate safety filtering. Over a short horizon, six closed-loop feedback behaviors are imagined and evaluated using analytic CBF compatibility together with a lightweight objective accounting for task progress, freezing, smoothness, and switching. To reduce planning-execution mismatch, the imagination and execution layers share consistent uncertainty sources for relative-motion delay, obstacle prediction, zero-order-hold motion, and command-execution residuals, while a hard CBF remains the final safety authority. In a 3,600-episode comparative benchmark across nine scenarios, BIG-CBF achieves the highest overall task success rate of 99.78% while substantially reducing downstream CBF intervention. On a physical omnidirectional robot with onboard Jetson Orin Nano computation, BIG-CBF completes all 15 evaluation runs without a recorded contact event. Matched hardware comparisons against the non-shared variant further show lower CBF intervention energy and activation frequency, supporting improved consistency between maneuver selection and safety-critical execution.

</details>


### [20] [VLBiMan++: Expanding the Generalization Boundary of Vision-Language Anchored One-Shot Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14310)
*Huayi Zhou,Wei Gao,Yiyang Han,Kui Jia,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLBiMan++ 是一个扩展框架，旨在提升视觉语言锚定的一次性双臂操作的泛化边界。它从单次人类演示出发，通过任务感知分解与视觉语言几何适应，在任务、物体、场景、本体和长期部署五个维度实现泛化，无需大规模遥操作演示或策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用双臂机器人操作需要可复用的任务先验，以应对日益多样的任务、物体、场景、本体和执行条件，同时避免大规模遥操作数据采集与策略重训练的高昂成本。

Method: VLBiMan++ 从单次人类演示开始，进行任务感知分解以识别可复用且可适应的技能组件，并采用视觉语言锚定的几何适应，将这些技能迁移到新配置而无需重训练。框架沿五个维度系统扩展泛化：任务、物体、场景、本体和部署。为此引入物体状态感知适应与轻量轨迹优化机制，以处理超出简单刚性6-DoF位姿变化的情况，并保持可靠的双臂协调。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，VLBiMan++ 在日益具有挑战性的设置中仍能保持较强的任务成功率和适应能力。

Conclusion: VLBiMan++ 推动一次性双臂操作从展示孤立可迁移性，迈向跨任务、物体、场景、本体和长期部署条件的更系统化、可扩展的泛化框架。

Abstract: Generalizable bimanual robotic manipulation requires a reusable task prior that can persist across increasingly diverse tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and execution conditions, thus avoiding the prohibitive cost of large-scale teleoperated demonstrations and policy retraining. In this work, we present VLBiMan++, an extended framework that expands the generalization boundary of vision-language anchored one-shot bimanual manipulation. Starting from a single human demonstration, VLBiMan++ performs task-aware decomposition to identify reusable and adaptable skill components, and employs vision-language grounded geometric adaptation to transfer these skills to novel configurations without retraining. Building on this foundation, we systematically extend generalization along five dimensions: task generalization through diverse and long-horizon skill compositions; object generalization across unseen categories, varying geometries, and more complex articulated or deformable objects; scene generalization under clutter, occlusion, and dynamic interference; embodiment generalization across heterogeneous dual-arm robotic platforms; and deployment generalization through prolonged closed-loop execution under repeated external perturbations. To support this broader scope, we further introduce object-state-aware adaptation and lightweight trajectory optimization mechanisms that accommodate changes beyond simple rigid 6-DoF pose variations while preserving reliable bimanual coordination. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that VLBiMan++ maintains strong task success and adaptation capability across these increasingly challenging settings. Overall, VLBiMan++ advances one-shot bimanual manipulation from demonstrating isolated transferability toward a more systematic and scalable framework for generalization across tasks, objects, scenes, embodiments, and long-term deployment conditions.

</details>


### [21] [Multi-Task Visual Perception Network with LLM Conditioning for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14297)
*Praveen Kumar,K. R. Guruprasad,Tushar Sandhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合视觉感知与大语言模型（LLM）的交互式导航框架，不依赖全局一致地图，通过基于局部视觉线索与自中心几何指令的序列动作规划，实现长期服务机器人导航并最小化漂移累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期导航中里程计漂移与传感器误差会逐步累积并退化2D地图，导致A*、RRT*、DiPPer、ViT-A*等传统路径规划算法随时间失效；同时希望提供用户友好的交互方式，并避免全局一致地图的维护开销。

Method: 集成视觉感知与LLM，通过文本或语音解释用户指令；不依赖易漂移的全局地图，而是基于局部视觉线索和自中心几何指令生成序列动作计划并依次执行；通过相对于即时目标进行重定位，采用最小累积漂移策略，支持已知与未知环境中的安全导航。

Result: 在真实世界和仿真数据上的实验表明，该方法相比其他方法有显著提升，能够实现准确、高效且无碰撞的导航。

Conclusion: 该框架无需维护传统全局地图，即可在长期导航中有效缓解漂移累积问题，提升路径规划与导航性能，且源代码已公开。

Abstract: Long-term navigation for service robots faces crit- ical challenges like the accumulation of odometry drift and sensor error, which progressively degrade 2D maps and renders traditional path planning algorithms (e.g., A*, RRT*, DiPPer, ViT-A*) ineffective over time. To address this, we propose a user-friendly, interactive framework that eliminates the reliance on globally consistent maps. Our approach integrates visual perception with Large Language Models (LLM) to interpret user commands via text or voice. Instead of relying on a drift- prone global map, the system generates a sequential action plan based on local visual cues and egocentric geometric instructions. These action plans are executed sequentially, allowing the robot to navigate known and unknown environments safely. By reset- ting localization relative to immediate targets, our framework effectively works with a minimum accumulation drift strategy, ensuring accurate, efficient, and collision-free navigation without the maintenance overhead of traditional mapping. Experiments on real-world and simulated data have shown significant improve- ments over other methods. Our source code is publicly accessible at this https URL .

</details>


### [22] [Learning Communication-Conditioned Generative Policies for Decentralized Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14268)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出去中心化通信条件化生成式多智能体避碰框架：用流匹配策略从特权离线示范学习潜在消息，支持协同生成、高效单步推理及通信中断鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体避碰在部分可观测下需协调，但集中式规划与显式通信常不可用，且执行时需完全去中心化；如何从无通信信号的特权示范中学习隐式交互并保持灵活推理？

Method: 以带全局状态的特权离线示范训练流匹配策略；示范无显式通信，智能体学习交换并聚合编码交互意图的潜在消息。测试时，无条件生成对应独立行为，通信条件生成实现无集中规划协同；结合滚动时域推理，单步生成短时域动作序列，执行仅依赖局部观测与消息，完全去中心化。

Result: 仿真中避碰性能接近专家，对更密集、未见多智能体场景泛化强；通信丢包下性能优雅退化；实现真实机器人零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 表明无需集中规划或显式通信即可在去中心化执行中实现协同避碰，兼具高效推理、鲁棒性和真机迁移能力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a decentralized communication-conditioned generative framework for multi-agent collision avoidance. Agents generate short-horizon action sequences using a flow-matching policy trained from privileged offline demonstrations with access to global state. The demonstrations do not include explicit communication signals; instead, agents learn to exchange and aggregate latent messages that encode interaction-relevant intent under partial observability. This formulation supports flexible inference at test time, where unconditioned generation corresponds to independent behavior and communication-conditioned generation enables coordinated interaction without centralized planning. The resulting policies operate in a fully decentralized manner at execution time, relying only on local observations and learned messages. Combined with a receding-horizon inference scheme, the proposed approach enables efficient single-step inference of short-horizon action sequences and degrades gracefully under communication dropouts. Extensive simulation results demonstrate near-expert collision avoidance performance and strong generalization to denser, unseen multi-agent scenarios, along with zero-shot transfer to real-robot experiments.

</details>


### [23] [VGFM: Expressive Robot Policies via Dense Value Guidance in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14261)
*Prajwal Koirala,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Value-Guided Flow Matching（VGFM），一种可扩展的离线强化学习框架，在基于流的策略中实现密集价值引导，同时避免 BPTT 和额外算法开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模离线交互数据训练控制策略，生成模型可提供丰富多模态动作表示，但多步生成策略的策略改进仍具挑战：在生成轨迹上引入基于价值的目标常带来显著训练复杂度，如时间反向传播（BPTT）、辅助架构或蒸馏损失。

Method: VGFM 将策略参数化为动作空间（x-prediction）中的条件流匹配模型，使每个中间流步骤都产生可由标准离线 RL critic 直接评估的有效机器人动作。价值引导可在随机采样的流时间上施加，无需对整个生成轨迹求导；同时通过改变底层流 ODE 的离散化保持推理时灵活性且无需重新训练。

Result: 在 OGBench 的机器人运动与操作任务上评估，VGFM 在严格评估协议下于广泛任务中取得强性能，且仅需极少超参数调优。

Conclusion: VGFM 为长时程、目标导向的机器人控制中的表达性策略学习提供了一种简单、可扩展且有效的方法。

Abstract: Recent robot learning paradigms increasingly rely on large offline datasets of robotic interactions to train control policies. Expressive generative models enable rich and multimodal action representations, expanding the capability of this paradigm for complex robotic control. However, policy improvement with multi-step generative actors remains challenging. In offline reinforcement learning (RL), incorporating value-based objectives along generative trajectories often introduces substantial training complexity, including backpropagation through time (BPTT), auxiliary architectures, or distillation losses. We propose Value-Guided Flow Matching (VGFM), a scalable offline RL framework that enables dense value-guided shaping within a flow-based policy while avoiding BPTT and additional algorithmic overhead. VGFM parameterizes the policy as a conditional flow-matching model in action (x-prediction) space, ensuring that each intermediate flow step produces a valid robot action that can be directly evaluated by a standard offline RL critic. This design allows value guidance to be applied at randomly sampled flow times without differentiating through the entire generative trajectory, while preserving inference-time flexibility by varying the discretization of the underlying flow ODE without retraining. Evaluated on robotic locomotion and manipulation tasks in OGBench, VGFM achieves strong performance across a wide range of tasks under rigorous evaluation protocols. With minimal hyperparameter tuning, these results demonstrate that VGFM provides a simple, scalable, and effective approach for expressive policy learning in long-horizon, goal-oriented robotic control.

</details>


### [24] [Task-Specified Active Metrological Inspection with Measurement-Steered VLA Manipulation and Deterministic Evidence Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14219)
*Zhiling Chen,Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Matthew P. Castanier,David Gorsich,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出FRAME——一种面向高混合低批量（HMLV）制造的分层双臂主动计量检测框架，将检测指令与结构化规格转化为可追溯的合格性证据，并通过确定性计量与准入校验避免不完整证据导致误判PASS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMLV制造中零件、规格与工单频繁变化，检测系统需免重复任务编程；现有检测自动化多假设传感序列预定义，通用机器人智能体又偏向任务完成而非计量证据的完整性与有效性。

Method: 形式化“任务指定的主动计量检测”，提出FRAME分层双臂框架：学习到的操作与标定激光轮廓测量协同；任务管理器对需求进行接地与调度，主动表面对应验证物理对象到规格的定位，证据记忆跟踪测量来源、可采信性与覆盖率；学习组件可提议检测目标与物理访问动作，但由确定性基准接地测量、可采信性检查、覆盖率审计与合格性评估阻止不完整或未验证证据授权PASS。

Result: 一系列物理实验表明，FRAME实现更高的端到端检测可靠性、更少的错误接受以及更短的任务完成时间。

Conclusion: FRAME通过确保计量证据的可追溯、可采信与覆盖完整，为HMLV制造中的任务指定主动计量检测提供了可靠且高效的机器人检测方案，并防止不充分证据触发PASS。

Abstract: High-mix low-volume (HMLV) manufacturing requires inspection systems to adapt to changing parts, specifications, and work orders without repeated task-specific programming. Existing inspection automation typically assumes predefined sensing sequences, while general purpose robot agents optimize task completion rather than the completeness and validity of metrological evidence. We formulate task-specified active metrological inspection and propose From Requirements to Admissible Metrological Evidence (FRAME), a hierarchical dual-arm framework that converts an inspection instruction and structured specification into traceable conformance evidence. FRAME coordinates learned manipulation with calibrated laser profilometry: a task manager grounds and schedules requirements, active surface correspondence verifies physical-to-specification localization, and evidence memory tracks measurement provenance, admissibility, and coverage. Learned components may propose inspection targets and physical access actions, but deterministic datum-grounded measurement, admissibility checks, coverage auditing, and conformance evaluation prevent incomplete or unverified evidence from authorizing PASS. A series of physical experiments shows that FRAME achieves higher end-to-end inspection reliability, fewer false accepts, and shorter task completion time.

</details>


### [25] [ITA-LaCAM: A Complete and Scalable TAPF Solver via Assignment-Aware Configuration-Space Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14208)
*Yimin Tang,Han Zhang,Shao-Hung Chan,Junsoo Kim,Erdem Bıyık,Sven Koenig,Jingkai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ITA-LaCAM 是一种受 LaCAM 与 ITA-CBS 启发的完备且可扩展的 TAPF 求解器，通过在每个联合构型节点维护智能体到目标的匹配，并在生成后继时增量修复匹配，实现自适应重分配并保持 LaCAM 的完备性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合目标分配与路径规划（TAPF）要求同时为智能体分配目标并规划无碰撞路径。现有方法难以在避免显式枚举组合分配空间的同时，兼顾完备性、可扩展性与自适应重分配能力。

Method: ITA-LaCAM 在每个联合构型节点中携带智能体到目标的匹配。当生成后继节点时，仅对移动过的智能体增量修复匹配，并利用这些目标引导 PIBT 的后继生成。该设计无需显式枚举组合分配空间，即可实现自适应重分配，同时保留 LaCAM 的完备性与可扩展性。

Result: 在 8 张地图、5--200 个智能体的 9,760 个基准实例上，ITA-LaCAM 求解了 100% 的实例，而配置 DBS-Hungarian 的 IR-TAPF 求解了 95.6%。在两者均求解的实例中，ITA-LaCAM 在 84.0% 的对比中更快找到初始解，并在 65.0% 的实例中取得更低的总成本。

Conclusion: ITA-LaCAM 是一种完备且可扩展的 TAPF 求解器，通过增量匹配修复与目标引导的 PIBT 后继生成，在求解成功率、初始解速度与解质量上均优于 IR-TAPF 等基线方法。

Abstract: Combined Target Assignment and Path Finding (TAPF) requires assigning targets for agents while simultaneously planning collision-free paths. We present ITA-LaCAM, a complete and scalable TAPF solver inspired by LaCAM and ITA-CBS. In ITA-LaCAM, each joint-configuration node carries an agent-to-target matching. When a successor is generated, ITA-LaCAM incrementally repairs the matching for the agents that moved and uses the targets to guide PIBT successor generation. This design enables adaptive reassignment without explicitly enumerating the combinatorial assignment space, while preserving LaCAM's completeness and scalability. Across 9,760 benchmark instances on eight maps with 5--200 agents, ITA-LaCAM solved 100% of the instances, compared with 95.6% for IR-TAPF configured with DBS-Hungarian. ITA-LaCAM found an initial solution faster in 84.0% of the comparisons and achieved a lower sum of costs in 65.0% of the instances solved by both methods.

</details>


### [26] [Novel Ex-vivo Calf Brain Model with Integrated Sub-Skull Force Sensors to Access Simulated Neurosurgical Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14198)
*Hamad Binhammad,Matheus Ballestero,Mohammed Babgi,Seana Shaka,Nima Hemati,Rothaina Saeedi,Aiden Mazidi,Bianca Giglio,Rukun Dou,Houssem-Eddine Gueziri,Amir Hooshiar,Rolando F. Del Maestro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出并验证了一种便携式离体力传感平台，用于在模拟神经外科手术中量化颅脑界面工具-组织相互作用力，并证明其具有高精度、低检测限和良好稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科组织操作要求高精度，但在真实条件下工具与组织间操作力的大小很少被量化；因此需要客观、高保真的力测量手段，以支持技能训练和绩效评估。

Method: 研究构建并验证便携式离体力学传感平台：将新鲜小牛脑组织作为脑实质生物替代物，置于3D打印人体颅骨模型中，配备6自由度力/扭矩传感器和实时数据采集系统。通过五项验证协议评估平台：与真值比较的准确性和动态保真度、使用0.5–50 g校准砝码的静态准确性和线性度、最小可检测力、不同解剖区域的空间一致性、手术铺巾影响以及长时间稳定性。

Result: 测量力与参考载荷高度一致（相关系数R=0.9997），RMSE<0.005 N，平均相对误差<2%；可可靠检测低至1 g（9.8 mN）的力；手术铺巾未引入明显信号失真；长时间记录漂移极小。

Conclusion: 该框架基于新鲜小牛脑组织，为技能训练和绩效评估提供了客观、高保真的力量化方法，并可作为其他外科手术中基于力的评估基础。未来工作将整合临床使用的外科器械以提高操作真实性，并推进临床试验以评估实用性、教育影响及实践相邻场景中的转化相关性。

Abstract: Surgical tissue manipulation demands precision; however, tool-tissue manipulation force magnitudes under realistic conditions are rarely quantified. To address this gap, we proposed and validated a portable ex-vivo force-sensing platform that measures tool-tissue interaction forces across the skull-brain interface during simulated neurosurgery. The system involves fresh calf brain tissue, used as a biological surrogate for brain parenchyma, placed in a 3D-printed human skull model equipped with a 6 degree-of-freedom force/torque sensor and a real-time data acquisition system. Five validation protocols assessed the accuracy and dynamic fidelity of the platform against ground-truth measurement, static accuracy and linearity using calibrated weights (0.5-50 g), minimum detectable force, spatial consistency across different anatomical regions, effect of surgical draping, and long-duration stability. Across protocols, measured forces showed excellent agreement with reference loads (correlation R = 0.9997), with RMSE < 0.005 N and mean relative error under 2%. The platform reliably detected low-magnitude forces down to 1 g (9.8 mN), while surgical drapes introduced no meaningful signal distortion and prolonged recordings exhibited minimal drift. Overall, the proposed framework provides objective, high-fidelity force quantification for skill training and performance assessment using fresh calf brain tissue and may serve as a foundation for force-based evaluation across other surgical procedures. Future work will integrate clinically used surgical instruments to increase procedural realism and will progress toward clinical trials to evaluate usability, educational impact, and translational relevance in practice-adjacent settings.

</details>


### [27] [DreamSat-Bench: Development and Initial Testing of a Testbed for AI-Based Pose Estimation from 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14183)
*Alex Posadas-Nava,August Berne,Giovanni Lavezzi,Kareena Shah,Alejandro Carrasco,Josiane Uwumukiza,Giacomo Battaglia,Paolo Panicucci,Minduli C. Wijayatunga,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并初步验证了DreamSat-Bench，一个用于基准测试AI相对导航技术的模块化交会与近距离操作（RPO）测试台，支持从数字仿真到物理现实的软硬件在环迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自主相对导航中仿真到现实差距大、缺乏统一基准，以及面对未准备空间资产时无先验几何模型可用的问题，需要可定制轨道环境与光照、能贯通仿真与硬件部署的测试平台。

Method: DreamSat-Bench集成软件在环与硬件在环机器人流水线，统一MuJoCo、Isaac Lab和LeRobot等机器人学习工具，并用机械臂复现3D轨迹；平台可定制轨道环境与光照。作者评估端到端视觉导航管线：DreamSat进行单视图3D重建，FoundationPose对未知航天器进行零样本6-DoF跟踪，并开展定点保持、绕飞表征等任务代表性轨道段测试及参数研究。

Result: 初步测试表明，该平台可量化重建延迟、网格分辨率、轨道距离和光照几何对位姿估计精度的影响；硬件在环初步验证了管线的物理部署，并识别出目标对称性和累积跟踪漂移是鲁棒导航的关键因素。

Conclusion: DreamSat-Bench为在无先验几何模型条件下成熟自主导航能力提供了严格框架，有助于推动针对未准备空间资产的相对导航从仿真走向物理部署。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and initial testing of DreamSat-Bench, a modular rendezvous and proximity operation testbed designed to benchmark AI-based relative navigation techniques. By integrating a software- and hardware-in-the-loop robotic pipeline, the platform enables a seamless transition from digital simulation to physical reality. DreamSat-Bench unifies state-of-the-art robotic learning tools such as MuJoCo, Isaac Lab, and LeRobot into a single benchmarking platform, utilizing robotic arms to trace 3D trajectories. The platform allows for extensive customization of orbital environments and lighting to evaluate the simulation-to-reality gap. We demonstrate the testbed's utility by evaluating an end-to-end vision-based navigation pipeline that pairs DreamSat, a generative AI framework for single-view 3D reconstruction, with FoundationPose for zero-shot 6-DoF tracking of unseen spacecraft. Initial testing explores mission-representative orbital segments, including fixed-point station-keeping and fly-around characterization. Through a series of parametric studies, we quantify the impacts of reconstruction latency, mesh resolution, orbital range, and illumination geometry on pose estimation accuracy. Finally, a preliminary hardware-in-the-loop campaign qualitatively validates the physical deployment of the pipeline, identifying target symmetry and accumulated tracking drift as critical factors for robust navigation. DreamSat-Bench provides a rigorous framework for maturing autonomous navigation with unprepared space assets in the absence of prior geometric models.

</details>


### [28] [GIFT: Glove-Inferred Force Transfer: Force-Aware Human-to-Robot Skill Transfer from a Wearable Sensing Glove to a Robot Hand Without Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14173)
*Tzah Sarusi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIFT 通过物理单位级力接口而非共享触觉传感器，将人类手套测得的指尖力以牛顿为单位迁移到机器人，并用电机电流残差估计力，在杯子抓握保持任务中实现低握持力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从传感手套到机器人的技能迁移通常依赖共享硬件，如同一只触觉手套或两个触觉传感器间的学习对齐，限制了跨本体部署。

Method: GIFT 使用可穿戴手套记录手指弯曲、已校准指尖力和腕部朝向，头戴相机记录演示，演示时无需机器人。部署时，机器人基于执行器电流相对自由空间基线的残差估计力，经校准映射为牛顿；策略在手套空间状态下预测手指位置目标，机器人仅通过重定向解码器和力估计器两个校准适配器接入。

Result: 在杯子抓握保持任务中，用相同演示训练两个 action-chunking 策略，一个有指尖力输入，一个将力输入置零。预设样本量和指标的 50 次 rollout 评估中，两个策略各 25 次全部成功；有力度输入时每次 rollout 保持阶段握力估计中位数低 53%（1.20 N 对 2.55 N，单侧 Mann-Whitney U 检验，p<0.0001）。观察消融中，仅视觉策略 0/15 次抓取成功，给手部命令状态的策略能抓取，而力输入决定握持力度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在人类手上测得的力通道可经五手套通道到七机器人执行器的重定向映射，在双方不共享传感器、机器人端无触觉硬件的情况下迁移到机器人手。

Abstract: Human-to-robot skill transfer from sensing gloves has so far relied on shared hardware: the same tactile glove worn by the demonstrator and the robot, or a learned alignment between two tactile sensors. We present GIFT (Glove-Inferred Force Transfer), a pipeline in which the interface between human and robot is a physical unit rather than a shared sensor: fingertip force is measured in newtons on the human side and estimated in newtons on the robot side. A wearable glove records finger flexion, calibrated fingertip force, and wrist orientation, while a head-mounted camera records the demonstration; no robot is present. At deployment, the robot estimates force from actuator-current residuals relative to a free-space baseline, through a calibrated mapping to newtons, so any position-controlled hand that reports motor current can serve as the deployment platform. The policy uses a glove-space state and predicts finger-position targets; the robot enters only through two calibrated adapters, a retargeting decoder and a force estimator. We evaluate GIFT on a cup grasp-and-hold task with two action-chunking policies trained on the same demonstrations, with fingertip-force inputs retained in one and zeroed in the other. In a 50-rollout evaluation with sample size and metrics fixed before scoring, both policies succeeded in all 25 rollouts. The median of the per-rollout hold-phase grip-force estimates was 53% lower with force inputs: 1.20 N versus 2.55 N (one-sided Mann-Whitney U, p<0.0001). In an observation ablation, a vision-only policy achieved 0/15 grasps, policies given hand-command state acquired the grasp, and the force inputs determined how hard the policy held. A force channel measured on the human hand thus transfers to a robot hand with no tactile hardware, through a retargeting map from five glove channels to seven robot actuators, with no sensor shared between the two.

</details>


### [29] [Visible Touch: Rendering Contact for Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14156)
*Metin Alp Dogan,Edward Sun,Feng Xu,Daniel Wu,Allen Peng,Dennis Hong,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Visible Touch：将接触信号以与视觉场景相同空间帧的可见形式呈现，使图像条件策略无需改架构即可利用触觉；配套低成本开源磁性接触传感器，在 LIBERO 与真实接触丰富任务上显著提升 BC-Transformer、miniVLA 和 π0.5 的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对稳健操作至关重要，但现代策略（包括预训练 VLA）通常仅依赖视觉与本体感觉。已有触觉融合方法需专用触觉硬件、独立触觉编码器或非图像策略骨干，与现代基于预训练 2D 视觉表示的图像条件策略不兼容。核心洞察是：瓶颈不在接触信息本身，而在其交付方式；若接触信号与策略已关注的场景处于同一空间帧，任何图像条件策略均可直接使用。

Method: 将接触信号暴露在与场景相同的空间帧中，实现无需架构变化的触觉集成，即 Visible Touch。配套设计低成本磁性接触传感器，开源并通过参数化 CAD-to-mold 流程由现货零件制造。在 LIBERO benchmark 上评估 BC-Transformer 与微调 miniVLA，在四个真实接触丰富任务上评估 π0.5，并进行接触集成策略的受控比较。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，Visible Touch 使 BC-Transformer 在 2-view 设置平均提升 15.7 个百分点，1-view 设置也有类似增益；微调预训练 VLA 时，miniVLA 在 LIBERO 平均提升 25 个百分点；π0.5 在四个真实接触丰富任务上使用定制传感器平均提升 30 个百分点。受控比较表明，接触集成策略显著影响触觉信息利用效果。

Conclusion: 将接触信息以与视觉同空间帧的可见形式交付，是一种兼容现代图像条件策略与 VLA 的有效触觉集成范式；无需专用策略架构即可获得显著性能提升，且低成本开源传感器方案具备可行性。

Abstract: Integrating contact information into visuomotor policies remains an open problem. Touch is essential to robust manipulation, yet most modern policies, including pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models, operate from vision and proprioception alone. Existing approaches to closing this gap require specialized tactile hardware, add separate tactile encoders, or commit to non-image policy backbones, all incompatible with the modern paradigm of image-conditioned policies built on pretrained 2D visual representations. Our key insight is that the bottleneck is not the contact information itself, but how it is delivered: when contact signals are exposed in the same spatial frame as the scene the policy already attends to, they become directly usable by any image-conditioned policy without architectural changes. We operationalize this insight in Visible Touch, paired with a custom low-cost magnetic contact sensor that is open-sourced and fabricated from off-the-shelf parts via a parametric CAD-to-mold pipeline. Across the LIBERO benchmark, Visible Touch improves BC-Transformer success by 15.7 percentage points on average in the 2-view setting, with similar gains in the 1-view setting; controlled comparisons show that the contact-integration strategy strongly affects how effectively tactile information is used. The pattern holds when fine-tuning pretrained VLAs: miniVLA on LIBERO gains 25 percentage points on average, and $\pi_{0.5}$ on four real-world contact-rich tasks gains 30 percentage points with our custom sensor.

</details>


### [30] [When Faster VLA Deployment Changes Closed-Loop Behavior: Task Success-Latency Analysis of SmolVLA Across PyTorch and ONNX Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14146)
*Rafiqul Islam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在RTX 2060上对VLA模型smolvla_libero进行PyTorch+AMP与ONNX Runtime CUDA EP的闭环对比评测：ONNX虽把tether-inspect p99从1181 ms降到601/532 ms，但Spatial成功率从70.0%跌至41.0%/40.0%；图审计发现所谓FP16/INT8产物实为字节相同的FP32图。静态语言宽度消融表明宽度24/32可将Spatial成功率恢复到75.0%/71.0%，且宽度24的ONNX在约一半延迟下与PyTorch+AMP基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型部署到加速推理运行时（如ONNX Runtime）虽能降低推理延迟，却可能改变闭环任务行为，因此仅报告延迟不足以评估部署方案，需要将延迟与闭环成功率联合考察。

Method: 在RTX 2060（6 GB）上，基于MuJoCo 3.3.2与LeRobot 0.6.1、固定seed 42，对HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero在LIBERO Spatial与Object两个suite上进行评测：主评测每suite 100 episodes，另做每suite 300 episodes的配对rollout；对比PyTorch+AMP与ONNX Runtime CUDA Execution Provider（CUDA EP），并对ONNX产物做图审计以核验其数值精度；同时进行静态语言token宽度消融（16/24/32）。

Result: PyTorch+AMP在Spatial/Object上成功率为70.0%/88.0%，tether-inspect p99为1181 ms；宣称的FP16与INT8 ONNX将p99降至601 ms与532 ms，但Spatial成功率跌至41.0%与40.0%，Object保持89.0%。图审计显示这两个产物均为字节相同的FP32图，故requested-INT8并非算子级INT8量化。语言宽度消融16/24/32对应Spatial成功率41.0%/75.0%/71.0%，而Object成功率与均匀基准延迟基本稳定。宽度24的ONNX在Spatial上成功率与PyTorch+AMP基线相当（Wilson置信区间重叠，双比例卡方p=0.53），延迟约为其一半。

Conclusion: 上下文（语言）宽度是该部署栈中影响Spatial闭环成功率的重要因素，但不能解释PyTorch与ONNX之间的全部差异；部署评测应同时报告延迟、推理产物审计、接口约束与闭环成功率，以避免加速带来的行为退化被掩盖。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) deployment can reduce inference latency while changing closed-loop task behavior. We evaluate HuggingFaceVLA/smolvla_libero on an RTX 2060 (6 GB) in LIBERO Spatial and Object (MuJoCo 3.3.2, LeRobot 0.6.1, seed 42), comparing PyTorch+AMP with ONNX Runtime CUDA Execution Provider (CUDA EP). The main evaluation uses 100 episodes/suite; a paired rollout uses 300 episodes/suite. PyTorch+AMP reaches 70.0%/88.0% Spatial/Object success at 1181 ms p99. Requested-FP16 and requested-INT8 ONNX reduce tether-inspect p99 to 601 ms and 532 ms, while Spatial success falls to 41.0% and 40.0% and Object remains at 89.0%. A graph audit shows those artifacts are byte-identical FP32 graphs, so the requested-INT8 row is not operator-level INT8 quantization. A static language-width ablation (16/24/32 tokens) yields Spatial success of 41.0%, 75.0%, and 71.0%; widths 24 and 32 recover much of the Spatial drop while Object success and uniform-bench latency stay approximately stable. Width-24 ONNX Spatial success is comparable to the PyTorch+AMP baseline at roughly half the latency (Wilson intervals overlap; two-proportion chi-squared p=0.53). Context width is an important contributor in this stack; it does not account for every PyTorch-vs-ONNX difference. Deployment evaluation should jointly report latency, artifact inspection, interface constraints, and closed-loop success. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [31] [ResSafe: Learning Safety Filtering with Residual Reinforcement Learning for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15988)
*Gechen Qu,Tong Zhang,Bike Zhang,Yen-Jen Wang,Koushil Sreenath,Claire Tomlin,Jason Jangho Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用残差强化学习作为隐式安全过滤机制，将人形机器人控制中的任务性能与安全解耦：名义策略专注任务，残差策略学习安全修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人高维动力学、接触丰富交互和对扰动敏感，使得安全控制困难；尽管强化学习已能实现有效运动与运动跟踪，学习策略仍可能产生导致失稳或跌倒的不安全动作。单一名义策略难以同时兼顾性能、安全与鲁棒性，且多目标奖励项调参困难。

Method: 采用残差强化学习框架，将性能与安全解耦：名义策略仅优化任务性能，残差策略学习安全修正动作。该残差策略被用作隐式安全过滤器，而非依赖显式安全约束或单一策略内竞争性奖励项的精细调参。

Result: 该解耦方式带来更好的性能—安全帕累托权衡，并避免在单一策略训练中仔细调节多个相互竞争的奖励项；实验表明残差策略能够充当隐式安全过滤器。

Conclusion: 残差强化学习可作为一种隐式安全过滤机制，通过解耦任务性能与安全修正，为人形机器人安全控制提供更优的性能—安全折中。

Abstract: Safe control of humanoid robots remains challenging due to their high-dimensional dynamics, contact-rich interactions, and sensitivity to disturbances. Although reinforcement learning has enabled effective locomotion and motion tracking, learned policies can still generate unsafe actions that lead to instability or falls. In this work, we propose residual reinforcement learning as an implicit safety-filtering mechanism for safe humanoid control. Instead of relying on a single nominal policy to simultaneously balance performance, safety, and robustness, we decouple performance and safety. The nominal policy focuses solely on task performance, while a residual policy learns safety corrections. This decoupling leads to a better performance--safety Pareto trade-off and avoids the need for careful tuning of multiple competing reward terms within a single policy training. We show that the residual policy can act as an implicit safety filter.

</details>


### [32] [MessyMem: Learning-from-Doing Memory for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15976)
*Anuva Banwasi,William Muckelroy III,Priya Sundaresan,Linfeng Zhao,Jeannette Bohg,Cherie Ho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MessyMem，一种面向移动操作机器人的持久记忆系统，使其能跨任务复用交互经验，并在仿真与真机实验中显著提升长时程任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人常将每个任务视为全新任务：紧凑场景表示缺失交互获得的知识，原始视频历史难以查询，VLM 规划器仅在推理时推理且不持久更新知识。因此，机器人无法在发现柜子锁住或抽屉中物体后复用这些经验。

Method: MessyMem 维护空间落地的 3D 场景图，编码物体与位置；通过交互学习属性与结果并增强场景图；关联视觉观测以支持细粒度回忆。该系统在仿真和真实移动操作机器人上评估。

Result: 在跨越 3 小时以上的连续 25 任务仿真中，MessyMem 达到 80.0% 任务进度，分别比最强消融高 14.8 个百分点、比最强外部基线高 28.9 个百分点；同时能从数千个存储关键帧及超过一小时前的历史中检索任务相关证据。

Conclusion: 持久记忆系统可让移动操作机器人从经验中学习并跨未来任务复用知识，从而提升长时程、多房间、多访问场景下的任务性能。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators deployed across many rooms and visits should improve with experience: after discovering that a cabinet is locked or finding an object in a drawer, the robot should reuse that knowledge rather than start each task from scratch. Yet today's robots often treat each task as new: compact scene representations omit interaction-derived knowledge, raw video histories are difficult to query, and VLM planners reason at inference time without persistently updating what the robot knows. We present MessyMem, a persistent memory system that enables mobile manipulators to learn from experience and reuse that knowledge across future tasks. It maintains a spatially grounded 3D scene graph of objects and locations, augments it with properties and outcomes learned through interaction, and links visual observations for fine-grained recall. We evaluate MessyMem in simulation and on a real mobile manipulator. In a continuous 25-task simulation spanning over 3 hours, MessyMem achieves 80.0% task progress, outperforming the strongest ablation by 14.8 percentage points and the strongest external baseline by 28.9 points, while retrieving task-relevant evidence from thousands of stored keyframes and over an hour into the past.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Single-Axis Testing: Paired Evaluation of Compound Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15940)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIBERO-CTRL 六轴基准，匹配评估视觉-语言-动作策略在单轴扰动与同时多轴扰动下的表现，揭示聚合成功率会掩盖复合鲁棒性的真实变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略通常逐个扰动评估，虽能诊断对单一分布偏移的敏感性，但真实部署常同时遭遇多种偏移，单轴鲁棒性指标能否组合推断复合鲁棒性尚不明确。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-CTRL：六轴基准，对每个初始状态配对单轴条件和匹配的同时多轴条件，从而区分两类相反结果变化：涌现失败（单轴全成功但同时失败）与补偿成功（至少一个单轴失败但同时成功）。

Result: 两类相反转变可在聚合层面相互抵消：即使两种转变率之差统计上不显著，仍有高达 29.0% 的匹配初始状态结果改变；在六个策略和三个严重度下，最受影响条件中结果变化达 34.5%。两类转变的相对比例随策略和严重度变化，且随机策略独立重评估下转变率相近。

Conclusion: 复合鲁棒性不能仅由聚合的单轴成功率刻画；需要匹配的逐实例评估，才能揭示联合扰动如何改变策略行为。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are typically evaluated one perturbation at a time, providing a useful diagnosis of their sensitivity to individual distribution shifts. Real-world deployment, however, may involve several shifts simultaneously, and it remains unclear how these individual robustness measurements compose. We ask whether compound robustness can be inferred from single-axis evaluations. We introduce LIBERO-CTRL, a six-axis benchmark that pairs each initial state across single-axis conditions and a matched simultaneous condition. This design reveals two opposing outcome changes that aggregate success rates cannot distinguish: emergent failures, where all single-axis rollouts succeed but the simultaneous rollout fails, and compensated successes, where at least one single-axis rollout fails but the simultaneous rollout succeeds. Because one transition decreases compound success while the other increases it, they can cancel, making aggregate compound performance appear consistent with single-axis measurements even when individual outcomes differ substantially. These opposing transitions can largely cancel in aggregate: even when the difference between the two transition rates is not statistically distinguishable from zero, as many as 29.0% of matched initial states still change outcome. Across six policies and three severity levels, such outcome changes reach 34.5% in the most affected condition. The relative prevalence of the two transitions varies across policies and severities, while the transition rates remain similar under independent re-evaluation of stochastic policies. Compound robustness therefore cannot be characterized from aggregate single-axis success rates alone; matched per-instance evaluation is needed to reveal how joint perturbations alter behavior.

</details>


### [34] [Touch2Trace: Tactile-Driven Imitation Learning for Dexterous Cable Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15921)
*Matteo Grimaldi,David Klee,Ziling Chen,Tong Jian,Wonju Lee,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Saleh Nabi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Touch2Trace 提出一种基于触觉的模仿学习系统，用于灵巧线缆追踪，并通过真实实验系统评估编码器预训练、控制频率、时间上下文与空间分辨率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作可变形物体需要指尖级持续调节压力、摩擦与初始滑移；其中线缆追踪极具挑战，需拇指与食指反复捏合与卷曲将线缆送入手中。

Method: 提出 Touch2Trace：面向定制 32×32 压阻传感器 TacV5，用自监督学习预训练触觉编码器；基于遥操作演示，用行为克隆训练轻量 Transformer 策略；在 Tesollo DG-5F 手上以 60 Hz 部署。系统不依赖视觉或显式线缆状态估计。

Result: 相较仅本体感觉基线，触觉反馈将平均追踪距离从 0.2 cm 提升至 20.1 cm，成功率从 0% 提升至 93%，并实现对未见线缆与布线条件的零样本迁移；同时给出编码器预训练、控制频率、时间上下文和空间分辨率对策略性能的系统性真实世界表征。

Conclusion: 结果表明触觉驱动策略可显著提升可变形物体灵巧操作的可靠性，并量化了触觉系统中关键参数的影响。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation of deformable objects demands continuous fingertip-level regulation of pressure, friction, and incipient slip. We study one of the most challenging cases: dexterous cable tracing, feeding a cable through the hand with repeated pinch-and-curl motions of the thumb and index finger. We introduce Touch2Trace, a tactile-driven imitation-learning system for this task, and provide, to our knowledge, the first systematic real-world characterization of how encoder pretraining, control rate, temporal context, and spatial resolution each shape policy performance. The winning learning recipe combines a tactile encoder pretrained for a custom 32 x 32 piezoresistive sensor (TacV5) via self-supervised learning with a lightweight transformer policy trained on teleoperated demonstrations via behavior cloning, deployed at 60 Hz on a Tesollo DG-5F hand. Tactile feedback without vision or explicit cable-state estimation significantly improves tracing performance versus a proprioception-only baseline: from 0.2 cm to 20.1 cm mean distance and 0% to 93% success rate, with zero-shot transfer to unseen cables and routing conditions. The results quantify the influence of key parameters in tactile-driven systems for reliable dexterous deformable object manipulation.

</details>


### [35] [SlipSense: Multimodal Tactile Learning for Low-Latency and Generalized Slip Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15910)
*Tong Jian,Aditya Thurvas Senthil Kumar,Xinyi Li,Ziling Chen,Tianyu Dai,Ali Sengul,Matteo Grimaldi,Wenjie Lu,Saleh Nabi,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SlipSense 多模态触觉滑动检测框架，基于 TacV5 传感器融合 32×32 压阻阵列与三轴 MEMS 加速度计，实现低延迟、低误报且可零样本跨平台泛化的滑动检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动检测对灵巧操作至关重要，但现有系统通常缺乏对检测延迟的精确表征以及跨平台泛化能力。

Method: 构建 TacV5 紧凑传感器：32×32 压阻阵列以 240 Hz 捕捉空间压力分布，三轴 MEMS 加速度计以 8 kHz 捕捉摩擦诱发振动，提供互补滑动线索；SlipSense 框架执行模态特定编码、传感器内融合、跨模态注意力与 240 Hz 因果时序预测；在覆盖 37 个物体、140 万帧的数据集上实验。

Result: 实验证明两种模态具有互补性；SlipSense 达到 96.7% Macro F1，假阳性率低于 1.6%，并在 23.1 ms 内检测出 76% 的滑动事件；仅用 UMI 数据训练时，无需重训练即可零样本泛化到 Tesollo 灵巧手，并跨未见物体、不同传感器单元和机器人平台迁移。

Conclusion: 结果表明，高频多模态触觉融合可显著提升滑动检测的精度、时效性与跨平台泛化能力，为灵巧操作中的可靠滑动感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Slip detection is fundamental to dexterous manipulation, yet existing systems often lack precise characterization of detection latency and cross-platform generalization. We present SlipSense, a multimodal tactile slip-detection framework built on TacV5, a compact sensor integrating a $32 \times 32$ piezoresistive array operating at 240 Hz and a 3-axis MEMS accelerometer operating at 8 kHz. The piezoresistive array captures spatial pressure distributions, while the accelerometer captures friction-induced vibrations, providing complementary slip cues. The framework performs modality-specific encoding, intra-sensor fusion, and cross-modal attention with causal temporal prediction at 240 Hz.
