Experiments on a dataset of 1.4 million frames spanning 37 objects demonstrate the complementarity of the two modalities. SlipSense achieves 96.7% Macro F1 with a false-positive rate below 1.6%, detecting 76% of slip events within 23.1 ms. When trained solely on UMI data, SlipSense generalizes zero-shot to a Tesollo dexterous hand, transferring across unseen objects, distinct sensor units, and robotic platforms without retraining.

</details>


### [36] [Goal-Oriented Communications for Physical AI: Design and Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15895)
*Shutong Chen,Wenkai Zhang,Adnan Aijaz,Miao Guo,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了面向Physical AI的端到端Goal-oriented Communication (GoC)测试床，并实现基于3D边界框、2D场景图和3D场景图三种语义表示的GoC框架；实验表明相较周期性传输原始图像，任务完成时间最多降低52.6%，成功率最多提升45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI需要频繁更新且时延敏感的 video stream 来感知、推理和交互，数据量高且时延要求严格，现有5G网络难以支撑。GoC通过只传输任务相关语义表示有望解决该挑战，但已有GoC框架主要在仿真中评估，尚未在Physical AI实际部署中验证其有效性。

Method: 搭建端到端GoC测试床：PiPER机械臂搭载RGB-D相机与5G模组，经5G OpenAirInterface网络连接NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin边缘服务器。提出并实现三种GoC框架，分别传输3D bounding boxes、2D scene graphs、3D scene graphs作为语义表示；三者共享面向闭环Physical AI的功能模块，包括语义提取、全栈5G传输、语言模型推理、digital twin validation与机器人控制。

Result: 在测试床上进行大量实验，相较周期性传输原始图像数据的传统框架，所提GoC框架将任务完成时间最多减少52.6%，任务成功概率最多提升45%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了GoC框架在实际部署中的有效性，为未来6G网络中高效、可靠的Physical AI应用铺平道路。

Abstract: Physical AI relies on frequently-updated, latency-sensitive video stream to perceive, reason, and interact with the physical world, resulting in strict latency requirements with much higher data volumes that existing 5G networks cannot support. Goal-oriented communication (GoC) offers as a promising approach to solve this challenge by transmitting only task-relevant semantic representations. However, existing GoC frameworks were mainly evaluated in the simulations while their effectiveness has never been validated in a practical deployment of physical AI application. In this work, we develop an end-to-end GoC testbed for Physical AI, which connects a PiPER robot arm equipped with an RGB-D camera and a 5G modem to an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin edge server through a 5G OpenAirInterface network. We propose and implement three GoC frameworks that transmit 3D bounding boxes, 2D scene graphs, and 3D scene graphs, as three types of semantic representations, respectively. They share the common functional modules designed for closed-loop Physical AI applications, including semantic extraction, full stack 5G transmission, language model inference, digital twin validation, and robotic control. Extensive experiments on our testbed show that our GoC frameworks reduce the task completion time by up to 52.6% and improve task success probability by up to 45%, compared to the traditional framework that periodically transmits the raw image data. These results validate the practical effectiveness of our GoC framework and pave the way for efficient and reliable Physical AI applications over future 6G networks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [37] [WLA$^3$: World Latent Action Modeling for Semantics, Dynamics, and Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15870)
*Peidong Liu,Zhiyuan Xiang,Mingyang Li,Wenhao Li,Jiale Zhang,Jiahao Sun,Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WLA$^3$ 提出以世界潜在动作模型（WLAM）为核心的通才策略框架，利用异构数据中的世界状态变化学习统一、低噪声的动作监督，并联合建模语义、动力学与运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略模型受限于异构数据缺乏统一且低噪声的动作监督；人类自我中心视频虽多，但仅少量具有高质量手部动作标签。观测到的世界状态转移可作为跨数据源共享的动作相关监督信号。

Method: 构建 WLAM：在局部时间窗口内学习多模态世界状态如何变化，将同步相机视角与可用的具身状态变化编码为紧凑局部潜在动作和更丰富的转移特征；通过部分模态重建与重叠窗口一致性增强鲁棒性。WLA$^3$ 复用该表示于三方面：局部潜在动作支持动作敏感物理动力学建模；片段级特征经 Semantic Latent Aggregate（SLA）直接监督 VLM；动作专家联合预测潜在动作与具身特定机器人控制。人类视频提供可扩展转移监督，机器人轨迹将共享表示落地为可执行原生控制。

Result: 在 LARYBench 上，最终 32D 潜在动作达到 67.89% 平均分类准确率；在六个真实机器人任务上，WLA$^3$ 平均成功率为 81.9%，优于 $\pi_{0.5}$ 的 66.2%。性能随通才策略模型中训练数据规模扩大而提升，人类视频支持人到机器人迁移。

Conclusion: WLA$^3$ 表明，基于世界潜在动作的统一表示可有效利用异构人类视频与机器人轨迹，提升通用策略模型的可扩展性、机器人任务成功率及人到机器人迁移能力。

Abstract: Scaling generalist policy models with heterogeneous data is limited by the lack of unified, low-noise action supervision. Human egocentric videos are abundant, but only a small fraction comes with high-quality hand-action labels. Observed world transitions offer a common source of action-related supervision across data sources. We introduce WLA$^3$ (World Latent Action Modeling for Semantics, Dynamics, and Kinematics), a unified generalist policy model framework built around representations learned by a World Latent Action Model (WLAM). WLAM first learns how multimodal world states change over a local interval, encoding synchronized camera views and available embodiment-state changes into a compact local latent action and a richer transition feature. Reconstruction from partial modalities and consistency across overlapping windows encourage robust transition representations. WLA$^3$ reuses them across semantics, dynamics, and kinematics: local latent actions support action-sensitive physical-dynamics modeling, segment-level features directly supervise the VLM through a Semantic Latent Aggregate (SLA), and an action expert jointly predicts latent actions together with embodiment-specific robot controls. Human videos provide scalable transition supervision, while robot trajectories ground the shared representation in executable native controls. On LARYBench, the final 32D latent action reaches 67.89\% average classification accuracy. WLA$^3$ achieves 81.9% average success across six real-robot tasks versus 66.2% for $\pi_{0.5}$. Performance improves as generalist policy model mid-training data scales, and human videos support human-to-robot transfer. Project page can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [38] [DuctAM: A Duct-Assisted Quadrotor-Based Aerial Manipulator Enabling High-Force Push-and-Pull Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15861)
*Yi Wang,Rui Jin,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Yizhuo Yang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型空中操作平台DuctAM，通过沿交互轴集成的两个涵道风扇与姿态-力解耦控制，显著增强推拉交互所需的水平力，同时保持稳定飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人空中操作器（UAM）将无人机能力从感知扩展到物理交互；推拉是需持续水平力且保持稳定飞行的基本操作原语，但传统无人机需大幅改变姿态才能产生水平力，限制交互能力。

Method: DuctAM在四旋翼交互轴上集成两个涵道风扇，采用姿态-力解耦控制方案，在不依赖大姿态变化的情况下实现可控水平力；并通过大量真实世界实验验证。

Result: 八字轨迹跟踪实验在四旋翼与涵道模式下均实现稳定飞行与精确运动控制；力测量实验量化了解耦纵向力能力；推车、关门、开抽屉等推拉任务验证了实用性；相比传统无人机，在保持稳定飞行的同时显著提升水平交互力。

Conclusion: DuctAM有效提升了水平交互力能力并保持稳定飞行，验证了其在推拉操作中的实用性与优越性。

Abstract: Uncrewed Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) extend the capabilities of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) from perception to physical interaction. Among various aerial interactions, push-and-pull operations are fundamental manipulation primitives that require sustained horizontal forces while maintaining stable flight. In this paper, we propose DuctAM, a compact aerial manipulation platform that enhances horizontal force capability for push-and-pull interactions using two ducted fans integrated along the quadrotor interaction axis. An attitude-force decoupled control scheme enables controllable horizontal forces without requiring large attitude changes. Extensive real-world experiments are conducted to validate the DuctAM. Figure-eight trajectory tracking experiments demonstrate stable flight and accurate motion control in both quad and duct modes. Force-measurement experiments quantify the decoupled longitudinal force capability of DuctAM. Finally, representative push-and-pull interaction tasks, including cart pushing, door closing, and drawer opening, verify the practical effectiveness of DuctAM. The results show that DuctAM achieves significantly improved horizontal interaction force capability while maintaining stable flight compared with conventional UAVs.

</details>


### [39] [Uncertainty-Guided Sparse Refinement for Action Chunking Transformer Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15840)
*Chenyang Wang,Yuntian Wang,Xiaoxiong Yang,Dingde Jiang,Siao Liu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出不确定性引导细化（UGR），一种针对分块式视觉运动策略的稀疏细化框架，通过估计每步时序不确定性并仅对最不确定的时间步进行残差修正，在RoboTwin五个双臂操作任务上多数取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块动作预测方法常失败于少数关键时间步的预测误差，而非整个动作块均匀误差；因此均匀细化效率低且缺乏针对性，需要将修正集中在最关键的时间步上。

Method: UGR采用粗到细设计：先预测完整动作块，再从粗层隐藏状态估计每步时序不确定性；随后用二值掩码选出最不确定的时间步，并仅对这些步施加残差校正。不确定性分支与粗动作预测器解耦，以明确细化增益来自不确定性引导修正，而非额外预测器容量。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准的五个双臂操作任务中，UGR在四个任务上取得最佳成功率，相较ACT基线最多提升13个百分点；消融实验表明其优于全块细化和位置无关块细化。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的稀疏细化能有效定位并修正关键时间步误差，从而提升分块式视觉运动策略在长时程机器人操作中的性能。

Abstract: Learning chunk-based visuomotor policies for long-horizon robot manipulation remains challenging. Recent action-chunking methods have shown promising performance by predicting temporally extended action sequences. However, their failures are often dominated by prediction errors at a small number of critical timesteps rather than uniformly poor predictions across the entire action chunk, making uniform refinement inefficient and insufficiently targeted. To address this bottleneck, we propose Uncertainty-Guided Refinement (UGR), a sparse refinement framework for chunk-based visuomotor policies. Specifically, UGR follows a coarse-to-refine design: it first predicts a full action chunk, estimates per-step temporal uncertainty from the coarse hidden states, and applies residual correction only to the most uncertain timesteps selected by a binary mask. The uncertainty branch is decoupled from the coarse action predictor, enabling clean attribution of the refinement gains to uncertainty-guided correction rather than additional predictor capacity. Extensive experiments on five dual-arm manipulation tasks from the RoboTwin benchmark show that UGR achieves the best success rate on four tasks, improves over the ACT baseline by up to 13% absolute, and outperforms both full-chunk and position-agnostic block refinement in ablation studies.

</details>


### [40] [JEPLO: Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning for LiDAR-Based Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15770)
*Qihao Yuan,Yixuan Qiu,Ziyu Cao,Ming Cao,Kailai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPLO：面向腿足机器人的单阶段、无建图LiDAR感知运动学习框架，通过PE-JEPA世界模型与CJTS师生训练管线学习自中心地形表征，实现仿真到现实迁移，并在遮挡、稀疏和噪声等退化感知下保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR在感知腿足运动中的探索少于RGB-D，且现有LiDAR方法通常依赖显式建图；因此需要一种无建图、轻量且鲁棒的LiDAR感知运动方案。

Method: 提出JEPLO单阶段学习框架；设计本体-外感JEPA（PE-JEPA）世界模型，从机载观测（包括原始LiDAR扫描）学习预测性自中心地形表征；进一步提出并行JEPA师生（CJTS）管线，在仿真中用深度强化学习和简单奖励公式训练由JEPA潜在表征引导的运动策略。

Result: 实现成功的sim-to-real迁移，支持全向穿越多样地形，包括长楼梯和高箱，且机载计算轻量；相比现有感知运动框架，在遮挡、稀疏和噪声导致的感知退化下更鲁棒；分析验证JEPLO能在挑战条件下保留任务相关信息。

Conclusion: JEPLO为无建图、LiDAR感知的腿足运动提供了有效框架，提升了退化感知鲁棒性，并开源了实现、实验数据集和硬件设计。

Abstract: Light detection and ranging (LiDAR) remains less explored than RGB-D sensing for perceptive legged locomotion, and existing LiDAR-based approaches often rely on explicit mapping. We present JEPLO (Joint-Embedding Predictive learning for legged LOcomotion), a single-stage learning framework for mapping-free, LiDAR-based perceptive locomotion for legged robots. We introduce a proprio-exteroceptive JEPA (PE-JEPA) world model to learn predictive egocentric terrain representations from onboard observations, including raw LiDAR scans. A concurrent JEPA-teacher-student (CJTS) pipeline is further proposed to train a locomotion policy informed by JEPA latent representations in simulation using deep reinforcement learning with a simple reward formulation. The framework achieves successful sim-to-real transfer, enabling omnidirectional traversal of diverse terrains, including long staircases and high boxes, with lightweight onboard computation. Evaluations demonstrate greater robustness than existing perceptive locomotion frameworks, particularly under degraded perception caused by occlusion, sparsity and noise. Further analysis validates JEPLO's ability to retain task-relevant information under these challenging conditions. We open-source our implementation, experimental datasets, and hardware setup designs this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [Bench2Dex: Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation Across Dexterous Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15726)
*Zhenjie Yang,Yideng Zhang,Dongjie Zhang,Chenyu Jiang,Xianshuai Liu,Yufeng Li,Zuhao Ge,Xingyu Jiao,Zheng Zhang,Kaiyu He,He Wang,Yuwen Zhong,Yi Deng,Muyun Jiang,Xianliang Huang,Haisheng Su,Donghang Zhang,Jian Zhang,Xue Yang,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bench2Dex：一个跨12种灵巧手的视触觉双臂操作仿真基准，包含统一触觉接口、26个任务、约1.3K人类遥操作演示及鲁棒性扰动评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉能提供视觉难以推断的接触信息，但灵巧手触觉硬件尚未统一，不同手在手指结构、接触表面和传感器布局上差异大，仿真触觉信号也与物理传感器测量存在差异，导致难以在一致实验设定下研究跨多种灵巧手的视触觉操作。

Method: 改造现有机器人模型，加入共享仿真触觉接口，将局部接触几何转换为类图像触觉观测，为不同手形态提供一致观测格式，而不复现特定物理触觉传感器输出。基准包含26个双臂操作任务（工具使用、铰接物体交互、多阶段操作）和约1.3K人类遥操作演示，提供同步视觉、触觉、本体感觉、动作和物体状态观测及可执行任务指标。鲁棒性方面，将七类扰动分为不变性轴（正确动作不变）和等变性轴（正确动作随扰动变化）。在Bench2Dex上评估ACT、Diffusion Policy、pi0.5和GR00T N1.5。

Result: 在Bench2Dex上评估了ACT、Diffusion Policy、pi0.5和GR00T N1.5，并报告了它们的性能与失败模式。

Conclusion: Bench2Dex旨在作为研究跨灵巧手视触觉学习的平台；它不假设仿真触觉观测可替代真实触觉感知，而是在触觉硬件与仿真模型仍发展时，为算法开发提供共享设定。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides contact information that can be difficult to infer from vision alone, but tactile hardware for dexterous hands has not converged to a common design. Dexterous hands differ in finger structure, contact surfaces, and sensor layouts, while simulated tactile signals still differ from measurements produced by physical sensors. These factors make it difficult to study visuo-tactile manipulation across diverse dexterous hands within a consistent experimental setting. We present Bench2Dex, a simulation benchmark for visuo-tactile bimanual manipulation across 12 dexterous hands. We adapt existing robot models with a shared simulated tactile interface that converts local contact geometry into image-like tactile observations. The interface provides a consistent observation format across different hand morphologies without attempting to reproduce the output of a specific physical tactile sensor. Bench2Dex includes 26 bimanual manipulation tasks that involve tool use, articulated-object interaction, and multi-stage manipulation, together with about 1.3K human-teleoperated demonstrations. The benchmark provides synchronized visual, tactile, proprioceptive, action, and object-state observations, together with executable task metrics. For robustness, we group seven perturbation types into invariance axis, where the correct action does not change, and equivariance axis, where the correct action changes together with the perturbation. We evaluate ACT, Diffusion Policy, pi0.5, and GR00T N1.5 on Bench2Dex and report their performance and failure modes. Bench2Dex is meant as a platform for studying visuo-tactile learning across dexterous hands. It does not assume that simulated tactile observations can replace real tactile sensing; it offers a shared setting for algorithm development while tactile hardware and simulation models are still evolving.

</details>


### [42] [Continuous Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting for Contact-Rich Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15716)
*Jiahao Liu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMDIR将固定阻抗演示转换为连续可变阻抗控制器，并构建TMIR作为模仿学习的结构化监督；在真实接触任务中优于离散MDIR，FastMPO显著加速，但下游完成可靠性仍不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定阻抗演示与现有MDIR难以生成连续可变阻抗控制，也缺乏可用于模仿学习的结构化监督；需要同时提升任务保持、降低姿态偏差/力波动/峰值力，并提高求解效率。

Method: 提出CMDIR扩展MDIR；用TMIR将演化任务坐标系与控制器指令配对；通过Demo-relative Compromise动力学保留移动基座传输及控制/物理度量不匹配，导出位移、反作用冲量与扰动敏感度准则；Quality-to-Fast在预定义有限空间中从开发路径自动编译求解器结构，对每个演示重新实例化，并用多分辨率评估认证候选；FastMPO作为快速求解方案。

Result: 在3个真实接触任务、225次重定向控制器试验中，完整CMDIR相比离散MDIR提高平均任务代理保持率，并降低平均姿态偏差、力波动和峰值力。FastMPO相对C-MPO取得5.8–9.4倍加速，闭环结果相当。下游实验表明完整TMIR监督界面可学习；成功执行中力波动和峰值力更低，但不同任务与环境下的完成可靠性仍不均衡。

Conclusion: CMDIR/TMIR可将固定阻抗演示转化为可学习的连续可变阻抗控制监督，兼顾性能与效率；但跨任务和环境的完成可靠性仍是局限，需进一步改进。

Abstract: CMDIR extends Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) to transform fixed-impedance demonstrations into continuous variable-impedance controllers, which can also serve as structured supervision for imitation learning. Continuous Task-Manifold Impedance Representation (TMIR) pairs an evolving task frame with controller instructions. Demo-relative Compromise dynamics retain moving-basis transport and control/physical metric mismatch, yielding displacement, reaction-impulse, and perturbation-sensitivity criteria. Quality-to-Fast automatically compiles a solver structure from development paths within a predefined finite space, re-instantiates that structure for each demonstration, and certifies the resulting candidate by multi-resolution evaluation. Across 225 retargeted-controller trials in three real contact tasks, full CMDIR improves mean task-proxy retention and reduces mean pose deviation, force fluctuation, and peak force relative to discrete MDIR. FastMPO achieves a $5.8$--$9.4\times$ speedup over C-MPO with comparable closed-loop outcomes. Downstream experiments demonstrate learnability of the complete TMIR supervision interface; lower force fluctuation and peak force are observed among successful executions, while completion reliability remains uneven across tasks and environments.

</details>


### [43] [Volumetric Harmonic Field Navigation for Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15680)
*Shuxiu Jia,Amartya Mukherjee,Yating Yuan,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次在实体四旋翼上验证体积调和场导航：将预计算体积调和场与约束预测规划器耦合，规划器在预测位置查询场值而非提取全局参考路径；结构化3D与长迷宫测试中，调和引导相比Dijkstra引导具有更大最小间隙和更低RMS jerk，但路径更长，并由Crazyflie实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱3D环境中的四旋翼导航需要全局引导，同时局部运动受碰撞与运动学/动力学约束；调和势虽能通过全局边值问题提供稠密引导，但将体积调和场与受约束的实体四旋翼运动耦合仍是未解决的实验问题。

Method: 预计算体积调和场，并与受约束的预测规划器耦合；规划器在预测位置上查询调和场，而不是先提取一条全局参考路径。在结构化3D场景和长迷宫场景中测试，并使用Crazyflie进行实机验证。

Result: 结构化3D测试中，调和引导比匹配的Dijkstra引导获得更大的最小间隙和更低的RMS jerk，但路径更长；当两者使用同一通道时该规律仍成立。长迷宫测试的路线远超单个预测时域，Crazyflie试验验证了物理执行。作者称这是首个实体四旋翼体积调和场导航演示。

Conclusion: 全局构造的调和引导可直接支持实体四旋翼上的局部约束运动生成。

Abstract: Quadrotor navigation in cluttered 3-D environments requires global guidance while local motion remains subject to collision and motion limits. Harmonic potentials provide dense guidance from a global boundary value problem, but coupling a volumetric harmonic field to constrained physical quadrotor motion remains an open experimental problem. We couple a precomputed volumetric harmonic field with a constrained predictive planner that queries the field at predicted positions instead of extracting a global reference path. In Structured 3-D tests, harmonic guidance yields larger minimum clearance and lower RMS jerk than matched Dijkstra guidance, at the cost of longer paths; the same pattern remains when both methods use the same passage. Long maze tests span routes far beyond one prediction horizon, and Crazyflie trials validate physical execution. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first physical quadrotor demonstration of volumetric harmonic field navigation. The results show that globally constructed harmonic guidance can directly support local constrained motion generation on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [44] [Tracking the Ground: Online Lidar Identification of Robot-Induced Soil Deformation in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15667)
*Tom Montagnon,Johann Laconte,Benoit Thuilot,Wonjae Cho,Roland Lenain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于激光雷达在线估计车辆引起土壤变形的框架，并用降阶参数模型构建可观测、可解释的土壤状态，为土壤感知机器人奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业面临效率与可持续性挑战，土壤健康是关键；车辆-土壤相互作用会破坏土壤结构并造成地表变形，但现有方法通常未将土壤状态视为变量，难以显式考虑机器人行驶对土壤的影响。

Method: 建立量化车辆通行引起土壤变形的框架；采用降阶参数模型，以物理可解释参数表征土壤行为；基于激光雷达观测在线估计土壤变形演化，持续更新并形成可观测的土壤状态表示。

Result: 在不同土壤条件下的实验表明，该方法能够捕捉机器人引起的土壤变形。

Conclusion: 使土壤响应在作业过程中可测量、可解释，为土壤感知机器人操作提供基础；估计的土壤状态可用于调整机器人行为，以减少土壤退化。

Abstract: Agriculture faces many challenges, and robotic systems can play an important role in addressing them by improving the efficiency and sustainability of field operations. Among these challenges, preserving soil health is a critical concern, as vehicle-soil interactions can degrade the soil structure and produce unwanted surface deformation. A key step toward soil-aware robotics is to explicitly account for how vehicle traffic deforms the ground, yet soil state is typically not treated as a variable. We address this gap by proposing a framework to quantify traffic-induced soil deformation and estimate its evolution online from lidar observations. The method relies on a reduced-order parametric model that represents the soil behavior via physically interpretable parameters, yielding a continuously updated and observable representation of soil state. Experiments conducted in different soil conditions demonstrate the ability of the approach to capture deformation induced by the robot. By making soil response measurable and interpretable during operation, the proposed framework establishes a basis for soil-aware robotic operation, in which the estimated state can be exploited to adapt robotic behaviors in order to reduce soil degradation.

</details>


### [45] [Flow-Matched Motion Priors: Online Optimal-Transport Rewards for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15631)
*Yilin Zou,Chenghua Liu,Chenglong Wu,Fanghua Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Flow-Matched Motion Priors (FMP)，用熵最优传输构建 rollout 到专家运动库的路径，并通过流匹配学习在线标量奖励，在 Unitree G1 上实现比 AMP 和重心 OT 奖励更稳定、跌倒更少的行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMP 的对抗奖励在策略与专家支撑集相距较远时可能失效；朴素 OT 将匹配的专家后继平均为重心目标，会跨步态相位平均并削弱目标关节运动，因此需要能沿路径提供有效信息的运动先验奖励。

Method: FMP 将当前 rollout 历史与专家运动库通过熵最优传输耦合；每次策略更新前，沿 rollout-to-expert 路径用流匹配训练神经势函数，并辅以端点梯度监督和相对价值校准；actor 仅接收物理观测，奖励保持标量。

Result: 受控奖励模型实验中，FMP 比仅价值拟合或仅端点拟合具有更强拟合外泛化。Unitree G1 的 5000 万转移匹配实验显示：示范重置下稳定前行 0.727 m/s，固定默认姿态下 0.338 m/s；固定姿态条件跌倒 129 次，而仅端点对照为 243 次；相较静态 score-gradient 教师，动态 FM 在插值比例 0.25 和 0.50 处降低 score-increment 误差，并减少 29% 离线拟合时间。

Conclusion: FMP 通过路径流匹配与熵 OT 提供标量奖励，缓解 AMP 在支撑集远离时失效以及重心平均削弱运动的问题，提升运动先验泛化、真实机器人行走稳定性和训练效率。

Abstract: Learning a motion prior requires a reward that guides a policy from its current behavior toward demonstrated motion. Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP) provide such a reward with a discriminator. However, adversarial objectives can become uninformative when policy and expert supports are far apart. A naive use of optimal transport (OT) averages matched expert successors into a barycentric target. Averaging across gait phases can weaken the target's joint motion. We introduce Flow-Matched Motion Priors (FMP), an online scalar reward learned from paths connecting current rollout histories to an expert motion bank. Entropic OT supplies the coupling. Before each policy update, we train a neural potential with flow matching (FM) along the rollout-to-expert paths, endpoint-gradient supervision, and relative-value calibration. The actor receives only physical observations and the reward remains a scalar, as in AMP. Controlled reward-model experiments show substantially better generalization beyond the fitting rollout than value-only or endpoint-only fitting. On Unitree G1, matched 50-million-transition experiments compare FMP with AMP, a barycentric OT reward, and nested ablations under demonstration and fixed-pose initialization. FMP produces stable forward walking at 0.727 m/s from demonstration resets and 0.338 m/s from a fixed default pose. In the fixed-pose condition, it incurs 129 falls versus 243 for the endpoint-only control. Against a static score-gradient teacher, dynamic FM reduces score-increment error at interpolation fractions 0.25 and 0.50 while using 29% less offline fitting time.

</details>


### [46] [An Information-Space Perspective to Scene Graph Sufficiency for Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15587)
*Başak Sakçak,Francesco Verdoja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在信息空间框架下形式化基于场景图的规划，定义场景图迁移系统及导航/操作动作语义，并通过信息映射合并与剪枝节点得到派生场景图和商迁移系统，进而给出判定约简场景图对规划是否充分的两个条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境规划需要能表达对象、关系与可供性的任务规格，场景图虽合适，但在大规模环境中图规模过大，阻碍高效规划。已有任务感知剪枝和分层抽象研究，但仍缺乏通用的、以任务为中心的“何为规划充分场景图”的形式化。

Method: 将场景图上的规划建模于信息空间框架中，定义场景图迁移系统以及导航和操作的动作语义；引入信息映射来合并、剪枝节点，并诱导商迁移系统，同时加入运动基元以表示合并节点上的高层动作；用两个条件刻画充分性：(i) 信息映射产生确定性商，(ii) 任务在派生轨迹上适定，从而保证派生模型上找到的规划在最大系统上可行。

Result: 作者在示例环境的一个任务上展示了该框架，并给出了充分与不充分的约简场景图实例。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了任务中心场景图充分性的通用形式化，将高层符号规划与低层运动可行性联系起来，为大规模场景图下的高效规划提供了理论判据。

Abstract: Planning in complex environments requires task specifications grounded in representations that capture objects, relations, and affordances; scene graphs meet this need, but their size in large environments hinders efficient planning. While task-aware pruning and hierarchical abstractions have been explored, a general, task-centric formalization of what constitutes a sufficient scene graph for planning remains open. This paper provides such a formalization by modeling planning over scene graphs within an information-spaces framework through the definition of scene graph transition systems and relevant action semantics for navigation and manipulation. We then introduce derived scene graphs via information mappings that merge and prune nodes and induce quotient transition systems augmented with motion primitives to capture higher-level actions over merged graph nodes. Sufficiency is characterized by two conditions: (i) the information mapping yields a deterministic quotient, and (ii) the task is well-posed over derived traces, ensuring plans found on the derived model are feasible on the maximal system. We illustrate the framework using a task over an example environment, showing both sufficient and insufficient reduced scene graphs.

</details>


### [47] [DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15570)
*Jing Lyu,Shuanghao Bai,Runze Xiao,Zhenyu Liao,Wenxing Tan,Zihan Tang,Ruochuan Shi,Cheng Peng,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Badong Chen,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIDO 通过将多步视频生成模型的收敛动态蒸馏到单步去噪中，并结合以交互为中心的表示引导，在显著降低闭环控制延迟的同时保持机械臂抓取与操控所需的关键交互动态，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 及真实机器人任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 使用视频生成模型预测未来视觉动态以支持机器人操控，但迭代去噪会带来额外延迟，不利于闭环控制。作者发现去噪过程中视觉内容收敛速度不同：静态背景结构较早形成，而夹爪与被操控物体在第一步后仍模糊，其交互动态需后续去噪才显现。因此，简单将多步视频模型截断为一步虽能保留场景结构，却会丢失对操控最关键的交互动态。

Method: 提出 DIDO，将多步视频模型已收敛的动态蒸馏到单步去噪中。方法结合分布匹配蒸馏与以交互为中心的表示引导：一方面把多步生成压缩为一次前向传播；另一方面显式使用有监督的边界框视觉推理 token 建模夹爪、被操控物体及其交互。此外，DIDO 将目标物体在多个模型层中的表示与预训练 DINOv3 编码器特征对齐，以在单步中保留相关实体及其未来动态，同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Result: DIDO 在 LIBERO 上平均成功率为 99.0%，在 LIBERO-Plus 上为 76.6%，在 RoboTwin 上为 92.0%，并展现出向真实机器人操控中长时程任务与泛化任务的有效迁移能力。

Conclusion: DIDO 表明，通过蒸馏多步视频模型的收敛动态并以交互为中心进行表示引导，可在单步去噪中保留操控关键实体与未来动态，从而兼顾低延迟与高性能，并具备真实机器人任务迁移潜力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) use video generation models to predict future visual dynamics for robotic manipulation, but iterative denoising introduces additional latency for closed-loop control. We empirically find that visual content converges at different rates during denoising. Static background structure forms early, whereas the gripper and manipulated object remain blurry after the first step, with their interaction dynamics emerging only through subsequent denoising. Consequently, naively truncating a multi-step video model to one step preserves scene structure but loses the interaction-centric dynamics most critical for manipulation. To address this issue, we propose DIDO, which distills the converged dynamics of a multi-step video model into a single denoising step. DIDO combines distribution matching distillation with interaction-centric representation guidance. Beyond compressing multi-step generation into one forward pass, DIDO explicitly models the gripper, manipulated object, and their interaction using supervised bounding-box visual reasoning tokens. Additionally, DIDO aligns the target object's representations across multiple model layers with features from a pretrained DINOv3 encoder. This interaction-centric guidance helps the distilled model preserve both the relevant entities and their future dynamics in a single step, while substantially reducing inference latency. DIDO achieves an average success rate of 99.0\% on LIBERO, 76.6\% on LIBERO-Plus, and 92.0\% on RoboTwin, while also demonstrating effective transfer to long-horizon and generalization tasks in real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [48] [StereoPatch: Patch-Aligned RGB-Depth Fusion for Spatial Perception in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15509)
*Yanan Zhou,Zhaoyan Qian,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StereoPatch：一种将配准深度几何与 RGB patch 对齐的视觉表征，用于提升视觉运动策略对控制相关几何歧义的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似场景可能因目标位置、物体高度或接触几何变化而需要不同动作；预训练 RGB 特征可能将几何上不同的状态映射为相似策略输入，而简单加入深度又需从有限示范中额外学习 RGB-D 对应关系。

Method: 在共享二维 patch 网格上，通过非对称交叉注意力把深度信息注入对应 RGB 特征，形成 patch 对齐的 StereoPatch Tokens；该几何感知表征可直接条件化通用视觉运动策略，且不改变其原有学习目标。

Result: 在六个真实机器人任务中，StereoPatch 的闭环成功率高于仅外观、仅几何、原始 RGB-D 和晚期融合基线；在三个仿真套件中验证了跨策略架构兼容性、空间泛化与操作限制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，解决控制相关几何歧义的有效方式是将深度直接对齐到用于动作预测的视觉特征，而非将其作为独立模态提供。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot imitation learning have produced visuomotor policies that predict actions directly from visual observations. Yet visually similar scenes can require different actions as target position, object height, or contact geometry changes. Pretrained RGB features may map these geometrically distinct states to similar policy inputs, while simply adding depth requires the policy to learn RGB-depth correspondence from the same limited demonstrations used to learn control. We introduce StereoPatch, a patch-aligned RGB-depth representation that binds registered metric geometry directly to the RGB patches used for action prediction. On a shared 2-D patch grid, asymmetric cross-attention incorporates depth information into the corresponding RGB features before action decoding. The resulting StereoPatch Tokens provide a geometry-aware visual representation that can condition general visuomotor policies without changing their underlying learning objectives. Across six real-robot tasks, StereoPatch achieves higher closed-loop success than appearance-only, geometry-only, raw RGB-D, and late-fusion baselines. Additional experiments across three simulation suites evaluate compatibility across visuomotor policy architectures, spatial generalization, and operating limits. Results suggest that resolving control-relevant geometric ambiguity benefits from aligning depth directly with the visual features used for action prediction, rather than supplying it as an independent modality. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [49] [P-POSEMEM: Projective Semantic Memory for Consistent Language Grounding under Pose-Graph Rewrites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15475)
*Ha Sier,Ali Salmasi,Mengya Xu,Haizhou Zhang,Jie Lu,Zhuo Zou,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 P-POSEMEM：在 SLAM 位姿图优化、回环与压缩过程中，将语义观测作为出生关键帧上的不可变事件，并保留被边缘化关键帧的 Bayes-tree 消元条件，从而在重建的位姿、锚点与身份联合后验上积分语义似然，保持语言目标对应的物理对象命名一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人按语言指令导航时，语义记忆必须在底层 SLAM 位姿图优化、回环和压缩时仍指向同一物理对象。将每次检测提交到世界坐标的地图无法做到：回环会移动测量锚点，或求解器边缘化该锚点，导致查询选中不同对象，尽管两个图表示同一后验。

Method: P-POSEMEM 将每个观测存为其出生关键帧上的不可变事件，保留每个被边缘化关键帧的 Bayes-tree 消元条件，并在重建的位姿、锚点与身份联合后验上积分语义似然。提出 Dproj：推理等价的全图与边缘化图之间语言目标分布的总变差缺陷，直接度量该不一致性。检索由共享冻结检测器固定，以隔离记忆一致性增益。

Result: 在 40 个 HM3DSem 场景、112,000 次查询上，P-POSEMEM 复现全图 oracle（Dproj = 0），并相较所有减记忆基线减少目标翻转。在 8 轮、761 次回环、地图改写达 47 m 的实验中，当消元遵循回环时，Dproj 低于 10^-13 且 0/288 目标翻转；所有消融及插入时冻结坐标均出现目标翻转。在实时有界求解器下，同一记忆翻转 23/288，而冻结坐标为 53/288。预注册负对照被 Dproj 检出，但校准误差与导航成功率不变，表明这些度量捕获不同失效模式。

Conclusion: 语义记忆应保留观测事件与消元条件并基于联合后验推理，而非将检测固化到世界坐标；Dproj 能直接度量回环/边缘化导致的语言目标分布缺陷，将性能增益归因于记忆一致性，并区分记忆失效与校准/导航失效。

Abstract: A robot following language instructions needs its semantic memory to keep naming the same physical object while the SLAM pose graph underneath is optimized, loop-closed and compressed. Maps committing each detection to a world coordinate cannot: a closure moves the anchor it was measured from, or the solver marginalizes that anchor, and the query then selects a different object although both graphs represent the same posterior. P-POSEMEM stores each observation as an immutable event at its birth keyframe, retains the Bayes-tree elimination conditional of every marginalized keyframe, and integrates the semantic likelihood over the reconstructed joint posterior of poses, anchors and identities. Dproj, the total-variation defect between the language-goal distributions of inference-equivalent full and marginalized graphs, measures this directly. Over 40 HM3DSem scenes and 112,000 queries, P-POSEMEM reproduces the full-graph oracle (Dproj = 0) and reduces goal flips against every memory-reducing baseline. On an eight-run campaign whose 761 closures rewrote the map by up to 47 m, Dproj stays below 10^-13 with 0/288 goal flips when elimination follows the closures, where every ablation and a coordinate committed at insertion flip goals it does not; under a live bounded solver the same memory flips 23/288 against 53 for that frozen coordinate. A pre-registered negative control is detected by Dproj while leaving calibration error and navigation success unchanged, indicating that these measures capture distinct failure modes. Retrieval is held fixed by a shared frozen detector, isolating the gain to memory consistency. Code and data: this https URL .

</details>


### [50] [InterSocialBench: Benchmarking Human and LLM Preferences for Companion-Robot Social Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15455)
*Yaodan Xu,Boyang Guo,Yuqing Gu,Qingxin Zhang,Yiwen Deng,Meng Liu,Lintian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 InterSocialBench，一个用于评估伴侣机器人在家庭场景中社交行为选择的基准，强调不同个体偏好差异，避免用单一共识标签替代个人判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴侣机器人日常面临多种可行行为，但不同用户偏好不同回应；现有评估往往追求单一共识，难以反映真实人类分歧。

Method: 构建 210 个家庭场景和 18 种高层行为；收集 100 名人类参与者的判断，以及 7 个大语言模型在 16 种人格条件下产生的 23,520 条回应。每条人类标注保留首选动作，并明确合适与不合适候选。构建流程覆盖行为备选、竞争性情境线索、相关历史与未来任务。评估区分首选选择一致性（preferred-choice agreement）与明确拒绝（explicit rejection），并使用按场景分组的划分训练预测器；同时给出频率基线和人格投票基线。

Result: 在测试提示下，模型与人类的行为分布不同；匹配回应数量后多样性差距仍存在：人类每场景有 4.68 种不同选择，模型为 2.06–3.46 种。人类场景级多数一致率为 51.5%，说明存在分歧，而非存在普适预测上限。

Conclusion: InterSocialBench 支持评估社交行为选择，同时不将个体判断简化为单一共识标签。

Abstract: Companion robots face everyday situations in which several feasible behaviors may be appropriate, yet different people prefer different responses. We introduce InterSocialBench, a benchmark of 210 domestic scenarios and 18 high-level behaviors, pairing judgments from 100 human participants with 23,520 responses from seven large language models under 16 personality conditions. Each human annotation preserves a preferred action alongside explicitly appropriate and inappropriate candidates. A structured construction pipeline covers behavioral alternatives, competing situational cues, and relevant history and future tasks. Evaluation distinguishes preferred-choice agreement from explicit rejection, using scenario-grouped splits for trainable predictors. Simple frequency and persona-voting baselines illustrate these objectives. Across the tested prompts, model and human behavior distributions differ, and the diversity gap remains after matching response counts: humans exhibit 4.68 distinct choices per scenario, compared with 2.06--3.46 for the models. Human scenario-level plurality agreement is 51.5%, describing disagreement rather than a universal prediction ceiling. InterSocialBench supports evaluating social behavior selection without replacing individual judgments with a single consensus label.

</details>


### [51] [Learning to Exploit Passive Dynamics for Energy-Efficient Target Hopping of a Spring-Legged Quadcopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15447)
*Ruigang Chen,Qi Zhang,Zhicheng Zhong,Zhuorui Yun,Yizhar Or,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于PPO的单腿四旋翼跳跃控制策略，直接由估计状态映射到四个电机指令，并通过能量流形塑形与效率塑形奖励，在硬件实验中相较调优PID降低30.7%周期平均电耗和49.8%平均归一化总推力，同时保持可重复的指定高度跳跃与更集中的着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将空中推力与弹簧储能跳跃结合，使单腿四旋翼有望在复杂地形上运动；但启发式PID调参限制了主动推力与被动接触动力学之间的协调，难以兼顾能效与跳跃稳定性。

Method: 提出直接的估计状态到电机指令的PPO策略，控制四个电机，不使用显式跳跃状态机或底层姿态PID。奖励由两部分构成：能量流形塑形，用于质量归一化垂直能量跟踪与顶点状态锚定；效率塑形，使用历史感知功率估计器惩罚总体功率使用、施加额外空中功率代价，并惩罚空中近似静止。

Result: 在代表性硬件运行中，相较调优PID控制栈，基于PPO的控制栈将周期平均实测电功率降低30.7%，平均总归一化推力降低49.8%，同时保持可重复的指定高度跳跃，并实现更集中的着陆。

Conclusion: 结果与以下判断一致：该策略更好地利用了被动动力学，并降低了实测电力需求。

Abstract: Combining aerial thrust with spring-loaded hopping makes monopedal quadcopters promising for locomotion over complex terrain, but heuristic proportional-integral-derivative (PID) tuning limits coordination between active thrust and passive contact dynamics. We present a direct estimated-state-to-motor Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy that commands four motors without an explicit hopping state machine or low-level attitude PID. Its reward combines Energy-Manifold Shaping for mass-normalized vertical-energy tracking and apex-state anchoring with Efficiency Shaping, which uses a history-aware power estimator to penalize general power use, impose an additional airborne-power cost, and penalize airborne near-stationarity. In representative hardware runs, the PPO-based control stack reduced cycle-averaged measured electrical power by 30.7% and mean total normalized thrust by 49.8% relative to the tuned PID-based control stack, while retaining repeatable commanded-height hopping and more concentrated landings. These observations are consistent with improved use of passive dynamics and reduced measured electrical demand.

</details>


### [52] [Dynamics-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Agile and Energy-Efficient Locomotion of a Monopedal Hopping Quadcopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15399)
*Ruigang Chen,Qi Zhang,Zhicheng Zhong,Zhuorui Yun,Yizhar Or,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动力学感知强化学习框架，用于单腿跳跃四旋翼，通过能量约束与相位奖励实现高效稳定高速跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中腿式机器人兼具敏捷与能效，但复杂混合动力学下高速跳跃控制困难；强化学习易出现能量低效的“奖励黑客”问题。

Method: 将目标比能嵌入奖励，约束优化至物理可行的能量流形；奖励相位一致行为以鼓励仿生支撑相冲量；惩罚机电功率浪费以促使电机高效输出；无需启发式状态机即可在弹簧回弹期注入能量。

Result: MuJoCo仿真实现鲁棒高度调节和最高2.0 m/s前向速度跟踪，克服严重姿态-接触耦合；生成敏捷跳跃步态，能耗较悬停基线和低效基线分别降低82%和73%。

Conclusion: 动力学感知的奖励设计可避免奖励黑客，实现高效、稳定、高速的跳跃控制，提升空中腿式机器人的能效与敏捷性。

Abstract: Although aerial-legged robots offer combined agility and efficiency, controlling high-speed hopping under complex hybrid dynamics is challenging. Reinforcement Learning (RL) is promising but prone to energy-inefficient "reward hacking". We propose a Dynamics-Informed RL framework for a monopedal hopping quadcopter. By embedding a target Specific Energy into the reward, we constrain the optimization to a physically viable energy manifold, ensuring stable hopping behaviour. By rewarding the phase-consistent behavior, it can encourage bio-inspired stance-phase impulse. Furthermore, penalizing the electro-mechanical power waste induces the motors generate an efficient impulse. This enables the policy to inject energy strictly during spring restitution without heuristic state machines. MuJoCo simulations validate robust height regulation and forward velocity tracking up to 2.0 m/s despite severe attitude-contact coupling. Ultimately, our approach yields a highly agile hopping gait, reducing energy consumption by 82% and 73% compared to hovering baselines and inefficiency baseline, respectively.

</details>


### [53] [From Prediction to Decision: World-Model-Guided Action Selection for Continuous Pile Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15382)
*Ailing Zhang,Fan Gao,Song Zhang,Kawa Leong,Ziyu Wu,Yafei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出World-Action Model（WAM）用于轮式装载机自主挖掘：在闭环中生成、筛选并排序铲掘候选，联合预测地形变化与装载量，从而减少铲掘次数并提升全尺寸实机可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式装载机挖掘是顺序决策问题，每次铲掘都会改变后续可用地形；实际可用的世界模型必须准确预测动作后果、实时对候选动作排序，并能嵌入全尺寸机器的感知-决策-执行闭环。

Method: WAM先生成多个铲掘候选，剔除几何不可行候选，联合预测带符号地形变化和装载体积，执行预测装载量最大的候选，并从新观测地形重新规划；系统集成感知-提议-预测-选择-执行闭环，并采用ROS2/TensorRT部署。

Result: 在32个几何不相交的MinSlope测试回合中，将世界模型排序加入匹配的扩散提议后，平均铲掘次数从651.8降至540.6（降低17.1%），完成率保持32/32，且每个配对回合均有改善；完整系统对比中WAM完成32/32，独立训练的soft actor-critic策略完成29/32。输入表示、空间支持和五种架构的对比识别出准确且高效的物理结构预测器；在事件不相交的全尺寸装载机数据上评估接口，并部署完整闭环。ROS2/TensorRT实现在Jetson AGX Orin上处理5个候选耗时72.4 ms。

Conclusion: 仿真结果确立了决策层面的收益，物理实验证明该方法在真实世界闭环自主挖掘中的可行性。

Abstract: Wheel-loader excavation is a sequential decision problem in which every scoop changes the terrain available to subsequent actions. A practical world model must predict action consequences accurately, rank candidates in real time, and operate inside the closed loop of a full-size machine. We present the World-Action Model (WAM), which proposes multiple scoops, rejects geometrically inadmissible candidates, jointly predicts signed terrain change and loaded volume, executes the candidate with the largest predicted load, and replans from the newly observed terrain. On 32 geometry-disjoint MinSlope test episodes, adding world-model ranking to matched diffusion proposals reduces the mean scoop count from 651.8 to 540.6 (17.1%), preserves 32/32 completion, and improves every paired episode. In a complete-system comparison, WAM completes 32/32 episodes versus 29/32 for an independently trained soft actor-critic policy. Comparisons of input representations, spatial support, and five architectures identify an accurate and efficient physics-structured predictor. We further evaluate the interface on event-disjoint full-size-loader data and deploy the complete perception-proposal-prediction-selection-execution loop for autonomous excavation. The ROS2/TensorRT implementation processes five candidates in 72.4 ms on a Jetson AGX Orin. The simulation results establish decision-level gains, while the physical experiments demonstrate real-world closed-loop feasibility.

</details>


### [54] [Understanding User Preferences of a Slope-Aware Variable-Admittance Filter for a Robot Guide Dog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15362)
*Federico Esposito,Mario Selvaggio,Aaron Link,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究坡度感知可变导纳滤波器的刚度与阻尼参数对机器人导盲犬力交互中用户偏好的影响，发现上坡增刚度、下坡减刚度可改善主客观指标，而阻尼无显著主效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为视障人士开发基于四足机器人的导盲系统，需优化物理引导中的力交互体验；坡度自适应导纳参数如何影响用户偏好尚不明确。

Method: 系统由四足机器人、无传感器刚性手柄组成，结合路径跟随、基于动量的交互力旋量估计和可变导纳滤波器；滤波器刚度与阻尼根据深度相机提取的坡度信息在线调整。通过高保真仿真和蒙眼视力正常受试者实验，采用田口L9设计比较多种适应策略。

Result: 初步主效应结果显示：上坡增加刚度、下坡降低刚度可改善客观与主观指标；阻尼对指标无显著主效应。

Conclusion: 坡度自适应刚度策略有助于提升导盲机器人力交互的用户体验，阻尼参数影响不显著；该发现可为视障导盲系统的交互控制设计提供初步依据。

Abstract: This letter investigates how the parameters of a slope-aware variable-admittance filter influence user preferences in force-based interaction with a robotic guide dog for visually impaired individuals. The proposed system consists of a quadruped robot equipped with a sensor-free rigid handle for physical guidance. The framework combines path following, momentum-based interaction-wrench estimation, and a variable-admittance filter whose stiffness and damping are adapted online from slope information extracted by the robot's depth camera. The adaptation policies are evaluated through high-fidelity simulations and a human-subject study involving blindfolded sighted participants. Multiple strategies are compared using a Taguchi L9 design of experiments. Preliminary main-effect results suggest that increasing stiffness uphill and decreasing it downhill improves both objective and subjective metrics, whereas damping shows no significant main effect.

</details>


### [55] [Assistance Torque Estimation via Dynamics-Aware Optimization for Lower-Limb Exoskeleton in Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15352)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动力学模型的辅助力矩估计方法，并从优化角度直接生成电机辅助力矩，再训练数据驱动预测网络，实现复杂户外环境下的实时预测，为低成本自适应外骨骼辅助提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人体关节力矩估计依赖动作捕捉系统，存在户外可用性受限和部署成本高的问题；同时，将真实关节力矩直接缩放为电机力矩命令未必最优。受人体运动生成过程启发，需降低数据采集成本并直接生成更合适的电机辅助力矩。

Method: 基于动态模型，从优化角度直接生成电机辅助力矩，以降低数据采集成本；随后训练数据驱动的辅助力矩预测网络，使其能够在复杂户外环境中进行准确、实时的辅助力矩预测。

Result: 实验表明，优化（估计）后的辅助力矩与步态轨迹具有更好的相位一致性，并与任务特征更对齐。相较于零力矩条件，预测力矩可使代谢率降低11.8%-17.7%，心率降低8.9%-14.3%，峰值肌肉激活水平降低28.2%-54.0%。

Conclusion: 该方法无需依赖高成本动作捕捉和真实关节力矩直接缩放，能够实现有效的辅助力矩估计与实时预测，为低成本自适应外骨骼辅助提供了新视角。

Abstract: Ground-truth human joint torque estimation relies on motion capture systems, which suffer from limited outdoor usability and significant deployment expenses. Furthermore, direct scaling of ground-truth joint torques to obtain motor torque commands is not necessarily the optimal strategy. To address the aforementioned limitations, inspired by the human motion generation process, this paper proposes a novel assistance torque estimation method based on the dynamic model. From an optimization perspective, the proposed method directly generates motor-assist torque and lowers the cost of data acquisition. Then, a data-driven assistance torque prediction network is trained to enable accurate real-time prediction under complex outdoor environments. Experimental results demonstrate that optimized (estimated) assistance torque exhibits better phase consistency with gait trajectories and better alignment with task characteristics. Relative to the Zero torque condition, the predicted torque can decrease metabolic rate by 11.8%-17.7%, heart rate by 8.9%-14.3%, and peak muscle activation levels by 28.2%-54.0%, respectively. This provides a new perspective for low-cost adaptive exoskeleton assistance.

</details>


### [56] [Planning in the Backbone: DiffAdapterVLA for Native Continuous Trajectory Generation with Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15322)
*Changxin Lu,Xiaoliang Meng,Yu Wu,Rui Huang,Honglin Li,Tao Chen,Kaixuan Zhou,Yadong Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffAdapterVLA 将轨迹 token 注入 VLM 后层，在骨干网络前向计算中实现规划，通过轻量级逐层 DiffAdapter 与不对称联合注意力递归细化轨迹，在 NAVSIM 上以少量可训练参数实现低延迟闭环规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶视觉语言模型虽能融合视觉、路线、语言与驾驶上下文形成丰富先验，但其表示目标与连续驾驶规划脱节；通常只在 VLM 形成最终条件后才生成轨迹，使深度方向的条件计算未参与轨迹状态的逐步构建。

Method: 提出 DiffAdapterVLA，即 Planning in the Backbone：将显式轨迹 token 注入选定 VLM 后层，使轨迹状态进入骨干前向计算，并在不同深度与驾驶条件共同演化；用轻量级逐层 DiffAdapter 将计算组织为递归轨迹细化，并用不对称联合注意力保持条件流对轨迹规划的有向引导；仅适配轻量轨迹模块，而非独立轨迹规划器。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，以少量可训练参数实现高质量闭环规划，且端到端延迟低；验证了在 VLM 后层计算中联合演化轨迹状态与深度驾驶条件能够有效实现连续轨迹规划。

Conclusion: 将规划嵌入 VLM 骨干计算，而非依赖独立规划器，可高效地把现有驾驶先验转化为连续规划能力。

Abstract: Pretrained driving vision-language models (VLMs) integrate visual, route, language, and driving context into rich driving priors, yet their representation objectives remain separated from continuous driving planning. Existing methods typically begin trajectory generation only after the VLM has formed a final condition, leaving depth-wise condition computation outside the stepwise formation of trajectory state. We introduce DiffAdapterVLA, which realizes Planning in the Backbone: it injects explicit trajectory tokens into selected VLM late layers, bringing trajectory state into backbone forward computation, where it co-evolves with driving conditions at different depths. Lightweight layer-wise DiffAdapters organize this computation into recursive trajectory refinement, while asymmetric joint attention preserves directed guidance from the condition stream to trajectory planning. By placing planning within existing backbone computation rather than relying on an independent trajectory planner, DiffAdapterVLA adapts only lightweight trajectory modules to turn existing driving priors into efficient continuous planning capability. NAVSIM results show that it achieves high-quality closed-loop planning with low end-to-end latency using few trainable parameters, and demonstrate that jointly evolving trajectory state and depth-wise driving conditions in VLM late-layer computation effectively realizes continuous trajectory planning.

</details>


### [57] [Low Clearance Hinge Joint Mechanism Based on 3D Printing on Sheet Fabrication Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15276)
*Jaehyung Jang,Euibin Shin,Allison M. Okamura,Jee-Hwan Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于片材3D打印制造的低间隙铰链关节机构，省去装配胶粘剂，适用于几十厘米尺度机器人；比较三种铰链方案并选用低间隙设计，实现0.1 mm间隙，实验评估扭转阻力，最终应用于3自由度Delta机械臂并实现精确低间隙运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统折纸/铰链机构制造与装配常依赖胶粘剂，工艺复杂且难以保证低间隙，限制了其在几十厘米尺度机器人中的应用；因此需要一种可简化制造、无需粘合剂并具备低间隙的铰链关节机制。

Method: 采用sheet 3D打印/片材3D打印方法一体化制造低间隙铰链关节；比较三类铰链关节机构的优缺点，选择可针对不同面片厚度设计低间隙的铰链形式；分析其扭转角与弯曲力，实现0.1 mm间隙；通过实验评估由塑性变形和间隙引起的扭转阻力及所需扭矩。

Result: 实现了0.1 mm的铰链间隙；实验表明，塑性变形产生的扭矩足以约束铰链关节的非期望自由度，而由间隙引起的扭转所需扭矩很小；将该铰链关节应用于3自由度Delta机械臂，验证了低间隙下的精确运动。

Conclusion: 该3D打印片材低间隙铰链机构能够简化铰链制造、消除装配胶粘剂需求，并提供足以约束非期望自由度的扭转阻力，适用于几十厘米尺度机器人；在3自由度Delta机械臂上的应用证明了其低间隙与精确运动能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-clearance hinge joint mechanism based on the 3D printing on sheet fabrication method. This approach simplifies the fabrication of hinge mechanisms and overcomes limitations of conventional origami manufacturing by eliminating the need for adhesives commonly used during assembly, making it suitable for robots at the tens-of-centimeters scale. The advantages and disadvantages of three types of hinge joint mechanisms are compared, and a hinge joint that can be designed with low clearance for various facet thicknesses is selected. Based on the selected hinge joint, the twisting angle and bending force are analyzed, leading to the implementation of a clearance of 0.1 mm. Torsional resistance is experimentally evaluated to measure the torque required for twisting caused by plastic deformation and clearance. The results show that the torque associated with plastic deformation is sufficient to constrain the undesired degrees of freedom of the hinge joint, while the torque required for twisting due to clearance is minimal. Based on the analyzed data, the proposed hinge joint mechanism is applied to a 3-degree-of-freedom delta robot manipulator, demonstrating precise motion with low clearance.

</details>


### [58] [Distributed Safe Cooperative Vector Field for Trajectory Curvature Constrained Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15266)
*Zhouru Xiao,Tao Teng,Weijia Yao,Hui Zhang,Xiangke Wang,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受轨迹曲率约束的多机器人分布式安全协同向量场方法，通过自适应可调反应边界实现安全避碰，仅需邻居单个虚拟变量，并经仿真与实机实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际多机器人系统因机器人转向能力有限而存在轨迹曲率约束；若不加以考虑，机器人可能无法完成任务，且轨迹会偏离预期路径。

Method: 提出分布式安全协同向量场方法，由协同向量场和安全导向避碰向量场组成；设计具有自适应可调反应边界的避碰向量场，以适应机器人运动学曲率约束并保证避碰机动的物理可行性；该方法仅需每个邻居机器人的单个虚拟变量即可实现协同运动、避障和机器人间避碰。

Result: 通过仿真和真实多机器人平台实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在轨迹曲率约束下有效实现多机器人协同路径跟随与安全避碰，并降低协同所需的信息交互需求。

Abstract: Trajectory curvature constraints are inherent in practical multi-robot systems due to the limited turning capabilities of the robots. Without properly accounting for these constraints, robots may fail to accomplish assigned tasks, and their trajectories may diverge from the intended paths. This paper proposes a distributed safe cooperative vector field approach for multi-robot systems subject to trajectory curvature constraints. The proposed approach is composed of a cooperative vector field and a safety-oriented collision avoidance vector field, aiming to address the problems of cooperative motion and safe collision avoidance in multi-robot path-following tasks. A safety-oriented collision avoidance vector field with adaptively adjustable reactive boundary is developed to accommodate the kinematic curvature constraints of robots, thereby ensuring the physical feasibility of collision avoidance maneuvers. The proposed vector field requires only a single virtual variable from each neighboring robot to achieve cooperative motion and ensure both obstacle avoidance and inter-robot collision avoidance. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is validated through both simulations and real-world experiments on an actual multi-robot platform.

</details>


### [59] [A Vision Based Framework Integrating Attention and Action Cues for Interpretable Cognitive Workload Assessment in Human Robot Collaborative Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15232)
*Junyan Xionga,Naiyi Feng,Xingke Xia,Qihang Fan,Suchang Chen,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉的注意力-动作框架，用于人机协作装配中连续且可解释的工作负荷相关评估；该框架融合RGB-D、机器人状态与任务区域，构建操作者行为时序表征，并在齿轮箱装配实验中验证其与主观及生理指标关联，且无需穿戴传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作装配中，操作者需协调手动任务与实时任务信息及机器人行为，任务需求动态波动；传统工作负荷评估依赖穿戴式生理传感器，部署不便，因此需要非接触、连续且可解释的评估方法。

Method: 框架融合RGB-D观测、机器人状态和标定的任务相关区域，构建经时间确认的操作者行为表征，识别任务需求集中位置，并解释注意力与动作分离、任务情境变化或操作者犹豫时需求如何发展。在三级协作齿轮箱装配实验中，10名参与者参与，以主观评分和同步生理信号作为独立参考。

Result: 原始NASA-TLX评分证实各条件主观负荷递增，整体、心理和时间维度显著；视觉推导的HRC-CWL输出在9名具有完整相关数据的参与者中有7名与ECG特征显著相关；同步交互片段显示检测到的犹豫与生理活动存在时间对应；实时部署无需操作者额外佩戴传感器。

Conclusion: HRC-CWL可作为认知工效分析与自适应机器人辅助的可解释行为代理，而非工作负荷的直接心理生理测量。

Abstract: The introduction of human-robot collaboration (HRC) in industrial assembly operations is revolutionizing the manufacturing landscape. In this evolving environment, operators are required to seamlessly coordinate their manual tasks with real-time task information and robotic behaviors. These demands fluctuate during operation, yet conventional workload assessments depend on body-worn physiological sensors that complicate practical deployment. Here, we present a vision-based attention--action framework for continuous and interpretable workload-related assessment in HRC assembly. The framework combines RGB-D observations with robot states and calibrated task-related areas to construct a temporally confirmed representation of operator behavior. This representation identifies where task demand is concentrated and explains how it develops when attention and action diverge, the task context changes, or the operator hesitates. We evaluated the framework in a three-level collaborative gearbox assembly experiment with ten participants, using subjective ratings and synchronized physiological signals as independent references. Raw NASA-TLX ratings confirmed increasing perceived workload across conditions, with significant effects on overall workload and its mental and temporal dimensions. The vision-derived HRC-CWL output was significantly associated with ECG-derived features in seven of nine participants with complete correlation data. Synchronized interaction episodes further showed temporal correspondence between detected hesitation and physiological activity. Real-time deployment demonstrated that the framework can operate without requiring operators to wear additional sensors. These findings support HRC-CWL as an interpretable behavioral proxy for cognitive ergonomics analysis and adaptive robot assistance, rather than a direct psychophysiological measure of workload.

</details>


### [60] [X-WBC: A Cross-Embodiment Foundation Model for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15213)
*Juntong Zhang,Chun Gu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 X-WBC，一个跨具身全身控制基础框架，通过分离共享人类运动语义与具身特定执行，实现多机器人联合训练，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证其跟踪、跨命令源一致性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人形机器人全身控制扩展到通用部署，需要大规模人类运动语料和跨机器人本体共享的训练经验；现有方法通常为每个机器人单独训练策略，导致运动经验在不同具身之间相互孤立。

Method: X-WBC 将相对共享的人类运动语义与具身特定物理执行分离。以人为中心的命令 token 对齐完整人类运动、机器人参考运动和稀疏 VR 观测；因果 Transformer 从混合多机器人 rollout 中学习可复用时间结构；轻量机器人特定模块将共享表征映射到各机器人的本体感觉与动作空间。

Result: 在九种仿真具身、外部运动和四台真实机器人上，实验表明联合训练提升跟踪性能，对齐表征支持跨命令源的一致控制，且学习策略在训练语料之外仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将异构人形机器人作为联合数据源，并表明跨具身联合训练是通向全身控制基础模型的实用路径。

Abstract: Scaling humanoid whole-body control toward general-purpose deployment requires large human motion corpora and training experience shared across robot bodies. Existing methods usually train one policy per robot, leaving motion experience isolated across embodiments. We introduce X-WBC, a cross-embodiment foundation framework that separates relatively shared human motion semantics from embodiment-specific physical execution. Human-centered command tokens align full human motion, robot reference motion, and sparse VR observations. A causal Transformer learns reusable temporal structure from mixed multi-robot rollouts, while lightweight robot-specific modules map the shared representation to each robot's proprioception and action space. Across nine simulated embodiments, external motions, and four real robots, experiments show that joint training improves tracking, the aligned representation supports consistent control across command sources, and the learned policy remains competitive beyond the training corpus. These results support heterogeneous humanoids as joint data sources and establish cross-embodiment joint training as a practical route toward whole-body control foundation models.

</details>


### [61] [PredTac: Learning Contact-Rich Manipulation with Predicted Touch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15198)
*Weijia Fan,Daqiang Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PredTac：从因果视觉观察和机器人状态预测触觉状态，并将预测触觉作为策略学习与执行的显式接口，从而在无需物理触觉传感器输入的情况下提升接触丰富操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作受益于触觉反馈，但物理触觉传感器带来硬件、标定、同步与维护成本，导致策略学习和部署复杂。因此作者探索用预测触觉替代实测触觉输入。

Method: PredTac 先以触觉监督训练触觉预测器，再从因果视觉观察和机器人状态推断触觉状态；随后将预测触觉作为策略学习与执行时的显式接口，在下游策略训练和执行中均不需要实测触觉输入。在仿真和真机上评估三个接触丰富操作任务，并分析策略性能对预测接触内容的依赖。

Result: 仿真目标偏移评估中，预测触觉策略在 USB、Barbed-spike、Valve 上分别达到 27.0%、52.0%、44.7% 成功率，较视觉基线提升 8.0-13.7 个百分点。真机上预测触觉 ACT 在 USB 插入、Barbed 拔出、Valve 旋转上分别达到 70.0%、50.0%、90.0%，三任务均值 70.0%，接近实测触觉 ACT 的 72.2%，显著优于视觉 ACT 的 21.1%。固定策略干预显示性能对预测接触的空间结构敏感：在固定数值分布下重排空间结构使 Valve 成功率下降 10.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预测触觉无需作为策略输入的触觉传感即可为接触丰富操作提供有用接触信息，是替代实测触觉的可行方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation benefits from tactile feedback, yet physical tactile sensors introduce hardware, calibration, synchronization, and maintenance costs that complicate policy learning and deployment. We formulate predicted touch as an alternative to measured tactile input and present PredTac, a framework that learns to infer tactile states from causal visual observations and robot states and uses the predicted touch as an explicit interface for policy learning and execution. A tactile predictor is first trained with tactile supervision and then used to provide contact information without requiring measured tactile input during downstream policy training or execution. We evaluate PredTac across three contact-rich manipulation tasks in simulation and on a real robot, and further examine how policy performance depends on the predicted contact content. In simulation goal-offset evaluations, predicted-touch policies achieve 27.0%, 52.0%, and 44.7% success on USB, Barbed-spike, and Valve, respectively, improving over the visual baseline by 8.0-13.7 percentage points. On the real robot, predicted-touch ACT achieves 70.0%, 50.0%, and 90.0% success on USB insertion, Barbed extraction, and Valve rotation, respectively, with a three-task mean of 70.0%, approaching measured-touch ACT at 72.2% and substantially outperforming visual ACT at 21.1%. Fixed-policy interventions further show that performance is sensitive to the spatial structure of predicted contact, with spatial rearrangement at fixed value distributions reducing Valve success by 10.7 percentage points. These results demonstrate that predicted touch can provide useful contact information for contact-rich manipulation without requiring tactile sensing as a policy input.

</details>


### [62] [HarnessVLN: Unifying Training-Free Embodied Navigation through an Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15195)
*Yang Chen,Lirong Che,Zhenyu Huang,Wenbo Fu,Chuang Wang,Xu Cao,Daqi Liu,Yuzhe Yang,Jian Su,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出零样本、免训练具身导航框架 HarnessVLN，通过 Agent Harness 统一工具接口协调感知、检索、定位、导航、恢复与终止，在多个基准上超越免训练 SOTA，并完成人形机器人真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身导航要求智能体理解视觉观测、积累空间知识并执行动作以遵循指令或定位目标。训练型方法面临泛化挑战，而免训练方法虽利用多模态大语言模型，却常缺乏机制来协调动作提议与空间证据、任务进度及执行失败。

Method: HarnessVLN 是零样本、免训练框架。其 Agent Harness 通过统一工具接口协调感知、检索、定位、导航、恢复和终止；依据空间证据、几何可行性与子目标一致性验证规划器提议，并将结构化工具反馈纳入后续决策。分层事件记忆跟踪任务进度与执行历史；持久时空图维护可复用空间证据和失败标注，用于验证与恢复。可替换导航执行器将验证后的目标转换为可执行运动，使同一 Harness 协议支持指令跟随与物体目标导航。

Result: 在 R2R、RxR、HM3D-v2 和 HM3D-OVON 上成功率分别达到 60.8%、53.9%、76.0% 和 59.3%，超越此前免训练 SOTA。人形机器人部署进一步证明其在真实环境中对两类任务的适用性。

Conclusion: HarnessVLN 通过统一工具化 Harness、结构化空间记忆与事件记忆，提升了免训练具身导航的可靠性、可恢复性与跨任务通用性，并展现出真实世界部署潜力。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to interpret visual observations, accumulate spatial knowledge, and execute actions to follow instructions or locate objects. Training-based methods face generalization challenges, while training-free methods exploit multimodal large language models (MLLMs) but often lack mechanisms to reconcile proposed actions with spatial evidence, task progress, and execution failures. We present HarnessVLN, a zero-shot, training-free framework whose Agent Harness coordinates perception, retrieval, grounding, navigation, recovery, and termination through a unified tool interface. The Harness validates planner proposals against spatial evidence, geometric feasibility, and subgoal consistency, incorporating structured tool feedback into subsequent decisions. Hierarchical event memory tracks task progress and execution history, while a persistent Spatiotemporal Graph maintains reusable spatial evidence and failure annotations for verification and recovery. A replaceable Navigation Executor converts validated targets into executable motions, allowing the same Harness protocol to support instruction-following and object-goal navigation. HarnessVLN achieves success rates of 60.8%, 53.9%, 76.0%, and 59.3% on R2R, RxR, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, respectively, surpassing prior training-free SOTA results. Humanoid deployment further demonstrates its applicability to both tasks in real-world environments. The project page is: this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [LieSpline-DP: Lie-Group B-Spline Diffusion Policy for Smooth Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15162)
*Erxuan Xie,Bang Liu,Pingyun Nie,Xingkai Liu,Zhuang Fu,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LieSpline-DP 是一种李群 B 样条扩散策略，直接在 SE(3) 上生成末端执行器轨迹，并通过共享相邻规划块的边界控制位姿来保证跨块 C² 连续性，从而降低轨迹抖动并提升机器人操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略（Diffusion Policy, DP）虽是强大的机器人操作模仿学习方法，但会产生不连续、不平滑的轨迹。现有基于样条的动作表示虽能在单个动作块内提升平滑性，却无法保证跨块 C² 连续性，也未考虑 SE(3) 的群结构。

Method: 提出 LieSpline-DP：一种李群 B 样条扩散策略，直接在 SE(3) 上生成末端执行器轨迹，并通过让连续规划共享边界控制位姿来耦合相邻动作块，从而保证整个规划轨迹全程具有 C² 连续性。

Result: 在三个真实机器人任务中，LieSpline-DP 相比 DP 基线产生更低的轨迹抖动并取得更高的任务成功率。在涉及液体和柔性物体的真实任务中提升尤为显著：真实机器人实验中，LieSpline-DP 在倒水和挂钩水桶任务上均达到 100% 成功率，而 DP 基线分别仅为 10% 和 30%。

Conclusion: LieSpline-DP 通过李群 B 样条与跨块边界控制位姿共享，有效实现 SE(3) 上平滑且 C² 连续的轨迹生成，在真实机器人操作任务中优于 DP 基线，尤其适用于液体和柔性物体等挑战性场景。

Abstract: Diffusion Policy (DP) is a powerful Learning from Demonstration (LfD) method for robotic manipulation, yet it suffers from discontinuous and non-smooth trajectories. Spline-based action representations promote smooth motion within individual action chunks, but existing spline-based methods neither guarantee cross-chunk $C^2$ continuity nor account for the group structure of $\mathrm{SE}(3)$. We therefore propose LieSpline-DP, a Lie-group B-spline diffusion policy that generates end-effector trajectories directly on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ and couples consecutive plans by sharing their boundary control poses, ensuring $C^2$ continuity throughout the entire planned trajectory. Across three real-robot tasks, LieSpline-DP produces lower trajectory jerk and higher task success rates than the DP baseline. The gains are particularly pronounced in real-world tasks involving liquids and flexible objects: in our real-robot experiments, LieSpline-DP achieved a 100% success rate on both pouring and bucket hooking, whereas the DP baseline achieved only 10% and 30%, respectively.

</details>


### [64] [C$^2$Nav: Compare Before You Commit for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15142)
*Runtian Zheng,Congpeng Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出无训练框架 C2Nav：让 VLM 仅比较控制器构造的候选方案，而将几何、阈值、动作幅度与执行留在物理侧，从而在零样本 VLN-CE 中提升导航成功率，并表明受限决策接口与更强 VLM 推理能力互补而非可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本连续环境视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）常将基础视觉语言模型（VLM）放入导航环路，但要求其输出路点、像素、朝向、进度值或绝对到达决策等基数式/绝对式结果，这会把生成式回答耦合到几何量或不可逆承诺上。作者研究一种互补的模型-机器人接口：VLM 只比较控制器构造的替代方案，几何、阈值、动作幅度和执行仍由物理侧负责。

Method: 提出 C2Nav，一个无训练框架，包含三个协同模块：Seeing 对经过物理验证的候选视图执行序数 Gaze Election；Remembering 维护紧凑路线草图并比较相邻指令段假设；Arriving 结合犹豫阶梯、回看比较和可撤销回退以实现可靠停止。

Result: 在公开 OpenNav R2R-CE 100 协议上，C2Nav 使用 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 获得 41.0% OSR、31.0% SR、16.7% SPL；同一接口使用标准 GPT-5.5 模型达到 54.0% OSR、44.0% SR、29.0% SPL。整模块消融中，去掉 Seeing 后 SR 降至 14.0%，去掉 Remembering 后降至 25.0%，去掉 Arriving 后降至 29.0%。匹配的角色反转实验仅将比较式回答替换为基数式/绝对式问题，在空间、过渡和终止槽位上分别使 SR 降至 12.0%、28.0%、21.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，受限决策接口与更强 VLM 推理能力是互补关系，而非可相互替代；将 VLM 限定为比较器、把几何与执行留在物理侧，有助于提升零样本 VLN-CE 性能与可靠性。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation in continuous environments (VLN-CE) increasingly places foundation vision-language models (VLMs) inside the navigation loop. Existing systems commonly request cardinal outputs such as waypoints, pixels, headings, progress values, or absolute arrival decisions, coupling a generative response to geometric magnitude or an irreversible commitment. We study a complementary model-robot interface: the VLM compares controller-constructed alternatives, while geometry, thresholds, action magnitude, and execution remain on the physical side. We instantiate this idea in C2Nav, a training-free framework with three coordinated faculties. Seeing performs ordinal Gaze Election over physically vetted candidate views; Remembering maintains a compact route sketch and compares adjacent instruction-leg hypotheses; and Arriving combines a hesitation ladder, look-back comparison, and revocable walk-back for reliable stopping. On the public OpenNav R2R-CE 100 protocol, C2Nav with Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct obtains 41.0% OSR, 31.0% SR, and 16.7% SPL, while the same interface with the standard GPT-5.5 model reaches 54.0% OSR, 44.0% SR, and 29.0% SPL. Whole-faculty ablations reduce SR to 14.0% without Seeing, 25.0% without Remembering, and 29.0% without Arriving. Matched role inversions that replace only the comparative answer form with cardinal/absolute questions reduce SR to 12.0%, 28.0%, and 21.0% in the spatial, transition, and terminal slots, respectively. The results indicate that a constrained decision interface and stronger VLM reasoning are complementary rather than interchangeable.

</details>


### [65] [Legislating World-Model-Based Planning with Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15113)
*Dylan Waldner,Yiannis Kantaros,Guido Governatori,Risto Miikkulainen,Amir Banifatemi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究机器人法律规范的落地问题，提出并测量法律文本与运行时编码之间的两类同构鸿沟，并构建基于可废止道义逻辑（DDL）的法律规划栈，在仿真机械臂推方块任务中验证事前立法治理的有效性、效率、可审计性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人系统日趋通用，需要法律规范将其整合进社会。核心挑战在于：法律源文本与其编码之间的对齐存在同构问题，具体表现为感知错误导致法律推理输入原子事实错误（grounding isomorphism gap），以及同一法律结论可被多种忠实方式翻译为规划约束（ontological isomorphism gap）。

Method: 扩展法律源文本与其编码对齐的同构问题，并提出一个法律规划栈：使用可废止道义逻辑（DDL）约束运动规划器，利用学习到的世界模型进行规划并提供法律上下文，从而实现事前治理，在非法行动执行前进行干预。系统部署于仿真机械臂在3×3网格中推动方块的场景。

Result: （1）受法律约束的智能体比未受约束者显著更常守法，建模感知不确定性后守法率进一步提升；（2）法律推理在运行时高效，裁决可审计；（3）该栈能适应外生信号与内生规则变化；（4）世界模型与探针误差会污染DDL推理器的事实输入；（5）同一条法律可被多种忠实的度量解释所接受，导致守法率差异巨大。

Conclusion: 事前立法按预期发挥作用。通过标准化法律到运行时约束的映射，并借助感知改进事实 grounding，可缩小上述两类同构鸿沟，从而获得稳健法律，使机器人行为与社会规范对齐。

Abstract: As robotic systems grow more general, legal norms are needed to integrate them into society. This paper extends the isomorphism problem of aligning legal source texts with their encodings, and measures two key challenges to robot normative control: (1) the \textit{grounding isomorphism gap}, where perception error grounds false atoms for legal reasoning, and (2) the \textit{ontological isomorphism gap}, where one legal conclusion admits many faithful translations into planning constraints. The paper introduces a legal planning stack that employs Defeasible Deontic Logic (DDL) to constrain a motion planner. The stack leverages learned world models to plan and to provide legal context, enabling \textit{ex ante} governance that intervenes before an illegal action is executed. It was deployed on a simulated robot arm pushing a cube across a $3\times3$ grid. The findings were (1) the legislated agent abided substantially more often than the non-legislated one, and modeling perception uncertainty lifted abidance even further, (2) the legal reasoning ran efficiently at runtime and its verdicts were auditable, and (3) the stack adapted to exogenous signals and endogenous rule changes. Both gaps were measured: (4) world model and probe error corrupted the factual input for the DDL reasoner, and (5) a single law admitted several faithful metric interpretations yielding drastically different abidance. Thus, \textit{ex ante} legislation functions as intended, and closing these gaps with a standardized mapping from the law to runtime constraints and improved fact grounding from perception will yield robust laws that align robot behavior with society's norms.

</details>


### [66] [Task-Distribution-Aware Counterweight Synthesis and Constrained Co-Design for Serial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15082)
*Mohammad Abbadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务分布感知的被动配重综合框架，将运行分布 ρ(q) 显式纳入设计，以加权均方残差重力矩最小化获得配重矩闭式最优；三连杆案例表明最优配重质量随任务分布变化超过40%，并使零负载可行任务空间覆盖率由78.1%提升至93.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动配重是简单的重力补偿器，但仅根据单一姿态选择的配重，通常对机械臂实际执行的构型与任务并非最优，因此需要面向任务分布进行设计。

Method: 定义配重矩 p=m_c r_c 及重力力矩 -g p φ(q)，在运行分布 ρ(q) 下最小化加权均方残差重力矩，得到闭式最优 p*=E_ρ[τ_g φ]/(g E_ρ[φ²])；若负载重力矩对负载质量仿射，则最优配重矩也仿射：p*(m_p,ρ)=p_0*(ρ)+m_p K_p(ρ)。固定静矩时，附加配重惯量 I_c=p r_c、质量 m_c=p/r_c，故质量-半径选择欠定，需指定物理约束。以恢复的三连杆机械臂为案例，比较不同操作分布，并采用非支配前沿、额定力矩参考的全关节筛选及集中点质量轨迹研究。

Result: 在 r_c=0.20 m 下，零负载等效最优质量分别为：均匀关节空间 0.672 kg，近似均匀任务空间 0.683 kg，代表性拾放任务族 0.713 kg，高重力偏置分布 0.952 kg，仅因操作分布不同即变化超过40%。非支配前沿表明优选质量-半径对依赖所声明的工程边界。额定力矩参考的全关节筛选使零负载可行任务空间覆盖率由无补偿时的78.1%提升至均匀分布设计的93.7%。集中点质量轨迹研究给出暂定交叉：极剧烈运动时无配重更优，运动变慢时更强补偿更优。

Conclusion: 配重最优设计显著依赖操作分布与工程约束；任务分布感知框架可给出闭式及仿射最优解，并改善任务空间覆盖。执行器与动力学结果属于工程后果研究，而非物理验证。

Abstract: Passive counterweights are simple gravity compensators, but a counterweight selected from a single pose is not generally optimal for the configurations and tasks a manipulator actually executes. This paper develops a task-distribution-aware synthesis framework in which the operating distribution $\rho(q)$ enters the design explicitly. For a counterweight moment $p=m_c r_c$ with gravity torque $-gp\phi(q)$, the weighted mean-square residual gravity torque has the closed-form minimizer $p^*=E_\rho[\tau_g\phi]/(gE_\rho[\phi^2])$. If payload gravity torque is affine in payload mass, the optimum is also affine: $p^*(m_p,\rho)=p_0^*(\rho)+m_pK_p(\rho)$. For fixed static moment, added counterweight inertia is $I_c=pr_c$ while mass is $m_c=p/r_c$, so mass-radius selection is underdetermined unless physical constraints are specified. A recovered three-link manipulator is used as a case study. At $r_c=0.20$ m, zero-payload equivalent optima are 0.672 kg for uniform joint-space operation, 0.683 kg for approximately uniform task-space operation, 0.713 kg for a representative pick-and-place family, and 0.952 kg for a high-gravity-biased distribution, a change of more than 40% caused solely by the operating distribution. Nondominated fronts show that preferred mass-radius pairs depend on declared engineering bounds. A rated-torque-referenced all-joint screen increases zero-payload feasible task-space coverage from 78.1% without compensation to 93.7% for the uniform-distribution design. A lumped point-mass trajectory study gives a provisional crossover from no counterweight at very aggressive motion to stronger compensation as motion slows. These actuator and dynamic results are engineering consequence studies rather than physical validation.

</details>


### [67] [Steering Generative Robot Policies with Lexicographic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15014)
*Yixuan Jia,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种推理时引导冻结生成式机器人策略的方法，使其在不更新策略权重的情况下满足按字典序排列的部署目标与偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成式机器人策略虽能产生多样有效行为，但部署时会出现训练中未体现的需求与偏好，且这些目标的优先级会随部署而变化；例如需先满足本体可行性约束，再在可行范围内遵循用户偏好。

Method: 针对基于扩散或流匹配的冻结生成式策略，在采样器中引入两项修改：一是对采样轨迹施加动态屏障引导，约束低优先级更新，使高优先级代价至一阶不增加；二是采用级联筛选，按各优先级逐级过滤候选样本并选择执行样本。策略权重保持不变。

Result: 在导航基准上，该方法相比冻结策略提升了成功率、可通行性与偏好满足度，并显著优于调参后的加权和基线。相同方法迁移到 LIBERO 上的流匹配操作策略后，在不降低任务成功率的情况下提升偏好满足度。受控操作研究表明，在固定权重可匹配目标排序的场景中，动态屏障能在更宽的参数范围内达到相当的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 冻结的生成式机器人策略可通过推理时采样器修改有效遵循字典序部署目标，无需重新训练或修改权重，并具备跨导航与操作任务的可迁移性和更好的参数鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pretrained generative robot policies can produce effective behaviors across diverse environments, but deployment can lead to requirements and preferences that may not have been represented during training. Furthermore, at deployment, an operator, user, or application may assign these requirements and preferences a priority order that can vary across deployments. For example, embodiment-specific feasibility constraints may need to be satisfied first, while user-specific preferences guide behavior among the feasible options. We show that a frozen generative robot policy---based on either diffusion or flow matching---can be steered at inference time to respect such lexicographically ordered deployment objectives. To achieve this, we introduce two modifications to the sampler. First, we apply dynamic-barrier guidance to sampled trajectories, constraining lower-priority updates so that higher-priority costs do not increase (up to first order). Second, we select the executed sample using a cascade that successively filters candidate samples according to each priority level. The policy weights remain unchanged. On a navigation benchmark, we demonstrate that our method improves success, traversability, and preference compliance over the frozen policy, and achieves substantially better compliance than tuned weighted-sum baselines. The same method transfers to a flow-matching manipulation policy on LIBERO, where it improves compliance without reducing task success. A controlled manipulation study further shows that, in settings where a fixed weight can match the desired ordering, the dynamic barrier reaches comparable best performance over a substantially wider range of parameter settings.

</details>


### [68] [Atomic Motion Coordinate for Language-Steerable and Force-Responsive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15012)
*Jiaqi Zhai,Jingkai Zhao,Chen Yang,Siyuan Ma,Yutian Zhang,Liwen Yang,Qinglian Wu,Weiqi Fan,Yifei Wang,Yi Zheng,Chenxi Gu,Dong Wei,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Atomic Motion Coordinate（AMC），一种几何 grounded 的坐标表示，用于可语言引导且力响应的 VLA 操作，可显著提升原子运动分离、OOD 泛化与力适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：仅改变语言指令能否重定向 VLA 策略的末端执行器，还是视觉驱动的运动先验会主导控制。为此需要一种既能被文本/语言引导、又能响应接触力的操作表示。

Method: AMC 为每条机械臂定义 13 个带符号的平移、旋转和保持原子，在屏蔽视觉条件下由文本与前向运动学 grounding；该坐标通过加权码本对齐注入每个 action-expert block。接触历史通过有界球面残差调制同一坐标，并从固定 nominal latent 重新计算，仅重新生成尚未执行的 horizon 后缀。

Result: 在 7,520 次离线 horizon 干预中，opposite-atom separation 单臂/双臂达到 92.5%/83.1%，而 LA4VLA-style 为 39.1%/24.0%。每个任务 50 次真机试验中，AMC 将 OOD fruit progress 从 60.5% 提升至 87.8%；力适应将 Plug/Vase 从 59.0%/71.5% 提升至 78.5%/75.2%。

Conclusion: AMC 能有效重定向 VLA 运动并支持接触力自适应，在语言可引导性、OOD 泛化和力响应任务上显著优于 LA4VLA-style 等基线，表明几何原子坐标是提升 VLA 可操控性的有效方案。

Abstract: Can changing only the language instruction redirect a VLA policy's end effector, or does the visually driven motion prior dominate? We present Atomic Motion Coordinate, a geometry-grounded coordinate for steerable and force-responsive manipulation. Each arm owns thirteen signed translation, rotation, and hold atoms grounded from text and forward kinematics with vision withheld, and the coordinate is injected into every action-expert block via weighted codebook alignment. Contact history modulates the same coordinate through a bounded spherical residual that is recomputed from a fixed nominal latent to regenerate only the unexecuted horizon suffix. Across 7,520 offline horizon interventions, opposite-atom separation reaches 92.5/83.1% (single/dual) versus 39.1/24.0% for LA4VLA-style. Across 50 real-robot trials per task, AMC raises OOD fruit progress from 60.5% to 87.8%; force adaptation raises Plug/Vase from 59.0/71.5% to 78.5/75.2%.

</details>


### [69] [IMPACT-VLA: Interaction-aware Multimodal Propagation Attribution via Counterfactual Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15005)
*Jinwoong Kim,Sangjin Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMPACT-VLA，一种基于反事实轨迹的交互感知多模态传播归因方法，用于Vision-Language-Action策略，在30个LIBERO机器人操作任务上揭示模态贡献的阶段依赖性与跨阶段耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法主要测量局部敏感性或时间聚合重要性，难以捕捉执行阶段依赖的模态贡献以及跨阶段依赖；同时，各模态在哪些执行阶段影响任务成功、输入干预如何通过后续状态、观测和动作传播尚不清晰。

Method: IMPACT-VLA从成功参考rollout的动作转换构建行为阶段，将其与策略查询边界对齐，定义“阶段-模态块”为归因单元；通过闭环反事实重执行量化每个块对最终任务成功的贡献；进一步分析跨阶段非加性交互与轨迹传播，并区分行为恢复与功能恢复。实验在30个LIBERO机器人操作任务上使用OpenVLA-OFT进行。

Result: 在30个LIBERO任务中，25个任务（83.3%）出现主导模态转移；闭环归因比Static Action Perturbation更忠实地识别任务关键信息；在早期阶段输入替换下，负交互对的后期块边际增益增大约3.3倍；功能恢复可以在没有行为恢复的情况下发生。

Conclusion: 结果表明多模态输入在何时支持任务成功，以及其贡献在闭环执行中如何形成条件耦合；强调阶段依赖与跨阶段交互归因对于理解VLA策略的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies perform robot manipulation tasks using multimodal inputs such as visual observations, proprioceptive states, and language instructions. However, it remains unclear at which execution stages each modality contributes to final task success and how input interventions propagate through subsequent states, observations, and actions. Existing attribution approaches primarily measure local sensitivity or temporally aggregated importance, limiting their ability to capture phase-dependent contributions and cross-phase dependencies. We propose Interaction-aware Multimodal Propagation Attribution via Counterfactual Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Policies (IMPACT-VLA). IMPACT-VLA constructs behavioral phases from action transitions in a successful reference rollout, aligns them with policy query boundaries, and defines phase-modality blocks as attribution units. It then performs closed-loop counterfactual re-execution to quantify each block's contribution to final task success. We further analyze cross-phase non-additive interactions and trajectory propagation while distinguishing behavioral from functional recovery. Across 30 LIBERO robot manipulation tasks using OpenVLA-OFT, dominant-modality transitions occurred in 25 tasks (83.3%), and closed-loop attribution identified task-critical information more faithfully than Static Action Perturbation. Later-block marginal gains for negatively interacting pairs increased by approximately 3.3x under early-phase input replacement, while functional recovery could occur without behavioral recovery. These results reveal when multimodal inputs support task success and how their contributions become conditionally coupled during closed-loop execution.

</details>


### [70] [From Learned-Mode AV-Traffic Pairing to Planner Decisions: A Marginal-Preserving Study on Argoverse 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14997)
*Jingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究联合运动预测中AV未来与周围交通的配对结构是否影响规划器。通过保持边缘分布、候选、成本等固定的乘积控制，去除学习模式中的AV-交通配对，并在Argoverse 2上评估。结果显示配对结构会改变规划决策与相对成本，但actor-level预测指标不变；在近似浓度匹配后，记录轨迹结果差异大多消失，因此无法将结果效应与条件浓度变化分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合运动预测将AV未来与周围交通配对，但actor-level指标无法揭示该结构对下游规划器是否有用。需要干预配对，同时控制混杂因素，检验规划决策敏感性。

Method: 提出marginal-preserving product control：去除学习模式中的AV-交通配对，保留固定匀速配对；固定轨迹、规划条件前的actor-level边缘分布、候选、成本项与权重、fallback。干预也改变候选条件浓度。在Argoverse 2的1,400个held-out场景、12次运行中，比较route-level offline selections、recorded-trajectory regret、relative costs等；并进行concentration matching、pairing-strength与temperature sweep。

Result: τ=4 m时改变3.0%的route-level offline selections。Control-minus-joint recorded-trajectory regret为-0.026和-0.118（两种训练规模），crossed与seed-t区间跨零。τ=1 m时87.9%的route evaluations相对成本变化，8.1%的route-level offline selections变化。浓度匹配前描述性结果估计偏向control；匹配后大部分差距消失，剩余对比为+0.112和-0.047，crossed区间均跨零。Actor-level forecast metrics完全一致。配对强度与温度扫描显示决策对比随配对去除和更尖锐条件化而增大。

Conclusion: 联合预测中的AV-交通配对可改变规划器决策，即使actor-level指标不变；但近似浓度匹配表明无法将记录结果效应与条件浓度变化分离。因此该结构对规划有影响，但现有分析不能归因于学习模式配对本身。
