Abstract: Joint motion forecasts pair each autonomous-vehicle (AV) future with surrounding traffic, but actor-level metrics do not show whether that structure matters to a planner. We study this question with a marginal-preserving product control that removes AV-traffic pairing among the learned modes while retaining the fixed constant-velocity pair and holding trajectories, actor-level marginals before planner conditioning, candidates, the cost terms and weights, and fallback fixed. The intervention also changes candidate-conditioned concentration. Across twelve runs on 1,400 held-out Argoverse 2 scenarios, the intervention changes 3.0% of route-level offline selections at $\tau=4$ m. Control-minus-joint recorded-trajectory regret is $-0.026$ and $-0.118$ at the two training sizes; crossed and seed-$t$ intervals span zero. At $\tau=1$ m, relative costs change in 87.9% of route evaluations and route-level offline selections in 8.1%. Before concentration matching, descriptive outcome estimates favor the control. Most of this gap disappears along an approximate concentration-matching path; the remaining contrasts are $+0.112$ and $-0.047$, and both crossed intervals span zero. Actor-level forecast metrics remain identical. Pairing-strength and temperature sweeps show that the decision contrast grows with pairing removal and sharper conditioning. The intervention changes planner decisions even though actor-level metrics remain unchanged. The matching analysis, however, cannot separate any recorded-outcome effect of learned-mode AV-traffic pairing from the accompanying change in conditioned concentration.

</details>


### [71] [Two-Stage Personalized Gait Phase Estimation in Stroke Survivors During Exoskeleton-Assisted Walking: An Offline Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14984)
*Hyungseok Ryu,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估了面向卒中患者的个性化步态相位估计：基于大腿IMU，将健康步态预训练模型通过两阶段序贯自适应迁移至患者，并在动力膝外骨骼行走中验证精度与嵌入式时序。Transformer最终相位误差最低（2.90±1.13%步态周期），但LSTM在嵌入式实时性上存在少量截止期错失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中患者步态异质性大，健康人预训练模型直接应用时相位估计误差高；动力外骨骼辅助行走需要高精度、低时延且可个性化更新的步态相位估计。因此需评估功能IMU对齐与两阶段序贯自适应能否兼顾离线可行性与嵌入式计算时序。

Method: 使用大腿IMU信号，以足跟力敏电阻（FSR）提供参考相位标签用于离线自适应与评估。Stage 1建立蒸馏正则化的受试者特定模型；Stage 2采用低秩自适应（LoRA）进行条件细化。比较LSTM、TCN和Transformer；5名卒中患者在动力膝外骨骼下行走；采用留一受试者超参数选择及顺序测试后自适应Stage 2回放；并进行策略消融（每周期更新与条件更新）和嵌入式测试。

Result: 相对于未自适应基线，Stage1+2将平均受试者相位RMSE分别降低84.2%、77.0%和60.7%；Transformer最终误差最低，为2.90±1.13%步态周期，足跟着地时序误差23.7±4.5 ms。消融显示每周期更新通常误差最低或接近最低，条件更新降低更新频率且精度差异小。个性化后，对齐对相位误差影响因模型而异，但保持或改善足跟着地检测，并降低LSTM和Transformer的足跟着地时序误差。嵌入式测试中TCN和Transformer在Stage 2更新时保持100 Hz推理且无截止期错失，LSTM在6.6%推理中错过10 ms截止期；所有更新在0.8 s内完成。

Conclusion: 结果支持该框架用于外骨骼辅助行走的离线可行性与嵌入式计算时序；个性化步态相位估计可显著降低卒中患者相位误差，但需权衡模型精度与实时更新开销。

Abstract: This study evaluated personalized gait phase estimation for stroke survivors using functional inertial measurement unit (IMU) alignment and two-stage sequential adaptation of models pre-trained on healthy gait. The estimator used signals from a thigh-mounted IMU. Heel force-sensitive resistor measurements provided reference phase labels for offline adaptation and evaluation. Stage 1 established a distillation-regularized participant-specific model, and Stage 2 performed conditional refinement using low-rank adaptation. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN), and Transformer models were evaluated in five stroke survivors walking with a powered knee exoskeleton using leave-one-subject-out hyperparameter selection and sequential test-then-adapt Stage 2 replay. Relative to the non-adapted baselines, Stage 1+2 reduced the mean participant-wise phase root mean square error by 84.2%, 77.0%, and 60.7%, respectively. The Transformer achieved the lowest final error (2.90 +- 1.13$% of the gait cycle) and heel-strike timing error (23.7 +- 4.5ms). Policy-specific ablations showed that every-cycle updates generally produced the lowest or near-lowest error, whereas conditional updating reduced the update frequency with small accuracy differences. After personalization, alignment produced model-dependent changes in phase error while preserving or improving heel-strike detection and reducing heel-strike timing error for the LSTM and Transformer. Concurrent embedded tests showed that the TCN and Transformer maintained 100-Hz inference during Stage 2 updates without deadline misses, whereas the LSTM missed the 10-ms deadline in 6.6% of inferences. All updates completed within 0.8s. These results support the offline feasibility and embedded computational timing of the proposed framework for exoskeleton-assisted walking.

</details>


### [72] [Comparing Trajectories from Positions Alone: Curvature-Based Time Alignment and Drift Error Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14936)
*Effie Daum,Daniele De Martini,Claire Dune,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对机器人状态估计、定位与SLAM中轨迹评估依赖ATE/RPE却常隐含未说明假设的问题，提出一套标准化轨迹评估协议，核心是基于曲率信号的时间对齐和按行驶距离归一化的误差度量，并显式处理时间同步、采样对齐与外参标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人领域难以获得比待评估轨迹更准确的大规模独立参考轨迹，而常用ATE/RPE工具依赖未明确说明的假设与参数；若不报告这些设定，误差会误导且阻碍公平比较。

Method: 提出轨迹评估协议：采用基于曲率信号的新型时间对齐方法，使用按行驶距离归一化的误差度量；显式考虑时间同步、采样对齐和外参标定，并通过敏感性分析量化其影响。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值或定量结果；所述结果为该协议可提升状态估计、定位与SLAM轨迹评估的严谨性、可复现性和标准化程度，并通过敏感性分析揭示同步、对齐与标定因素的影响。

Conclusion: 该协议为轨迹评估提供更严格、可复现、标准化的方案，有助于减少因未报告评估假设而导致的误导性误差与不公平比较。

Abstract: In field robotics, acquiring independent large-scale reference trajectories more accurate than the evaluated estimates remains an open challenge. The domain is widely reliant on Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and Relative Pose Error (RPE), computed with automated tools, that rest on assumptions and evaluation parameters rarely made explicit. When unreported, the errors can be misleading and hinder fair comparisons. This paper introduces a trajectory-evaluation protocol for standardized and reliable accuracy assessment in state estimation, localization, and Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). The approach combines a novel temporal alignment method based on curvature signals with an error metric normalized by travelled distance. We explicitly account for temporal synchronization, sampling alignment, and extrinsic calibration, quantifying their influence through a sensitivity analysis. The proposed protocol contributes to more rigorous, reproducible, and standardized trajectory evaluation.

</details>


### [73] [Exact Feasibility Certification and Optimal Responsibility Allocation for Multi-Robot CBF Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14935)
*Chandan Kumar Sah,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多机器人CBF安全滤波器的精确可行性证书与共享约束最优分配方法，可解释并缓解QP不可行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人CBF安全滤波器可能不可行，但QP失败无法说明冲突原因、责任智能体/交互以及如何解决，因此需要可行性诊断与冲突消解手段。

Method: 针对异构控制仿射动力学与凸输入集，将安全约束需求与执行器供给分离，构造量化可行性储备的精确证书；据此判断CBF增益调节或增加驱动能否解决不可行，并定位冲突智能体/交互。进一步提出最大化最差局部可行性裕度的共享安全约束分配算法，多面体输入集下可化为线性规划。

Result: 在320组配对闭环仿真中，所提分配将不可行控制步从约50%降至6.2%，安全违规运行从118/160降至24/160；在52次不可行事例中，证书识别出的交互经松弛后在94%情况下恢复可行性。

Conclusion: 该可行性证书能有效诊断多机器人CBF不可行性并指导共享安全约束分配，显著提升闭环控制可行性与安全性。

Abstract: Multi-robot Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety filters can become infeasible, but a failed quadratic program (QP) does not indicate why the conflict occurred or how to resolve it. To address this, we develop an exact feasibility certificate for multi-agent CBF filters with heterogeneous control-affine dynamics and convex input sets. The certificate quantifies a feasibility reserve by separating the demand imposed by safety constraints from the available actuator supply. This decomposition shows when CBF gain tuning or increased actuation can and cannot resolve infeasibility, and identifies the agents and interactions responsible for the conflict. We further propose an algorithm to optimally allocate shared safety constraints by maximizing the worst local feasibility margin, yielding a linear program for polyhedral input sets. In $320$ paired closed-loop simulations, the proposed allocation reduces infeasible control steps from roughly $50\%$ to $6.2\%$, and reduces safety-violating runs from $118/160$ to $24/160$. In addition, across $52$ infeasibility events, the certificate identifies an interaction whose relaxation restores feasibility in $94\%$ of cases.

</details>


### [74] [HydroMap: Probabilistic Water Surface Elevation Mapping for Semantic Scene Representation in Inland Waterways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14903)
*Zhongbi Luo,Yunjia Wang,Herman Bruyninckx,Peter Slaets*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HydroMap，一个里程计解耦框架：利用双目观测重建内河水面高程，并与结构地图融合，生成持久概率高程图及统一的 2.5D 语义地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内河自主水面载具需要持续表示周边结构与水面，但 LiDAR SLAM 对水面回波稀疏或缺失，导致重建场景中水面缺失。

Method: HydroMap 将每帧水面点与传播的双目及位姿不确定性构成联合栅格观测，连续观测融合为持久概率高程图；再与结构几何结合，转换为包含水面、边界、结构和上方区域的统一 2.5D 语义表示。

Result: 在 Pohang Canal 和 Leuven Vaart 数据集上，相对于同一地图坐标系中的 LiDAR 参考，高程 RMSE 低于 5 cm；高程图和语义图分别以 2 Hz 和 1 Hz 发布。

Conclusion: HydroMap 以持续的水面表示补充 LiDAR 地图，支持内河航道中的下游导航任务。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles operating in inland waterways require a persistent representation of both surrounding structures and the water surface. LiDAR-based simultaneous localization and mapping often produces sparse or missing water returns, leaving this operational surface absent from the reconstructed scene. We propose HydroMap, an odometry-decoupled framework that reconstructs water surface elevation from stereo observations and integrates it with the structural map. Per-frame water points form joint cell observations with propagated stereo and pose uncertainty, and successive observations are fused into a persistent probabilistic elevation map. Semantic map conversion then combines the elevation map with structural geometry in a unified 2.5D representation of water, boundaries, structures, and overhead regions. On the Pohang Canal and Leuven Vaart datasets, the elevation RMSE remains below 5 cm relative to LiDAR references expressed in the same map frame. The elevation and semantic maps are published at 2 Hz and 1 Hz, respectively. HydroMap thereby complements LiDAR maps with a persistent representation of the water surface for downstream navigation in inland waterways.

</details>


### [75] [Real-World Reinforcement Learning with MPC Scaffolding for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14878)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Zhaodong Yang,Jinda Cui,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以采样型模型预测控制（MPC）作为支架的真实世界灵巧强化学习框架，无需人类示范或纠正动作，即可高效学习并迁移到灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界强化学习有望直接从物理交互中学习灵巧操作策略，但早期探索效率低且失败代价高；因此需要一种结构化先验和任务导向引导来降低学习成本与干预需求。

Method: 使用少量采样型MPC轨迹初始化离线回放缓冲区，并预训练actor与critic；在线学习阶段，MPC间歇性引导数据收集，离策略Soft Actor-Critic同时利用先前MPC经验和新增物理交互进行训练，控制权逐渐从MPC过渡到学习策略。

Result: 在16自由度Allegro手上进行连续手内旋转：用20条硬件采集的MPC轨迹（12分钟）初始化后，在线RL 7分钟即达到纯策略评估100%成功率（5/5）；在线训练平均约掉落3次物体；学习20分钟后策略旋转速度超过MPC控制器5倍以上；实现连续1000次旋转且超过110分钟无掉落。消融显示MPC预训练、保留MPC经验和在线MPC引导具有互补收益；并展示了对不同物体几何形状的快速适应以及目标条件重定向能力。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了高效、低干预的真实世界灵巧强化学习，表明采样型MPC脚手架可显著提升真实物理交互中的学习效率与策略性能。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising route to dexterous manipulation policies that can adapt directly from physical interaction, but learning is hindered by inefficient early exploration and costly failures. We propose a framework that uses sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) as scaffolding for real-world dexterous RL, providing structured prior experience and task-directed guidance during learning without human demonstrations or corrective actions. A small set of MPC trajectories is first used to populate an offline replay buffer and to pretrain the actor and critic. During online learning, MPC intermittently guides data collection while an off-policy Soft Actor-Critic learner trains from both prior MPC experience and newly collected physical interaction, with control gradually transitioning to the learned policy. On continuous in-hand rotation with a 16-DoF Allegro hand, the method reaches 100\% success in policy-only evaluation (5/5 trials) after 7 minutes of online RL, following initialization with 20 MPC trajectories collected on hardware in 12 minutes. Online training incurs about three object drops on average. After 20 minutes of online learning, the policy achieves more than five times the rotation speed of the MPC controller. It completes 1000 consecutive rotations over more than 110 minutes without a drop. Ablations show complementary benefits from MPC-based pretraining, retained MPC experience, and online MPC guidance. We further demonstrate rapid adaptation to different object geometries and successful goal-conditioned reorientation, showing that the framework enables efficient, low-intervention, real-world dexterous RL.

</details>


### [76] [Primitive-Informed Sampling-Based MPC for Multi-Fingered Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14868)
*Emek Barış Küçüktabak,Karankumar Patel,Jinda Cui,Zhaodong Yang,Kazuhiro Sasabuchi,Jun Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基元引导的采样MPC框架，用于多指灵巧手操作；通过低维操作基元偏置采样并优化关节残差，结合rollout约束，在Allegro手上实现连续手内旋转、抓取与臂手协调任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样MPC无需对复杂接触动力学求梯度，但直接在高维关节空间探索效率低，且性能强烈依赖采样分布。

Method: 使用编码协调手指运动的低维操作基元来偏置采样；同时优化关节级残差，使基元运动适应当前手-物构型；在前向仿真中用任务相关rollout约束剔除不可行轨迹，提高采样预算利用率。

Result: 在物理Allegro手与同步MuJoCo数字孪生上评估。消融表明基元和残差对可靠连续手内旋转均必要；仅增加采样预算不能恢复协调；rollout约束显著提升成功率。单物体提取的基元可跨物体尺寸和模型失配保持有效；框架还支持抓取、物体重定向及臂手协调reach-grasp-transport，基元可来自仿真训练策略和人类手部运动数据。

Conclusion: 基元引导的采样MPC能有效缓解高维灵巧操作中的探索难题，并展现出跨物体、跨模型误差与多任务扩展能力。

Abstract: We present a primitive-informed sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) framework for multi-fingered dexterous manipulation. Sampling-based MPC avoids the need for gradients through complex contact dynamics, but direct exploration of the high-dimensional joint space is inefficient and makes performance strongly dependent on the sampling distribution. Our framework biases sampling using low-dimensional manipulation primitives that encode coordinated finger motions, while simultaneously optimizing joint-level residuals to adapt these motions to the current hand-object configuration. Task-related rollout constraints reject infeasible trajectories during forward simulation, improving the effective use of the sampling budget. We evaluate the approach on a physical Allegro hand using a synchronized MuJoCo digital twin. Ablations show that both the primitive and residual are necessary for reliable continuous in-hand rotation, that increasing the sampling budget alone does not recover this coordination, and that rollout constraints substantially improve success rate. A primitive extracted from a single object remains effective across object sizes and under model mismatch, and the framework further supports grasping, object reorientation, and coordinated arm-hand reach-grasp-transport, using primitives extracted from both a simulation-trained policy and human hand-motion data.

</details>


### [77] [Belief-Adaptive Online Autonomy for Quadrotor UAV Navigation under GNSS Degradation in Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14806)
*Deepak Kumar Panda,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种信念自适应在线自主框架，在EKF中引入显式GNSS信任建模、二阶在线信念自适应和延迟感知乱序测量处理，以提升GNSS退化城市空域中四旋翼的状态估计与轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空域中GNSS受多径、遮挡和延迟影响，产生非平稳且时间相关的误差，导致传统GNSS-IMU融合性能下降；因此需要无需先验环境知识和离线训练的可靠在线自主能力。

Method: 将GNSS信任表示为潜在信念状态，用于调制测量权重和多径偏差不确定性，并利用EKF一致性信号在线更新；采用二阶在线信念自适应而非反应式协方差调参，同时处理延迟导致的乱序测量；框架保留经典GNSS-IMU融合结构，可直接集成到现有飞行控制管线。

Result: 在包含相关多径、随机延迟和障碍约束的仿真城市空中交通场景中，相比朴素、自适应和一阶基线，所提方法展现出更好的信念收敛、更平滑的轨迹以及更低的估计与跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 该框架保持经典GNSS-IMU融合结构并可直接集成，能够在GNSS退化环境中支持鲁棒的在线自主飞行。

Abstract: Reliable online autonomy is critical for quadrotor operation in urban airspaces, where global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) measurements suffer from multipath, blockage, and latency issues, introducing non-stationary, temporally correlated errors that degrade conventional GNSS-IMU fusion. This paper presents a belief-adaptive online autonomy framework that augments an extended Kalman filter (EKF) with explicit GNSS trust modelling, second-order online belief adaptation, and latency-aware out-of-sequence measurement handling. GNSS trust is represented as a latent belief state that modulates measurement weighting and multipath bias uncertainty, and is updated online using EKF consistency signals. Unlike reactive covariance tuning, the proposed approach enables proactive and stable sensor trust adaptation without prior environmental knowledge or offline training. Evaluation in simulated urban air mobility scenarios with correlated multipath, stochastic latency, and obstacle constraints demonstrates improved belief convergence, smoother trajectories, and reduced estimation and tracking errors compared to naive, adaptive, and first-order baselines. The framework preserves classical GNSS-IMU fusion structure and can be integrated directly into existing flight control pipelines, supporting robust online autonomy in GNSS degraded environments.

</details>


### [78] [Language-Guided Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Sensor Material Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14783)
*Mashood M. Mohsan,Muhayy Ud Din,Binzhao Xu,Ahmad Abubakar,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言引导蒸馏框架，学习传感器鲁棒的触觉表征，在少样本和跨传感器迁移上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要触觉来安全可靠地操作物体，因为柔软度、纹理和接触稳定性等属性难以仅靠视觉推断；但视觉触觉传感器受光学、弹性体特性和光照差异影响，对同一材料的观测不同，导致在单一或多传感器上训练时泛化能力差。语言可编码跨硬件不变的触觉高层语义属性（如粗糙、柔软、滑溜），提供天然的传感器无关监督信号。

Method: 构建含39K样本、带人工标注材料标签的触觉-语言数据集；训练触觉编码器，将传感器特定的触觉图像与语言嵌入在共享语义空间中对齐，形成语言引导蒸馏框架。

Result: 在100-shot设置下达到95%准确率；跨传感器迁移平均提升13.3%准确率；在六个现有触觉数据集上最高获得19%准确率增益。

Conclusion: 语言引导蒸馏能够实现可扩展且硬件无关的触觉表征学习；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Robots need touch to manipulate objects safely and reliably, as many properties, such as softness, texture, and contact stability, are hard to infer from vision alone. However, vision-based tactile sensors yield different observations of the same material due to variations in optics, elastomer properties, and illumination, leading to poor generalization when trained on a single or multiple sensors. We propose a language-guided distillation framework for learning sensor-robust tactile representations. Language encodes high-level semantic properties of touch (e.g., rough, soft, slippery) that remain invariant across sensing hardware, providing a natural sensor-agnostic supervisory signal. We construct a 39K-sample touch-language dataset with human-annotated material labels and train a tactile encoder to align sensor-specific tactile images with language embeddings in a shared semantic space. We evaluate our approach for few-shot learning and cross-sensor transfer and benchmark it on six existing tactile datasets. Our method achieves 95% accuracy in the 100-shot setting, improves cross-sensor transfer by an average of 13.3% accuracy, and yields up to 19% accuracy gains across six existing tactile datasets. These results demonstrate that language-guided distillation enables scalable and hardware-agnostic tactile representation learning. Code and dataset are available at this https URL

</details>


### [79] [A Personalized Dynamic Balance Evaluation Paradigm for Hip Exoskeleton-Assisted Walking under Unexpected Ground Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14765)
*Yun Chen,Oluwasegun T. Akinniyi,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受试者特异的复合平衡代价，融合7个生物力学子指标，在单纯形上学习非负权重，并结合经验贝叶斯层次模型估计各髋辅助条件为最优的后验概率与高概率候选集；在3名受试者、46种条件、单侧皮带滑动扰动中，80%后验概率集中于1–5个候选条件，优于等权融合与PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 髋外骨骼有望改善意外步态扰动后的恢复，但个性化助力仍困难：平衡是多维的，且人在环实验通常样本小、噪声大，需要可量化、个体化并考虑不确定性的平衡评价与候选条件选择方法。

Method: 构建受试者特异复合平衡代价，整合稳定裕度、质心动力学、全身角动量等7个生物力学子指标；将子指标转换为方向对齐、无量纲的代价特征；在单纯形上学习非负融合权重；结合经验贝叶斯层次模型，估计每个测试条件为最优的后验概率P(best)及高概率候选集大小K0.8。评估中3名受试者以1.1 m/s行走，接受单侧皮带滑动扰动，覆盖46种髋辅助条件，全预算分析每条件B=4次重复，并进行留一重复重拟合。

Result: 全预算分析中，选择器将80%后验概率集中在46个条件中的1–5个；等权融合为2–12个，PCA融合为4–37个。选中条件试验的观测复合代价低于无扭矩试验，P2和P3达到名义p<0.05。留一重复重拟合显示所有受试者平均留出秩相关为正，学习权重与候选集具有中等稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些概念验证结果支持使用受试者特异复合平衡评价，在基于扰动的的人在环实验中进行候选条件选择；更小的候选集有望缩短个性化实验，并减少受试者暴露于重复扰动的次数。

Abstract: Hip exoskeletons may improve recovery from unexpected gait perturbations, yet personalizing assistance remains difficult because balance is multidimensional and human-in-the-loop experiments are small-sample and noisy. We present a participant-specific composite balance cost that integrates seven biomechanical sub-metrics spanning margin of stability, center-of-mass dynamics, and whole-body angular momentum. The sub-metrics are converted to direction-aligned, dimensionless cost features, and nonnegative fusion weights are learned on the simplex. Coupled with an empirical-Bayes hierarchical model, the learned-composite selector estimates each tested condition's posterior probability of being best, P(best), and a high-probability candidate set with size $K_{0.8}$. The framework was evaluated with three participants walking at 1.1 m/s during unilateral belt-slip perturbations across 46 hip-assistance conditions. In the full-budget analysis (B = 4 repeats per condition), the selector concentrated 80% of the posterior probability within 1 to 5 of 46 conditions, compared with 2 to 12 for equal-weight fusion and 4 to 37 for principal component analysis fusion. This smaller candidate set could shorten personalization experiments and limit participants' exposure to repeated perturbations in future studies. Selected-condition trials showed lower observed composite costs than no-torque trials, with nominal p < 0.05 for P2 and P3. Leave-one-repeat-out refits yielded positive mean held-out rank correlations for all participants and moderate stability of the learned weights and candidate sets. These proof-of-concept results support participant-specific composite balance evaluation for candidate selection in perturbation-based human-in-the-loop experiments.

</details>


### [80] [An Adaptive Fixed-Time Line-of-Sight Guidance Scheme for 3D Path Following of Underwater Vehicles: Theory and Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14756)
*Hanzhi Yang,Jalil Chavez-Galaviz,Nina Mahmoudian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于AUV三维路径跟踪的鲁棒固定时间自适应LOS制导框架，在无需修改底层控制器的情况下实现预设时间收敛、快速侧滑补偿和弯曲路径性能提升，并通过仿真与Iver 3 AUV实验验证其优于现有自适应LOS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在动态不确定海洋环境中可靠路径跟踪至关重要，但传统LOS制导依赖渐近收敛，扰动恢复慢且跟踪性能不可预测；现有鲁棒控制方法常需修改载体控制器，限制其在商用AUV平台上的实际应用。

Method: 将固定时间稳定性理论与LOS制导结合，构建鲁棒固定时间自适应LOS制导框架，保证路径跟踪在预设时间内收敛且收敛时间不依赖初始条件；设计固定时间自适应估计器快速补偿洋流引起的时变侧滑扰动；引入时变前视机制提升弯曲路径跟踪性能；利用Lyapunov分析证明框架的固定时间稳定性。

Result: 数值仿真和物理实验表明，相比最先进的自适应LOS方法，该框架具有更优的跟踪精度、收敛速度和抗干扰能力。仿真中，时变前视变体在弯曲路径跟踪中将横向和垂直跟踪RMSE分别降低69.37%和67.46%；Iver 3 AUV现场实验中，所提固定时间制导相比传统自适应LOS将直线路径平均跟踪误差降低56.35%，弯曲路径降低27.59%。

Conclusion: 所提方法为复杂海洋环境下AUV实现可靠自主导航提供了一种实用的制导级解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable path tracking is crucial for autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating in dynamic and uncertain marine environments. However, traditional line-of-sight (LOS) guidance methods rely on asymptotic convergence, resulting in slow disturbance recovery and unpredictable tracking performance. Existing robust control methods typically require modifications to the underlying vehicle controller, limiting their practical application on commercial AUV platforms. This paper proposes a robust fixed-time adaptive LOS guidance framework for 3D path tracking for AUVs. By combining fixed-time stability theory with LOS guidance, this method guarantees path tracking convergence within a preset time range, with the convergence time independent of initial conditions. Furthermore, a fixed-time adaptive estimator is developed to rapidly compensate for time-varying sideslip disturbances caused by ocean currents. A time-varying look-ahead mechanism is also introduced to improve tracking performance on curved paths. Lyapunov analysis proves the fixed-time stability of the proposed framework, and numerical simulations and physical experiments demonstrate that, compared to state-of-the-art adaptive LOS methods, this framework exhibits superior tracking accuracy, convergence speed, and anti-interference capability. In simulation, the time-varying look-ahead variant reduced cross-track and vertical-track RMSE by 69.37\% and 67.46\%, respectively, during curved-path tracking. In field experiments with an Iver 3 AUV, the proposed fixed-time guidance reduced average tracking error by 56.35\% in straight-path evaluation and 27.59\% in curved-path evaluation compared with conventional adaptive LOS this http URL proposed method provides a practical guidance-level solution for achieving reliable autonomous navigation of AUVs in complex marine environments.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Dead Reckoning: A Point of View on Camera--DAS--GNSS Continuity in Road Tunnels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14748)
*Lyes Saad Saoud,Hesham A. Rakha,Mona Jaber,Moussa Ayyash*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张隧道内定位应视为基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性问题，而非基于最后一次可信卫星定位的外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路隧道遮挡卫星信号，而网联自动驾驶车辆仍需连续、可归因且完整性受限的定位。传统外推方法无法满足这些要求。

Method: 提出一种基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性框架：可信的门户卫星解提供全球锚点，分布式声学传感(DAS)提供连续运动证据，摄像头提供稀疏身份与车道锚点，车载传感提供短期动态，边缘计算负责关联、融合、完整性监测与受保护的重捕获。地面真值仅用于离线校准与验证。

Result: 本文制定了一个可证伪的研究与部署议程，旨在从同步多模态证据推进至经过验证且完整性感知的隧道定位连续性。

Conclusion: 隧道定位不应依赖卫星定位外推，而应作为基础设施辅助的跨模态轨迹连续性问题来处理，并通过多模态融合与完整性监测实现。

Abstract: Road tunnels remove satellite visibility where connected and automated vehicles still require continuous, attributable, and integrity-bounded positioning. This Point of View argues that tunnel localization should be treated as infrastructure-assisted cross-modal track continuity, not as extrapolation from the last trusted satellite fix. A trusted portal satellite solution provides the global anchor, distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) continuous motion evidence, cameras sparse identity and lane anchors, onboard sensing short-term dynamics, and edge computing association, fusion, integrity monitoring, and guarded reacquisition. Ground truth is reserved for offline calibration and validation. The article develops a falsifiable research and deployment agenda for progressing from synchronized multimodal evidence to validated, integrity-aware tunnel positioning continuity.

</details>


### [82] [A Novel Robot-Assisted Learning Pedagogy for Children with ASD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14710)
*Laura Boccanfuso,Erin Barney,Marilena Mademtzi,Claire Foster,Quan Wang,Colette Torres,Lisa Chen,Brian Scassellati,Pamela Ventola,Frederick Shic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估一种融入“通过教学学习”教学法的机器人辅助自闭症干预，比较机器人与人类同伴条件下学龄ASD儿童在社交技能任务上的表现与参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往机器人辅助自闭症干预多将机器人作为教师、临床助手或能力更强的同伴，并依赖受训专业人员保证效果；但多数ASD儿童在主流学校普通课堂中度过80%以上时间，却少有研究将经过验证的课堂教学法纳入机器人干预。

Method: 研究引入一种面向学龄ASD儿童社交技能提升的新型教学法，将learning-by-teaching（通过教学学习）融入机器人辅助干预，并设置机器人条件与人类同伴（human confederate）条件，比较两种条件下儿童在社交技能任务中的表现，同时考察参与度及场景顺序影响。

Result: 80%参与者在机器人条件下表现更好（机器人条件平均表现63%，人类同伴条件37%），且不受场景顺序影响；90%参与者在机器人条件下参与度显著更高（机器人平均参与度61%，人类同伴32%），该效应未出现在人类同伴条件；分析表明机器人条件下的总体参与度促进了表现提升。

Conclusion: 在learning-by-teaching情境中使用机器人可能有助于提升ASD儿童的参与度，并改善其在简单社交技能任务上的表现。

Abstract: Interaction paradigms used in robot-assisted autism intervention have historically employed robots as teachers, clinical assistants, or more-abled peers to promote a variety of social skills. These modalities often leverage the expertise of trained practitioners to ensure that child-robot interactions are productive or clinically grounded to yield positive therapeutic benefits for children across the autism spectrum. Yet, despite the fact that the majority of children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) attend mainstream schools and spend 80% or more of their time in the general classroom [27], there is a paucity of research incorporating validated classroom teaching pedagogies into robot-assisted autism interventions. In this work, we introduce a novel teaching methodology for advancing social skills in school-aged children with ASD. We evaluate the effectiveness of a novel robot-assisted autism intervention which incorporates the learning-by-teaching pedagogy and explores the comparative benefits of employing a robot versus a human confederate for improved performance on a set of social skills tasks. Results show that 80% of study participants performed better in the robot condition (mean performance in the robot condition=63%, mean performance in the confederate condition=37%), irrespective of the scenario order. Further, 90% of all participants were significantly more engaged in the robot condition (mean engagement: robot=61%, confederate=32%) and, while the effect did not result in the confederate condition, analyses indicate that overall engagement in the robot condition contributed to improved performance. These results suggest that robots employed in a learning-by-teaching context may help enhance engagement and improve performance on a simple social skills task for children with ASD.

</details>


### [83] [Skill Composition for Legged Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14647)
*Daniel Gigliotti,Flavio Maiorana,Fabio Patrizi,Luca Iocchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性论述，主张把“独立子策略的组合”当作独立的研究问题，而不是交给随手可得的实现机制去处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体端到端策略要覆盖一切行为，训练慢、收敛不稳、难以单独验证；而针对窄问题的专用控制器训练快、收敛可靠、可独立验证。真正的薄弱环节在于技能之间的切换与过渡。若控制权能被安全、随时地在专用策略间传递，则“机器人下一步做什么”的决策可交给性质完全不同的组件（规划器、自动机、符号控制器），其行为可事先审查。

Method: 以论证为主，不含具体算法或实验：提出可靠组合是使分散技能成为可复用技能库的关键，并给出组合机制应满足的条件——能在任意时刻安全地移交控制权，从而使高层决策与底层执行解耦（策略只负责“执行”）。

Result: 摘要未报告任何实验、数据或量化结果，属于立场/观点论文，只给出论证性主张。

Conclusion: 将子策略组合视为一等研究问题，可实现可用的技能库；进而把“做什么”交给可预先审查的规划器、自动机或符号控制器，使机器人被信任的行为变得可验证。

Abstract: Robots, and humanoid robots in particular, are increasingly competent at individual behaviors, each obtained by training a specialized controller. A specialized skill is quick to train, converges reliably because the problem it faces is narrow, and can be validated on its own, none of which is true of a single end-to-end policy asked to cover everything. What remains fragile is the transition between them. We argue that the composition of independent sub-policies deserves to be treated as a research problem in its own right, rather than as an implementation detail left to whatever mechanism happens to be at hand. Reliable composition is what turns a collection of separate skills into a repertoire that can be used, extended and shared. More fundamentally, if control can be passed between specialized policies safely, and at any moment, the choice of what the robot should do next can be delegated to a component of an entirely different nature, such as a planner, an automaton or a symbolic controller, whose behavior can be inspected in advance. The policies would then only ever have to act, and what the robot can be trusted to do would become verifiable.

</details>


### [84] [REVOLVE: An Automated Closed-Loop Framework for Evolving Robot Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14633)
*Hanyu Liu,Qian Li,Yizhu Ding,Jiayi Wen,Keqiang Ren,Yunsheng Ma,Tao Jian,Zhihua Wang,Zhuofan Yu,Xinran Li,Zhigong Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REVOLVE 是一个以极少人工干预实现机器人操作策略持续进化的自动化闭环框架，通过自动重置与纠错、双循环进化，将真实部署转化为持续学习过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动机器人操作策略虽提升任务执行与泛化，但真实部署仍高度依赖人工进行失败评估、纠正和环境重置，且模型难以从失败与纠正经验中持续学习。

Method: 提出 REVOLVE，基于统一软件平台集成数据采集、策略训练与部署、失败恢复和持续学习。其 ARC 架构自动重置环境并干预纠正策略失败；DLE 将真实交互与失败–纠正数据反馈至策略学习，并利用外部不匹配记忆优化智能体判断，从而持续改进操作策略与监督智能体。

Result: 在四个真实机器人操作任务中，经过五次迭代，REVOLVE 将平均策略成功率提升 18.5%，智能体判断准确率提升 8.5%，同时将数据采集和部署测试中的人工投入分别减少 94.4% 和 95.1%。

Conclusion: REVOLVE 将真实世界部署转变为闭环学习过程，能够持续积累并利用执行经验，在显著减少人工干预的同时实现策略和监督模型的持续进化。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-driven robot manipulation policies have substantially improved task execution and generalization. However, real-world deployment still relies heavily on humans for failure assessment, correction, and environment reset, while models often fail to continually learn from failures and corrective experience. We present REVOLVE (Robot Evolving via Orchestrated Loops, Verification, and Experience), an automated closed-loop framework for evolving robot manipulation with minimal human intervention. Built on a unified software platform, REVOLVE integrates data collection, policy training and deployment, failure recovery, and continual learning into a single closed-loop workflow. Its Automated Reset and Collection (ARC) architecture automatically resets the environment and intervenes to correct policy failures. Dual-Loop Evolution (DLE) continually improves the manipulation policy and agent by feeding real-world interaction and failure--correction data back into policy learning and using an external mismatch memory to refine agent judgments. Experiments across four real-world manipulation tasks show that, after five iterations, REVOLVE improves average policy success rate by 18.5% and agent judgment accuracy by 8.5%, while reducing human effort in data collection and deployment testing by 94.4% and 95.1%, respectively. These results demonstrate that REVOLVE transforms real-world deployment into a closed-loop learning process that continually accumulates and uses execution experience, enabling continual evolution of both the policy and supervisory model with substantially less human intervention.

</details>


### [85] [Robotic Servo Tracking of Moving Targets with Dynamic Imitation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14589)
*Yazhe Luo,Sipu Ruan,Yifei Li,Diansheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模仿轨迹约束的机器人视觉伺服跟踪方法，可在移动目标跟踪中显式约束中间运动轨迹，实现动态避障与高精度收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉伺服跟踪方法通过将视觉残差映射为控制速度来获得快速响应，但对中间运动过程约束较弱，易导致轨迹不连续、振荡或保守行为，因此难以施加显式轨迹约束。

Method: 建立并分析机器人接近移动目标的动态模型及其收敛性；引入时间可伸缩变形机制，以及包含形状与幅值分量的轨迹调制，实时生成一系列轨迹；从这些轨迹中自适应确定跟踪点以形成动态约束；再根据目标位姿微分或跟踪关键特征计算机器人速度，使机器人沿约束轨迹运动。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，在复杂环境中，该方法能够实现动态避障和高精度收敛，并优于多种现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 该研究通过模仿轨迹约束将轨迹生成与视觉伺服跟踪结合，为移动目标跟踪提供了具有显式中间过程约束的可行方案，提升了复杂环境下的避障与收敛性能。

Abstract: Imposing explicit trajectory constraints in robot visual servoing remains challenging. Existing tracking methods achieve fast responses by mapping visual residuals to control velocities, but they have weak constraints on the intermediate motion process, which lead to trajectory discontinuity, oscillation, or conservative behaviors. To enable constrained tracking for moving targets, this paper proposes a servo tracking method based on imitation trajectory constraints. A dynamic model describing the robot approaching a moving target is formulated and analyzed for convergence. A time-scalable deformation mechanism and a trajectory modulation incorporating shape and amplitude components are introduced to generate a series of trajectories in real time, from which tracking points are adaptively determined to form dynamic constraints. The robot velocity is then computed from target pose differentials or tracked key features to follow the constrained trajectory. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can achieve dynamic obstacle avoidance and high-precision convergence compared with several state-of-the-art methods in complex environments.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Multi-Agent Task Assignment and Navigation in the Factory: from Simulation to Real Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14567)
*Abdalwhab Bakheet Mohamed Abdalwhab,Giovanni Beltrame,David St-Onge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FMAPPO，将2D LiDAR与任务状态融合用于分散式多机器人多机床看护；通过高保真仿真与ROS2实现仿真到现实部署，仿真中显著优于MAPPO和SMAPPO，并在真实移动机器人平台上验证了安全协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在机器人决策中潜力巨大，但在工业物理多机器人系统上部署仍面临挑战，尤其是分散式任务分配、安全导航以及仿真到现实迁移问题。

Method: 提出Feature-fusion Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization（FMAPPO），融合2D LiDAR测量与任务特定状态信息，实现安全分散式多机器人任务分配与导航；构建基于高保真机器人仿真和ROS2的完整sim-to-real流水线，并部署到真实移动操作平台（实验中机械臂禁用）；研究策略对命令更新频率的敏感性，并与MAPPO、SMAPPO进行对比。

Result: 仿真中FMAPPO显著优于最先进基线且效应量大：相比MAPPO和SMAPPO，零件交付提升106%和21%，零件收集提升48%和11%；机器利用率提高31和10个百分点；碰撞减少18%和15%；安全分数提高14和6个百分点。真实实验表明，学习到的分散式策略可在真实传感与控制约束下协调多机器人服务多台机器并保持安全运行。

Conclusion: FMAPPO验证了分散式多智能体强化学习在真实工业多机器人多机床看护中的可行性与安全性，并凸显命令更新频率对实际部署的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown considerable promise for robotic decision-making, yet deploying multi-agent RL (MARL) on physical multi-robot systems in industrial environments remains challenging. This paper investigates the real-world applicability of decentralized MARL for multi-robot multi-machine tending. We propose Feature-fusion Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (FMAPPO), which fuses 2D LiDAR measurements with task-specific state information to enable safe decentralized multi-robot task assignment and navigation. A complete simulation-to-reality pipeline was developed using high-fidelity robotic simulation and ROS2 and deployed on physical mobile-manipulator platforms operating under realistic real-world conditions, with the robotic arms disabled during the experiments. We further investigate the sensitivity of the learned policy to command update frequency, an important consideration for real-world deployment. Comparative evaluation in simulation demonstrated that FMAPPO significantly outperformed state-of-the-art baselines with a large effect size, achieving improvements of 106\% and 21\% in parts delivery and 48\% and 11\% in parts collection over MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. FMAPPO also increased machine utilization by 31 and 10 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions by 18\% and 15\% and increasing the safety score by 14 and 6 percentage points compared with MAPPO and SMAPPO, respectively. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrated that the learned decentralized policies can coordinate multiple robots to service multiple machines while maintaining safe operation under real-world sensing and control constraints. Videos of the real-world experiment are available online this https URL .

</details>


### [87] [GLAM: Training a latent world model over global spatiotemporal memory for active exploration and navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14561)
*I-Tak Ieong,Ruizhi Feng,Zhaoyang Lu,Yifei Cao,Jiayao Zhao,Leon Li,Senhua Zhu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GLAM目标条件潜在世界模型及GLAM NAV导航系统，基于全局时空记忆预测未来地图表示与机器人中心路点潜变量，在HM3D-ObjectNav子集上优于BSC-Nav基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动探索与语义导航要求具身智能体从部分观测构建记忆，预测空间记忆演化如何支持未来运动，并将预测转化为可执行规划。

Method: GLAM以历史地图token、导航目标和当前机器人位姿为输入，在共享潜在空间中联合预测未来地图表示与机器人中心路点潜变量；采用JEPA式潜在预测，直接作用于地图级潜token而非RGB重建，并使用预训练路点编码器-解码器监督与解码导航规划。训练数据由Habitat中HM3D v0.2场景重放ObjectNav专家轨迹并切片为多时间尺度预测样本。

Result: 在受控HM3D-ObjectNav子集复现设置中，GLAM NAV在成功率和SPL上均优于复现的BSC-Nav基线。

Conclusion: 基于全局时空记忆的潜在世界模型可有效支持目标条件导航，验证了地图级潜空间预测与路点潜变量结合在具身导航中的有效性。

Abstract: Active exploration and semantic navigation require an embodied agent to build memory from partial observations, predict how the evolution of observed spatial memory may support future motion, and convert that prediction into actionable plans. We present GLAM, a goal-conditioned latent world model trained over global spatiotemporal memory, and GLAM NAV, the complete navigation system built around it. Given historical map tokens, a navigation goal, and the current robot pose, GLAM jointly predicts future map representations and robot-centric waypoint latents, allowing future spatial context and navigation intent to be inferred in a shared representation space. The model follows a JEPA-like latent prediction paradigm, operates directly on map-level latent tokens rather than RGB reconstruction, and uses a pretrained waypoint encoder-decoder to supervise and decode navigation plans within GLAM NAV. Training data are collected by replaying ObjectNav expert trajectories in Habitat over HM3D v0.2 scene assets and slicing them into multi-timescale prediction samples. On a controlled HM3D-ObjectNav subset reproduction setting, GLAM NAV improves over a reproduced BSC-Nav baseline in both success rate and success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [88] [Language-Grounded Semantic Target Navigation for Autonomous Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14558)
*Yuqing Lin,Youngroung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义接地导航框架 SGNav，使自主水面艇能根据操作员语言描述在港口环境中识别并接近目标，并在仿真中取得高成功率与低误目标率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASV 日益需要在港口与港湾环境运行，操作员可能通过语言描述而非预定义坐标或固定目标标识指定导航目标；现有 ASV 导航方法主要执行预定义几何目标或任务特定目标，对语言接地目标指定关注不足。

Method: SGNav 集成文本引导语义接地、港口感知候选过滤、基于 CLIP 的语义验证、接地目标控制状态构建，以及基于近端策略优化（PPO）的闭环控制；将目标描述接地到艇载 RGB 观测，抑制视觉或语义无关干扰项，并将所选目标转换为紧凑的控制导向表示以供策略执行。

Result: 在仿真港口环境中，SGNav 在三类代表性目标到达任务上的成功率分别为 97.0±1.2%、92.0±1.5%、90.0±1.8%，语义目标准确率高于 97%，误目标率低于 3%；在留出港口布局上保持 97.7–98.7% 成功率。Task 3 消融中，移除港口感知过滤或语义一致性后，成功率分别降至 40.4±2.6% 和 50.4±3.1%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，语义接地、港口感知过滤与语义验证对可靠的语言接地 ASV 导航至关重要；所提出的感知到控制框架能够支持 ASV 在复杂港口环境中的语言接地目标接近机动。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) are increasingly expected to operate in ports and harbour environments, where operators may specify navigation targets through language-based descriptions rather than predefined coordinates or fixed target identifiers. However, existing ASV navigation methods mainly execute predefined geometric goals or task-specific objectives and give limited attention to language-grounded target specification. This study proposes Semantically Grounded Navigation (SGNav), a framework that enables an ASV to identify and approach a maritime target from an operator-provided description. SGNav integrates text-guided semantic grounding, harbour-aware candidate filtering, CLIP-based semantic verification, grounded target control-state construction, and Proximal Policy Optimisation-based closed-loop control. It grounds the target description in onboard RGB observations, suppresses visually or semantically irrelevant distractors, and converts the selected target into a compact control-oriented representation for policy execution. Experiments in simulated port environments show that SGNav achieves success rates of $97.0\pm1.2\%$, $92.0\pm1.5\%$, and $90.0\pm1.8\%$ across three representative target-reaching tasks, with semantic target accuracy above $97\%$ and wrong-target rates below $3\%$. SGNav also maintains $97.7$--$98.7\%$ success rates across held-out port layouts. In the Task~3 ablation study, removing harbour-aware filtering or semantic consistency reduces the success rate to $40.4\pm2.6\%$ and $50.4\pm3.1\%$, respectively. These findings demonstrate the importance of semantic grounding, harbour-aware filtering, and semantic verification for reliable language-grounded ASV navigation. These results indicate that the proposed perception-to-control framework can support language-grounded target approach manoeuvres of ASV under the complex port environments.

</details>


### [89] [NavPatch: Evidence-Guided Object-Level Costmap Correction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14543)
*Shiji Sun,Xingyu Tao,Hao Wang,Ling Wang,Zhengyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavPatch 是一种对象级导航地图修正层，利用视觉语言模型周期理解场景，对导航相关物体类别施加 ADD、REMOVE 或 EXTEND 修正，并结合质量过滤与跨帧维护决定补丁提交、替换或撤销，从而提升移动机器人导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人通常依赖几何地图避障与路径规划，但几何障碍表示未必符合物体对导航的实际影响，例如低矮线缆可能被漏检、柔性帘幕可能造成虚假阻塞、交通锥可能需要比其观测足迹更大的排除区域。

Method: NavPatch 通过开放词汇 grounding 定位物体实例，融合 LiDAR 与 RGB-D 观测提供 3D 支撑；以视觉语言模型进行周期性场景理解，为导航相关物体类别分配 ADD、REMOVE 或 EXTEND 修正；再经观测质量过滤和跨帧维护，决定每个修正 patch 的提交、替换或撤销。

Result: 在五种布局的 50 次真机试验中，NavPatch 总体成功率为 86.0%。四组配置、共 200 次运行的消融实验表明，相比仅基于当前观测的更新，成功率由 70.0% 提升至 86.0%，假提交率由 68.4% 降至 40.7%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于对象级语义修正、质量过滤与跨帧维护的导航地图更新能更准确表达物体对导航的影响，显著提高机器人导航成功率并降低错误提交。

Abstract: Mobile robots typically rely on geometric maps for obstacle avoidance and path planning, but the resulting obstacle representation does not always match how an object should affect navigation. A low lying cable may be missed, a flexible curtain may create spurious blockage, and a traffic cone may require an exclusion region larger than its observed footprint. We present NavPatch, an object level correction layer that assigns ADD, REMOVE, or EXTEND to navigation relevant object categories through periodic scene understanding with a vision-language model. Open vocabulary grounding localizes object instances, and LiDAR and RGB-D observations provide 3D support. Observation quality filtering and cross frame maintenance determine when each correction patch is committed, replaced, or revoked. In 50 real robot trials across five layouts, NavPatch achieves an overall success rate of 86.0%. An ablation study of four configurations with 200 runs in total shows that NavPatch improves the success rate from 70.0% to 86.0% and reduces the false commit rate from 68.4% to 40.7% compared with updates based only on the current observation.

</details>


### [90] [Fault Diagnosis for Underwater Vehicles using Moving Horizon Estimation and Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14539)
*Fotis Panetsos,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种显式考虑未建模动态的水下航行器执行器故障检测与诊断框架，结合移动地平线估计器、高斯过程和广义似然比检验，并通过水池实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器执行器故障诊断易受未建模动态影响，传统基于模型的方法可能因模型失配导致误报或漏报，因此需要在未建模动态存在时实现鲁棒的故障检测、隔离与故障幅值定量估计。

Method: 采用移动地平线估计器估计包含未建模动态和故障效应的集总扰动；利用高斯过程近似未建模动态，并给出不同工况下的均值和不确定性预测；在线阶段用广义似然比检验评估MHE集总扰动估计与GP预测之间的残差，以实现故障检测、隔离和幅值估计。

Result: 在实验室水池中进行了实验验证，表明该方法在开环和闭环控制下均具有可靠的诊断性能，能够有效检测和隔离故障，并较准确地估计故障幅值。

Conclusion: 将GP预测融入诊断框架可增强对未建模动态的鲁棒性，为水下航行器执行器故障检测与诊断提供有效方案。

Abstract: This work proposes a model-based fault detection and diagnosis framework for underwater vehicles subject to actuator faults that explicitly accounts for the presence of unmodeled dynamics. To this end, a Moving Horizon Estimator (MHE) is developed to estimate the lumped disturbance, capturing both unmodeled and fault effects. Gaussian Processes (GPs) are employed to approximate the unmodeled dynamics, providing predictions of the corresponding mean and uncertainty across diverse operating conditions. During online operation, the residual between the MHE lumped disturbance estimate and the GP prediction is evaluated using a Generalized Likelihood Ratio Test. By incorporating GP-based predictions within the diagnostic framework, robustness to unmodeled dynamics is achieved, enabling effective fault detection and isolation as well as accurate quantitative estimation of fault magnitude. The proposed methodology is experimentally validated in a laboratory water tank, demonstrating reliable diagnostic performance under both open-loop and closed-loop control.

</details>


### [91] [EcoBoat: Design and Experimental Validation of an Autonomous Body-Board Boat For Cleaning Water Bodies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14450)
*M. Aman Ansari,Saifullah Khan,Rahul Kulkarni,PB Sujit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EcoBoat：一种基于改装船体的低成本自主水面载具，用于室内外水域漂浮垃圾收集；室内用超声波实现边界/避障与随机游走+边界跟随，室外用 GPS 地理围栏+随机游走覆盖，并采用 Tesla 阀启发收集篮防止垃圾逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清理游泳池、湖泊等水体通常需要大量人工，或依赖昂贵且传感器密集的机器人系统，因此需要一种低成本、通用且有效的自主漂浮垃圾收集方案。

Method: EcoBoat 基于改装船体构建；室内环境使用超声波传感器检测边界与障碍物，结合随机游走和边界跟随行为；室外环境采用 GPS 地理围栏与随机游走策略进行区域覆盖；关键设计是 Tesla 阀启发式收集篮，前进时允许垃圾进入，转弯或制动时防止其逃逸；整体采用极简传感与计算方案。

Result: 在游泳池和湖泊中的现场实验通过迭代改进验证了设计，表明该极简传感与计算方法能够在不同水体中实现有效且多场景适用的漂浮垃圾收集。

Conclusion: EcoBoat 证明低成本自主水面载具结合极简传感与计算即可实现有效的漂浮垃圾收集，为室内外多样化水域清理提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Cleaning water bodies such as swimming pools and lakes typically demands significant manual effort or reliance on costly, sensor-intensive robotic systems. This paper presents EcoBoat, a low-cost autonomous surface vehicle built on a modified hull, designed to collect floating debris in both indoor and outdoor water bodies. For indoor environments, EcoBoat uses ultrasonic sensors to detect boundaries and obstacles, combining random-walk motion with boundary-following behavior. For outdoor environments, it relies on GPS-based geofencing paired with a random-walk strategy for area coverage. A key design feature is a Tesla-valve-inspired collection basket that allows debris intake during forward motion while preventing its escape during turning or braking. Field experiments in pools and lakes validated the design through iterative refinement, demonstrating that a minimalist sensing and computation approach can achieve effective, versatile debris collection across diverse water bodies.

</details>


### [92] [Vision-Force Admittance Learning for Peg Insertion into a Movable Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14133)
*Yuzhong Chen,Yongqing Liang,Yunzhi Xu,Irving Fang,Chase Kidder,Hui-ping Wang,Raihan Haque,Yubiao Zhang,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VFAL框架，将异步视觉反馈与高频力基柔顺模型融合，并以视觉位姿估计作为正则项，实现动态环境下毫米级精密插孔操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境（移动机器人基座或未知运动目标）会引入显著不确定性，这与peg-in-the-hole等精密任务对高精度、高鲁棒性的要求存在根本冲突，因此需要兼顾在线适应与精密控制的方法。

Method: VFAL（Vision-Force Admittance Learning）融合异步视觉反馈与高频力基模型，利用视觉位姿估计作为正则项；采用先进视觉基础模型获取鲁棒的低频位姿信息，并引入失败恢复机制以提升鲁棒性；在线自适应调整插入策略以应对动态运动。

Result: 在真实世界实验中取得高成功率，并对多种peg及动态环境表现出强适应性与毫米级精度。

Conclusion: VFAL能够有效融合视觉与力觉信息，在动态和不确定环境下实现精密装配操作，验证了其在线适应能力、鲁棒性及实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Precise manipulation in dynamic environments, whether induced by a mobile robot base or a target with unknown motion, remains a major challenge in robotics. Manipulation in dynamic environments introduces substantial uncertainty, which fundamentally conflicts with the tight precision requirement of precise tasks such as peg-in-the-hole. We propose a Vision-Force Admittance Learning (VFAL) framework that fuses asynchronous visual feedback with a high-frequency force-based model, using visual pose estimations as a regularization term. VFAL adapts insertion strategies online to dynamic motion while maintaining millimeter-level precision. To obtain robust, low-frequency pose information, we employ state-of-the-art vision foundation models for visual pose estimation. Additionally, we incorporate failure recovery mechanisms to enhance overall robustness. We validate our approach in real-world experiments, demonstrating high success rates and strong adaptability to various pegs and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [93] [Gradient-Free Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Scalable Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14087)
*Zeyuan Feng,Ali Fuat Sahin,Santiago Thorup,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向控制-扰动仿射系统的离散时间神经可达性框架，通过Bellman-Isaacs值传播学习后向可达管与后向可达-回避管，实现高维安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamilton-Jacobi可达性可合成安全证书与鲁棒控制器，但高维非线性系统上面临维数灾难、连续时间神经求解器需精确空间值梯度、强化学习方法边界锚定弱且对抗策略优化非平稳等问题。

Method: 面向控制-扰动仿射系统，在离散时间下通过Bellman-Isaacs值传播学习BRT/BRAT；将方程驱动自监督与结构化策略学习结合，利用最优安全干预的bang-bang结构，从无梯度值探测构造近似教师动作，把对抗actor学习转为监督策略学习；并用学习到的actor以窗口化时间课程从终端边界反向训练值函数，逐窗作为下一窗边界条件以稳定长时程传播。

Result: 在最高80维基准问题上学习到准确的可达性值函数，稳定性优于现有基于学习的求解器；在F1-tenth赛车中处理超过16,000维自我中心观测输入，学习的安全滤波器零样本泛化到未见赛道并迁移到实体RC小车，实现实时鲁棒避碰。

Conclusion: 该框架缓解了高维HJ可达性学习中梯度依赖、边界锚定与长时程优化不稳定问题，并展示了从仿真到真实机器人、从低维状态到高维观测空间的可扩展性与迁移能力。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides a principled framework for synthesizing safety certificates and robust controllers for safety-critical robotic systems. However, applying reachability analysis to high-dimensional nonlinear systems remains challenging: classical grid-based solvers suffer from the curse of dimensionality, continuous-time neural solvers require accurate spatial value gradients, and reinforcement-learning-based approaches often suffer from weak boundary anchoring and non-stationary adversarial policy optimization. We propose a discrete-time neural reachability framework for control-disturbance-affine systems that learns backward reachable tubes (BRTs) and backward reach-avoid tubes (BRATs) through Bellman-Isaacs value propagation. Our key idea is to combine equation-driven self-supervision with structured policy learning: rather than computing explicit PDE-gradients, we exploit the bang-bang structure of optimal safety interventions to construct approximate teacher actions from gradient-free value probes, converting adversarial actor learning into supervised policy learning. To stabilize long-horizon value propagation, we leverage the learned actor to train the value function backward from the terminal boundary using a windowed temporal curriculum, where each window is used as the boundary condition for the next window. Across benchmark problems up to 80 dimensions, our method learns accurate reachability value functions while improving stability over existing learning-based solvers. We further demonstrate observation-space scalability on F1-tenth racing with over 16,000-dimensional egocentric inputs. The learned safety filter generalizes zero-shot to unseen tracks and transfers to a physical RC car, achieving real-time robust collision avoidance.

</details>


### [94] [Mobile Multi-Robot Navigation under Runtime Uncertainty via Koopman Operator Learning and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14058)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Konstantinos Karydis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Koopman算子学习动力学的NMPC框架，用于多移动机器人导航；在目标到达与编队控制中处理障碍物和随机打滑等不确定性，并通过仿真验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人导航受扰动与不确定性（如轮子打滑）影响，且难以获知真实动力学模型，因此需要结合学习模型与预测控制来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 采用Koopman算子理论从数据学习提升的双线性模型，以准确预测受扰动和不确定性影响的仿射输入系统；将该模型嵌入NMPC，求解目标到达和编队控制优化问题，实现闭环多机器人导航与编队。

Result: 在含障碍物、不同随机打滑程度、且未知真实动力学模型的轮式机器人环境中进行大量数值仿真，验证了目标到达和编队控制的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman的NMPC可有效应对运行时不确定性和未知动力学，实现多机器人闭环导航与编队控制。

Abstract: In this work, we developed a nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework that employs learned dynamics via the Koopman Operator theory for mobile multi-robot navigation. We formulated and solved NMPC problems using a lifted bilinear Koopman-based model that accurately predicts affine input systems affected by perturbations and uncertainties. Two exemplary multi-robot navigation problems are considered: target reaching and formation control. The output of our method enables closed-loop multi-robot navigation and formation control in environments populated with obstacles, whereby the Koopman operator-based model used in the NMPC formulation addresses runtime uncertainties, namely, various degrees of random wheel slipping. We validated the effectiveness of our method for both problems via extensive numerical simulations in different environments with wheeled robots affected by different amounts of slip and without knowledge of their true dynamic models.

</details>


### [95] [What Makes an Efficient VLA? Navigating Action-Head Design, Scaling, and Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13984)
*Luoyang Sun,Guoyang Xia,Fengfa Li,Lei Ren,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Fangxiang Feng,Kaike Zhang,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie,Jun Wang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定SigLIP2视觉编码器、Qwen2.5语言骨干与训练流程并配对实测端侧延迟的条件下，系统剖析VLA各模块贡献；发现动作头性能主要由初始化而非解码器结构、损失或推理预算决定，对齐后扩容才有效，且接近当下π系列VLA规模后收益锐减；据此提出紧凑模型EffVLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型由预训练视觉编码器、语言骨干和动作头组合而成，但其相对贡献尚未在受控且延迟配对的条件下确立；需要明确性能来源、模块扩展规律与高效设计原则。

Method: 固定SigLIP2与Qwen2.5骨干家族及训练管线，扫掠动作头设计与模块规模，并为每个配置测量端侧延迟；关键干预是将语言骨干最后若干Transformer层复制到动作头作为初始化以实现对齐；在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及真实SO-ARM101上验证。

Result: 动作头性能首要由初始化决定：复制语言骨干末层是最大杠杆、无延迟代价，且是各模块尺度下唯一均有效的轴；流匹配与更重解码器仅在头未对齐时有利、对齐后反转，额外推理遍数无可测收益。容量仅在对齐后带来高回报，且接近当今π系列VLA规模时收益急剧递减。EffVLA以紧凑模型在标准LIBERO匹配最强开源VLA，在多数LIBERO-Plus扰动轴领先且延迟更低，并以未改配方迁移到真实SO-ARM101。

Conclusion: 结果支持“表征迁移”解释：对齐动作头持续关注指令中的物体名词，并在权重空间保持接近语言骨干，而非从零重学动作；但作者强调这是最佳组织测量结果的解释而非已证因果，并指出可判定因果的对照实验。EffVLA给出对齐优先、再扩容的紧凑VLA配方。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine a pretrained vision encoder, a language backbone, and an action head, but their relative contribution has not been established under controlled, latency-paired conditions. We fix the backbone families (SigLIP2 and Qwen2.5) and the training pipeline, sweep action-head design and module scale, and pair each configuration with measured on-device latency. The study yields three findings. First, action-head performance is governed primarily by initialization rather than decoder architecture, loss, or inference budget: copying the last transformer layers of the language backbone into the head is the single largest lever, at no latency cost, and the only axis that helps at every module scale. Alignment also explains the other axes: flow matching and a heavier decoder pay off only while the head is misaligned and reverse once it is aligned, and extra inference passes give no measurable benefit; expressiveness appears to substitute for missing alignment. We read this as representation transfer: the aligned head keeps attending to the instruction's object nouns and stays close to the backbone in weight space rather than relearning to act from scratch. Because we reach alignment only through initialization, we offer this as the account that best organizes the measurements, not a demonstrated cause, and name the control that would settle it. Second, capacity pays only after alignment: the aligned action head is the highest-return module to scale. Third, those returns diminish sharply near the size today's $\pi$-series VLAs already use, so further growth buys little in-domain accuracy for its latency. These specify EffVLA, a compact model matching the strongest open-source VLAs on standard LIBERO, leading on most LIBERO-Plus perturbation axes at lower latency, and transferring to a real SO-ARM101 arm with the recipe unchanged.

</details>


### [96] [Rethinking the Implications of Human Feedback for Preference Learning in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13982)
*Qiping Zhang,Kate Candon,Debasmita Ghose,Marynel Vázquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出固定蕴含规则会阻碍机器人从人类反馈中学习偏好，提出 IMPLIED 方法动态学习并修正人类反馈的蕴含标签，从而提升偏好建模与机器人行为适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，标准偏好奖励学习先用有限人类反馈作为直接证据，再按固定蕴含规则推导未选可行动作的接受/拒绝标签。但用户研究表明，人类给出的蕴含标签常与固定规则不一致，而使用人类标签可显著提升 PIE 的奖励学习，因此固定规则会限制偏好学习。

Method: 提出 IMPLIED：将固定规则蕴含作为初始引导，同时随时间学习推断并修正对可行但未选动作的接受/拒绝标签，以建模人类反馈的隐含含义。

Result: 在录制的人机交互轨迹和实物机器人披萨制作研究中，IMPLIED 预测人类蕴含标签比固定规则方法和 LLM 基线更准确，接近人类标签 oracle；并降低偏好估计误差，使机器人动作在包含真实偏好奖励与任务特定奖励的组合奖励下更常表现为理性。

Conclusion: 通过学习推理人类反馈的蕴含关系，该方法可实现更忠实、更高效的人机协作中机器人行为自适应。

Abstract: In Human-Robot Interaction, the standard approach to learn a reward model that represents human preferences for robot behavior consists of three steps. First, the robot collects limited direct evidence from human feedback (e.g., positive or negative binary feedback). Then, the robot utilizes the direct evidence to derive accepted or rejected labels to feasible but unchosen actions using fixed implication rules. Finally, the robot updates the reward model with both the direct and derived evidence. Unfortunately, the fixed rule can hinder preference learning: in a user study with two collaborative simulation environments, human-provided implication labels often differed from the standard fixed rule, and using the human labels substantially improved reward learning with the Preference Learning from Implicit and Explicit Feedback (PIE) algorithm. Consequently, we propose IMPLIED, an implication modeling method that treats fixed-rule implications as an initial guide while learning to infer and revise accepted and rejected action labels over time. Across evaluations on recorded human-robot interaction trajectories and a physical robot pizza-making study, IMPLIED predicts human implications more accurately than the fixed rule approach and LLM baselines, approaching the performance of a human-label oracle. In turn, IMPLIED reduces preference-estimation error and leads to robot actions that are more often rational with respect to a combined reward (which includes the true preference reward and a task-specific reward) compared to baselines. By learning to reason about the implications of human feedback, this work enables more faithful and efficient robot behavior adaptation during human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [97] [Co-Speech with You: Training-Free Personalization of Robot Co-Speech Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13876)
*Bosong Ding,Selma Ancel,Giacomo Spigler,Murat Kirtay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重新训练的个人化共语手势生成流程，通过冻结的音频条件扩散先验、手势风格编码器和轻量条件适配器，从约10秒注册动作中提取可复用风格嵌入，并迁移到实体NAO机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人机器人需要在不重新训练模型的情况下适应新用户的共语手势风格，现有方法难以实现快速、可复用且保持动作质量的个性化。

Method: 结合冻结的音频条件扩散先验、手势风格编码器和轻量条件适配器；编码器先训练区分说话人身份，再与适配器用扩散目标联合微调；通过单次前向传播从约10秒注册动作中提取风格嵌入。同时发布Quest 3采集应用和十名参与者的自发共语动作数据集。

Result: 在留出说话人上，SRA从冻结先验的27.6%提升至69.5%，FGD保持34.2对34.8；替换为他人注册嵌入时SRA降至11.4%。生成手势重定向到实体NAO机器人，在五种TTS语音合成下仍保持67.3% SRA。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了无需重训练、基于短时注册动作的共语手势风格个人化，并在实体机器人部署中有效迁移，兼顾个性化与动作质量。

Abstract: Personal robots should adapt their co-speech gesture style to a new user without requiring model retraining. We present a training-free personalization pipeline that combines a frozen audio-conditioned diffusion prior with a gesture style encoder and lightweight conditioning adapters. The encoder is first trained to discriminate speaker identities and then jointly refined with the adapters using the diffusion objective, enabling a reusable style embedding to be extracted from approximately 10 seconds of enrollment motion through a single forward pass. To support this setting, we also release a Quest~3 capture application and a dataset of spontaneous co-speech motion from ten participants. We evaluate the system on held-out speakers using Style Recognition Accuracy (SRA) and Fr'echet Gesture Distance (FGD) to measure personalization and motion quality. Our approach improves SRA from 27.6\% for the frozen prior to 69.5\% while preserving motion quality (FGD 34.2 versus 34.8), and replacing the enrollment embedding with another person's reduces SRA to 11.4\%. The generated gestures are retargeted to a physical NAO robot, and this improvement also transfers to the robot deployment setting, where speech is synthesized using five TTS voices, retaining 67.3\% SRA.

</details>


### [98] [ReWeight: Leveraging Human Data for VLA Post-Training via Demonstration Retrieval and Sample Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13851)
*Chenwei Wang,Dianye Huang,Match W.L. Ko,Chenjia Bai,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReWeight 通过演示级检索与样本级加权，将第一视角人类演示有效融入 VLA 后训练，缓解跨具身差异并提升策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对特定机器人和任务后训练 VLA 模型需要域内演示，但机器人数据采集成本高；第一视角人类演示可扩展，却因直接混合人机数据带来跨具身差异并导致策略性能下降。

Method: 提出 ReWeight 框架：学习跨具身视觉运动表示，结合视觉观测与未来动作度量人类和机器人演示的行为相似性；基于最优传输检索与目标机器人数据相关的人类演示，并对跨具身差异更小的样本赋予更大权重。

Result: 基于 π_{0.5} 在 8 个仿真任务和 4 个真实任务、干净与随机化设置下评估；仿真平均成功率从仅机器人数据的 39%、随机混合人机数据的 44% 提升至 57%；真实环境平均成功率 68.8%，分别超过基线 28.8% 和 13.8%。

Conclusion: ReWeight 为将丰富的第一视角人类经验转化为可迁移的机器人学习监督提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Post-training vision-language-action (VLA) models for specific robots and tasks requires in-domain demonstrations, yet collecting diverse robot data is costly. Egocentric human demonstrations provide a scalable alternative, but directly mixing human and robot data can introduce cross-embodiment discrepancies and degrade policy performance. To address this challenge, we introduce ReWeight, a framework that incorporates human data into VLA post-training through demonstration-level retrieval and sample-level weighting. ReWeight learns a cross-embodiment visuomotor representation that combines visual observations with future actions to measure behavioral similarity between human and robot demonstrations. Based on optimal transport, it retrieves human demonstrations relevant to the target robot data and assigns larger weights to samples with smaller cross-embodiment discrepancies. We evaluate ReWeight using $\pi_{0.5}$ across eight simulation tasks and four real-world tasks under both clean and randomized settings. In simulation, ReWeight improves the average success rate of post-trained $\pi_{0.5}$ from 39% with only robot data and 44% with randomly mixed human-robot data to 57%. In the physical experimental setting, it achieves an average success rate of 68.8%, outperforming the baselines by 28.8% and 13.8%, respectively. Overall, ReWeight provides an effective paradigm for transforming abundant egocentric human experience into transferable supervision for robot learning. (Project webpage: this https URL )

</details>


### [99] [LePlanner: An Iterative Amortized Controller For World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13845)
*Saksham Bansal,Om Naphade,Chayan Aggarwal,Vrishin M*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LePlanner 是一种摊销式迭代控制器：在冻结的世界模型预测器中学习构建并精炼潜动作序列，通过“到达并保持”目标与动作高斯损失，解决滚动时域重规划中的目标到达拖延问题，从而以远低于搜索规划器的计算量实现快速、非线性物理控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构训练的世界模型能学到紧凑、结构化的潜表示，但在潜空间规划通常面临两难：CEM、MPPI、iCEM 等搜索规划器性能强，却需要大量预测器 rollout，导致每决策计算和延迟很高；策略方法只需单次前向传播，但在接触丰富任务中，当演示分布多模态时性能会下降。因此需要一种兼具搜索规划性能与策略方法效率、且适用于多模态离线数据的规划方法。

Method: 提出 LePlanner，一种摊销式迭代控制器，通过冻结的世界模型预测器学习构造和精炼潜动作序列。其训练采用 arrival-and-hold 目标，鼓励控制器在最早可行时域到达目标并保持在该目标处，以缓解 horizon-reset procrastination：即在滚动时域重规划中反复推迟目标到达的失败模式。此外引入 action-Gaussian 损失，使生成动作保持在离线数据集动作支撑集附近，从而提高离线数据约束下的可靠性。

Result: 在导航、接触丰富操作和连续控制环境中，LePlanner 匹配或超越搜索式规划器，同时所需预测器评估次数少一个数量级，每决策墙钟时间降低 3–49 倍。具体成功率为：PushT 98%、Reacher 100%、TwoRooms 100%、OGBench Cube 92%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在线搜索所发现的大量结构可以摊销进一个轻量级迭代策略中，从而无需在线优化即可实现快速、时域感知的非线性物理控制，并保持与搜索规划器相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: World models trained with joint-embedding predictive architectures learn compact, structured latent representations from physical interaction, yet planning in these latent spaces typically relies on one of two costly approaches. Search-based planners such as CEM, MPPI, and iCEM optimize action sequences through many predictor rollouts, achieving strong performance at the cost of high per-decision compute and latency. Policy-based methods amortize inference into a single forward pass but can degrade on contact-rich tasks where the demonstration distribution is multimodal. We propose LePlanner, an amortized iterative controller that learns to construct and refine latent action sequences through a frozen world-model predictor. LePlanner is trained with an arrival-and-hold objective that encourages the controller to reach the goal at the earliest feasible horizon and remain there. This addresses horizon-reset procrastination, a failure mode in which repeated receding-horizon replanning continually postpones goal arrival. An additional action-Gaussian loss keeps generated actions near the support of the offline dataset. Across navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous-control environments, LePlanner matches or exceeds search-based planners while requiring an order of magnitude fewer predictor evaluations and 3-49x lower wall-clock time per decision. It achieves success rates of 98% on PushT, 100% on Reacher, 100% on TwoRooms, and 92% on the OGBench Cube task. These results show that much of the structure discovered through online search can be amortized into a lightweight iterative policy, enabling fast, horizon-aware, nonlinear physical control without online optimization.

</details>


### [100] [GeomVLA: Unifying Scene, Motion, and Action in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13812)
*Ziyin Xiong,Nikos Gkanatsios,Moritz Reuss,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeomVLA 是一种视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，在共享的机器人中心 3D 坐标系中统一感知、隐式场景运动预测与动作生成；它利用深度和相机标定将预训练 VLM 特征提升为空间接地的 3D 场景 token，并通过 3D 场景轨迹去噪器和流式动作去噪器实现几何一致的动作生成，在 CALVIN 等基准与真实操作中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法多依赖 2D 语义特征，缺乏 3D 几何一致性；仅进行未来运动推理或把预测轨迹作为开环计划执行，不足以提升机器人操作性能。核心动机是在感知到动作的整个流程中，保持场景表示、运动预测与机器人动作之间的几何一致性。

Method: 1）利用深度和相机标定，将预训练 VLM 特征提升为空间接地的 3D 场景 token，同时保留 VLM 预训练获得的语义表示；2）引入任务条件的 3D Scene Trajectory Denoiser，学习场景点在 3D 中预期运动的隐式表示；3）不将预测轨迹作为开环计划直接执行，而是提取轨迹去噪器的中间运动 token，并通过几何感知注意力对 3D 流式动作去噪器进行条件化，从而生成动作。

Result: 在 CALVIN 上达到当前最优性能，在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin2.0 上具有竞争力，并在无需机器人动作预训练的真实世界操作设置中优于强基线。大量消融表明，仅靠未来运动推理并不足够，主要增益来自在感知到动作的整个流程中维持场景表示、运动预测与机器人动作之间的几何一致性。

Conclusion: GeomVLA 表明，将感知、场景运动预测与动作生成统一在机器人中心 3D 坐标系中，并保持全流程几何一致性，是提升 VLA 操作性能的关键；单纯预测未来运动或开环执行轨迹并非主要增益来源。

Abstract: We present GeomVLA, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model that unifies perception, latent scene motion prediction, and action generation within a shared robot-centric 3D coordinate frame. Our approach lifts pretrained VLM features into spatially grounded 3D scene tokens using depth and camera calibration, while retaining the semantic representations learned during VLM pretraining. We further introduce a 3D Scene Trajectory Denoiser, a task-conditioned module that learns a latent representation of how scene points are expected to move in 3D. Rather than executing the predicted trajectory as an open-loop plan, GeomVLA extracts intermediate motion tokens from the trajectory denoiser and uses them to condition a 3D flow-based action denoiser through geometry-aware attention. GeomVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on CALVIN, competitive performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, and outperforms strong baselines in real-world manipulation settings without robot-action pretraining. Extensive ablations show that future-motion reasoning alone is insufficient: the primary gains are associated with maintaining geometric consistency among scene representation, motion prediction, and robot actions throughout the perception-to-action pipeline.

</details>


### [101] [Force-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Hybrid Sensorless Force Estimation for Wheeled-Legged Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13779)
*Xuanqi Zeng,Jiaming Wang,Tianlin Zhang,Lingwei Zhang,Botian Xu,Weipeng Xia,Zhongyu Li,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合无传感器力估计的力感知强化学习方法，用于轮腿式机器人的全身loco-manipulation，在无末端力/力矩传感器控制下实现力调节与力/位置混合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力控全身loco-manipulation需在浮动基座与变化支撑接触下协调移动与手臂运动并调节末端交互力；末端力/力矩传感不可用使控制困难。

Method: 将末端力估计作为显式策略观测：结合广义动量观测、接触约束力旋量投影与时间残差学习，模型部分提取全身扰动中物理结构化分量，残差网络补偿运动相关偏差；估计力融入模式条件全身策略，采用逐轴力/位置选择器，统一实现自由空间运动、纯力调节及力/位置混合控制。

Result: 仿真表明无传感器力估计与力控性能提升；硬件在轮腿平台上通过定量阀门旋转与混合擦拭评估，以及力引导开门和零力人导运动得到验证。

Conclusion: 该力感知强化学习与混合无传感器力估计框架可在无末端力/力矩传感器条件下实现有效的轮腿式全身力控loco-manipulation，并具备仿真与实机可行性。

Abstract: Force-controlled loco-manipulation requires a whole-body policy to coordinate locomotion and arm motion while regulating end-effector interaction forces. This is challenging under floating-base dynamics and changing support contacts, particularly when end-effector force/torque sensing is unavailable for control. This paper presents a force-aware reinforcement learning approach with hybrid sensorless force estimation for wheeled-legged loco-manipulation. The proposed method provides a structured estimate of the end-effector force as an explicit policy observation, enabling force-guided contact behavior without using an end-effector force/torque sensor for control. The force estimate is obtained by combining generalized momentum observation, contact-constrained wrench projection, and temporal residual learning: the model-based components extract the physically structured part of the whole-body disturbance, while the residual network compensates the remaining motion-dependent bias. The estimated force is integrated into a mode-conditioned whole-body policy with an axis-wise force/position selector, allowing free-space motion, pure force regulation, and hybrid force/position control within one controller. Simulation results demonstrate improved sensorless force estimation and force-control performance. Hardware experiments further validate the proposed controller through quantitative valve-rotation and hybrid wiping evaluations, together with force-guided door opening and zero-force human-guided motion on a real wheeled-legged platform.

</details>


### [102] [When Do Learned Priors Help Visual Inertial Estimation? A Controlled Study of Prior Integration, Calibration, Initialization, and Backend Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13777)
*Jinchang Zhang,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出受控评测框架，区分几何视觉惯性估计中的融合收益与学习先验的增量价值，并用MonoViT运动先验加VINS在KITTI验证：性能提升本身不能证明学习先验有效，需同后端控制与多层面联合分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习组件越来越多被集成到视觉惯性估计器中，用于提供运动、深度、偏置、不确定性或置信度线索；但涨点可能来自后端、标定、初始化、时序关联或评价规范，而非真正有用的学习先验，因此需要控制变量来判断其真实贡献。

Method: 构建学习增强视觉惯性估计的受控框架，分离融合增益与学习先验的增量价值，评估四层证据：局部运动一致性、全局轨迹精度、物理状态正确性、数值一致性。将基于MonoViT的单目运动先验作为局部相对运动因子加入未改动的VINS后端，在匹配传感器流、时间戳、初始化、前后端设置和相机-IMU外参下比较原始VINS与学习先验VINS，并探查标定、初始化、状态耦合、尺度、BA和回环。

Result: 在KITTI固定参考外参下，平移APE RMSE原始VINS为31.4 m，学习先验VINS为31.8 m；在四个序列上，先验使平均APE仅变化-0.2%，而权重提高5倍则使平均APE恶化8.2%。在线外参更新使平均APE分别增加45.7%和52.1%，但平均RPE变化小于2%。

Conclusion: 仅凭融合性能不能确立学习先验的价值；可靠评测需要同后端对照，并联合分析先验兼容性、标定、初始化、全局漂移、物理状态误差和后端一致性。

Abstract: Learned components are increasingly integrated into geometric visual--inertial estimators to provide motion, depth, bias, uncertainty, or confidence cues. Yet it remains unclear whether gains arise from useful learned priors or from changes in the backend, calibration, initialization, temporal association, or evaluation gauge. We present a controlled framework for learning-augmented visual--inertial estimation that separates fusion gain from the incremental value of a learned prior and evaluates four evidence layers: local motion consistency, global trajectory accuracy, physical-state correctness, and numerical consistency. We instantiate the framework with a MonoViT-based monocular motion prior added as a local relative-motion factor to an unchanged VINS backend. We compare Original VINS and learned-prior VINS under matched sensor streams, timestamps, initialization, frontend/backend settings, and camera--IMU extrinsics, while probing calibration, initialization, state coupling, scale, bundle adjustment, and loop closure. On KITTI, with fixed reference extrinsics, translation APE RMSE is 31.4 m for Original VINS and 31.8 m with the learned prior. Across four recordings, the prior changes mean APE by only -0.2%, while a five-times-higher weight worsens it by 8.2%. Online extrinsic updates increase mean APE by 45.7% and 52.1%, respectively, while mean RPE changes by less than 2%. These results show that fusion performance alone cannot establish the value of learned priors. Reliable evaluation requires same-backend controls and joint analysis of prior compatibility, calibration, initialization, global drift, physical-state error, and backend consistency.

</details>


### [103] [Learning In-Hand Object Reaching to General 6D Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13761)
*Junxiao Lin,Tianyue Wu,Jie Yin,Jia Pan,Kaifeng Zhang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出POISE——一个面向多指灵巧手在手中6D物体位姿到达任务的仿真到现实强化学习框架，使物体在不脱手重抓的情况下被协调手指运动至相对于手掌的目标位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手中操作可避免反复抓取和大幅手臂运动，从而提升操作效率；但现有学习方法多聚焦于重定向、连续旋转或平移，而许多任务需要同时控制物体的位置与姿态。

Method: 将能力形式化为在手中6D物体位姿到达：从已有抓取出发，通过协调的手指运动将物体移动到手掌相对的目标位姿。提出POISE（Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)）仿真到现实强化学习框架，结合多样化稳定抓取初始化、目标与几何条件控制、自适应6D目标课程，以及用于位姿到达和抓取保持的紧凑奖励方案。

Result: 仿真中，多样化初始化将未见抓取成功率从40.1%提升至51.5%，掉落恢复率从33.8%提升至72.9%；课程学习将全范围成功率从6.2%提升至59.5%。硬件上，抓取保持奖励将三目标序列成功率从20%提升至80%。真实实验中，POISE可在多种物体几何和手腕朝向下连续到达6D目标且无需人工重置，并能从外部扰动中恢复。

Conclusion: POISE验证了在手中6D位姿到达的可学习性与sim-to-real可行性，提升了多指灵巧手在复杂位姿操作中的连续性与鲁棒性；作者将公开代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation allows multi-fingered dexterous hands to reconfigure grasped objects without releasing and regrasping them. This improves manipulation efficiency by reducing repeated grasp acquisition and large arm motions. However, most learning-based methods focus on reorientation, continuous rotation, or translation, whereas many tasks require joint control of object position and orientation. We formulate this capability as in-hand 6D object pose reaching: starting from an existing grasp, coordinated finger motions move the object to a palm-relative target pose. We present POISE (Palm-relative Object reaching In SE(3)), a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for this task. POISE combines diverse stable-grasp initialization, goal- and geometry-conditioned control, an adaptive 6D goal curriculum, and a compact reward scheme for pose reaching and grasp preservation. In simulation, diverse initialization raises held-out-grasp success from 40.1% to 51.5% and post-drop recovery from 33.8% to 72.9%; the curriculum raises full-range success from 6.2% to 59.5%. On hardware, the grasp-maintenance reward improves three-target sequence success from 20% to 80%. In real-world experiments, POISE reaches successive 6D targets without manual reset across multiple object geometries and wrist orientations, and recovers from external disturbances. To support further research in dexterous manipulation, we will release our code at this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [Decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation Under Degraded Communication: A Benchmark of Performance, Reliability, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13711)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在协作访问（CV）场景中系统基准测试六种去中心化MRTA分配器，揭示其在MinMax/MinSum路径、通信鲁棒性与需求、分配可靠性、计算负担及规模敏感性上的权衡与适用区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为嵌入式自主平台选择去中心化多机器人任务分配（MRTA）方法时，不能仅依据路径性能，还需综合考虑通信退化、可靠性、计算约束与可扩展性。

Method: 在CV场景中对比CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC、DMCHBA和DGA六种分配器；核心实验使用500对十目标实例、25种理想与退化通信条件（Bernoulli丢包、Gilbert-Elliott丢包、Rayleigh衰落），并补充预分配、执行集成计算以及网格尺寸、机器人密度和目标负载敏感性实验。

Result: 在24个受损核心条件下，DGA和DMCHBA平均MinMax行程最低，分别为24.49和24.78步；HIPC平均MinSum行程略优，为66.95步，DGA为67.22步，二者在所有受损条件中均列前二；DMCHBA发布强度最低，为2.08次/团队步。十目标预分配中，HIPC和DMCHBA在各测试条件下稳定可行，而ACBBA、PI和DGA随通信退化失稳或不可行。理想交付下，十目标主比较的中位全协议计算时间从DMCHBA的4.88 ms到DGA的1.346 s。静态路径质量下，DGA和DMCHBA仍是领先的MinMax方法；DGA在四类目标负载中的三类领先MinSum，HIPC在50目标时领先。随任务负载增加，静态与执行集成计算的排名出现分歧。

Conclusion: 结果识别出不同分配器在路径目标、通信行为、可靠性与计算约束下的不同操作区域，为嵌入式去中心化MRTA方法选型提供了依据。

Abstract: Selecting a decentralized Multi-Robot Task Allocation (MRTA) method for embedded deployment on autonomous platforms requires considering more than route performance alone. We benchmark six decentralized MRTA allocators (CBAA, ACBBA, PI, HIPC, DMCHBA, and DGA) in the Collaborative Visit (CV) scenario to characterize tradeoffs among MinMax and MinSum travel, communication robustness and demand, allocation reliability, computational burden, and scale sensitivity. The core study uses 500 paired ten-target instances across 25 ideal and degraded communication conditions spanning Bernoulli loss, Gilbert--Elliott loss, and Rayleigh fading, with additional campaigns examining pre-allocation, execution-integrated computation, and sensitivity to grid size, robot density, and target load. Across the 24 impaired core conditions, DGA and DMCHBA achieved the lowest mean MinMax travel at 24.49 and 24.78 steps, respectively. HIPC narrowly led mean MinSum travel at 66.95 steps, followed by DGA at 67.22, with both methods occupying the top two in every impaired condition. DMCHBA had the lowest publication intensity at 2.08 publications per team step. In ten-target pre-allocation, HIPC and DMCHBA remained viable and stable in every tested condition, while ACBBA, PI, and DGA lost stability or viability as communication degraded. Under ideal delivery, median full-protocol computation $\Cterm$ in the primary ten-target comparison ranged from 4.88 ms for DMCHBA to 1.346 s for DGA. Static route quality preserved DGA and DMCHBA as the leading MinMax methods, while DGA led MinSum at three of four target loads and HIPC led at 50 targets. Static and execution-integrated computation rankings diverged as task load increased. The results identify distinct allocator operating regions across route objective, communication behavior, reliability, and computational constraints.

</details>


### [105] [GROOVE: Geometry-Guided Reduction of Operational-Space Jerk in VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13695)
*Sangho Yun,Minsoo Kim,Minwoo Cho,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GROOVE 是一种在线调节器，用于分块视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略执行，在不重训练、不额外 VLA 推理的情况下，围绕原始三维末端执行器（EEF）路径搜索方向性修正区域，以降低块内和重规划边界处的 jerk，并限制相对原始规划的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块 VLA 策略每次查询执行多个命令，但块内及重规划边界处的 jerk 会导致振荡运动和剧烈的执行器瞬态，影响平滑性与执行稳定性。
